===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\README.md =====  
# 🍽️ OmniResto - Unified F&B Experience Platform

Welcome to **OmniResto**, a modern F&B platform designed exclusively for our single-brand restaurant chain. OmniResto bridges the gap between digital convenience and exceptional in-dining experiences across all of our physical locations.

---

## 🌟 Brand & Project Vision

As our restaurant chain expands, maintaining consistent food quality, operational excellence, and brand identity across every branch becomes our top priority. 

**OmniResto** was created to deliver a unified brand experience for our customers, whether they are ordering delivery from home, picking up on the go, or dining in at any of our branches. Powered by a centralized management ecosystem, OmniResto ensures every dish served reflects our core brand standards while adapting seamlessly to the local pace of each branch.

---

## 🔑 Core Pillars of the Platform

### 🛵 1. Unified Customer Experience
* **Seamless Online Delivery:** Customers enjoy a direct, branded ordering experience that automatically connects them to their nearest branch for optimal freshness and speed.
* **Smart Contactless Dine-In:** Guests seated at any branch can simply scan their table's QR code to explore the live menu, customize their orders, and pay effortlessly.
* **Chain-Wide Membership & Rewards:** A single, universal loyalty program where points earned at one branch can be redeemed across the entire chain.

### 🏢 2. Centralized Brand Control (HQ Management)
* **Master Menu Standardization:** Headquarters maintains full control over global menu offerings, promotional campaigns, and brand pricing.
* **Smart Order Routing:** Orders are automatically directed to the optimal branch location based on proximity and real-time kitchen capacity.
* **Cross-Branch Insights:** Real-time visibility into overall chain performance, popular dishes, and customer feedback.

### 🍳 3. Branch Autonomy & Operations
* **Kitchen Display Workflows:** Every branch operates with synchronized kitchen screens to ensure orders are prepared accurately and served hot.
* **Real-Time Local Stock Management:** Individual branches can instantly update item availability locally (e.g., if an ingredient runs out) without affecting other locations.

---

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\config\GlobalExceptionHandler.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.config;

import org.springframework.dao.DataIntegrityViolationException;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.MethodArgumentNotValidException;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ExceptionHandler;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestControllerAdvice;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

@RestControllerAdvice
public class GlobalExceptionHandler {

    @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentNotValidException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<Map<String, Object>> handleValidation(MethodArgumentNotValidException ex) {
        Map<String, String> errors = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        ex.getBindingResult().getFieldErrors()
                .forEach(e -> errors.putIfAbsent(e.getField(), e.getDefaultMessage()));
        ex.getBindingResult().getGlobalErrors()
                .forEach(e -> errors.putIfAbsent(e.getObjectName(), e.getDefaultMessage()));
        return build(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Dữ liệu không hợp lệ", errors);
    }

    // Phòng trường hợp 2 request đăng ký trùng email/số điện thoại cùng lúc
    @ExceptionHandler(DataIntegrityViolationException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<Map<String, Object>> handleIntegrity(DataIntegrityViolationException ex) {
        return build(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Email hoặc số điện thoại đã được sử dụng", null);
    }

    private ResponseEntity<Map<String, Object>> build(HttpStatus status, String message, Object errors) {
        Map<String, Object> body = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        body.put("status", status.value());
        body.put("message", message);
        if (errors != null) body.put("errors", errors);
        return ResponseEntity.status(status).body(body);
    }
}

  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\config\SecurityConfig.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.config;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.method.configuration.EnableMethodSecurity;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.builders.HttpSecurity;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.configuration.EnableWebSecurity;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.configurers.AbstractHttpConfigurer;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.configurers.AuthorizeHttpRequestsConfigurer;
import org.springframework.security.config.http.SessionCreationPolicy;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.bcrypt.BCryptPasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.security.web.SecurityFilterChain;
import org.springframework.security.web.util.matcher.AntPathRequestMatcher;
import org.springframework.security.web.util.matcher.RequestMatcher;
import org.springframework.web.cors.CorsConfiguration;
import org.springframework.web.cors.CorsConfigurationSource;
import org.springframework.web.cors.UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource;

import java.util.List;

@Configuration
@EnableWebSecurity
@EnableMethodSecurity
public class SecurityConfig {

    @Bean
    public PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder() {
        return new BCryptPasswordEncoder();
    }

    @Bean
    public CorsConfigurationSource corsConfigurationSource() {
        CorsConfiguration configuration = new CorsConfiguration();
        configuration.setAllowedOriginPatterns(List.of("*"));
        configuration.setAllowedMethods(List.of("GET", "POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE", "OPTIONS"));
        configuration.setAllowedHeaders(List.of("*"));
        configuration.setAllowCredentials(true);

        UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource source = new UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource();
        source.registerCorsConfiguration("/**", configuration);
        return source;
    }

    @Bean
    public SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {
        http
                .cors(cors -> cors.configurationSource(corsConfigurationSource()))
                .csrf(AbstractHttpConfigurer::disable)
                .authorizeHttpRequests(this::configureAuthorizeHttpRequests)
                .exceptionHandling(exceptionHandling -> exceptionHandling
                        .accessDeniedHandler((request, response, accessDeniedException) -> {
                            response.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_FORBIDDEN);
                            response.setContentType("application/json");
                            String msg = accessDeniedException.getMessage() != null ? accessDeniedException.getMessage() : "Access is denied";
                            response.getWriter().write("{\"error\":\"access_denied\",\"error_description\":\"" + msg + "\"}");
                        })
                        .authenticationEntryPoint((request, response, authException) -> {
                            response.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_UNAUTHORIZED);
                            response.setContentType("application/json");
                            response.getWriter().write("{\"error\":\"" + HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED.getReasonPhrase() + "\"}");
                        })
                )
                .sessionManagement(session -> session.sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS));

        return http.build();
    }

    private void configureAuthorizeHttpRequests(AuthorizeHttpRequestsConfigurer<HttpSecurity>.AuthorizationManagerRequestMatcherRegistry authorizeRequests) {
        authorizeRequests
                // Cho phép xác thực người dùng (Đăng ký, Đăng nhập, OTP)
                .requestMatchers(authApiPatterns()).permitAll()
                // Cho phép khách hàng thao tác
                .requestMatchers(customerApiPatterns()).permitAll()
                // Cho phép các endpoint nghiệp vụ F&B
                .requestMatchers(branchApiPatterns()).permitAll()
                .requestMatchers(menuApiPatterns()).permitAll()
                .requestMatchers(orderApiPatterns()).permitAll()
                .requestMatchers(userApiPatterns()).permitAll()
                .requestMatchers(tableApiPatterns()).permitAll()
                // Cho phép endpoint lỗi mặc định để thấy lỗi chi tiết
                .requestMatchers(new AntPathRequestMatcher("/error")).permitAll()
                .anyRequest().authenticated();
    }

    // region auth
    private static RequestMatcher[] authApiPatterns() {
        return new RequestMatcher[]{
                new AntPathRequestMatcher("/api/auth"),
                new AntPathRequestMatcher("/api/auth/**")
        };
    }
    // endregion

    // region customer
    private static RequestMatcher[] customerApiPatterns() {
        return new RequestMatcher[]{
                new AntPathRequestMatcher("/api/customers"),
                new AntPathRequestMatcher("/api/customers/**")
        };
    }
    // endregion

    // region branch
    private static RequestMatcher[] branchApiPatterns() {
        return new RequestMatcher[]{
                new AntPathRequestMatcher("/api/branches"),
                new AntPathRequestMatcher("/api/branches/**")
        };
    }
    // endregion

    // region menu
    private static RequestMatcher[] menuApiPatterns() {
        return new RequestMatcher[]{
                new AntPathRequestMatcher("/api/menu-items"),
                new AntPathRequestMatcher("/api/menu-items/**")
        };
    }
    // endregion

    // region order
    private static RequestMatcher[] orderApiPatterns() {
        return new RequestMatcher[]{
                new AntPathRequestMatcher("/api/orders"),
                new AntPathRequestMatcher("/api/orders/**")
        };
    }
    // endregion

    // region user
    private static RequestMatcher[] userApiPatterns() {
        return new RequestMatcher[]{
                new AntPathRequestMatcher("/api/users"),
                new AntPathRequestMatcher("/api/users/**")
        };
    }
    // endregion

    // region table
    private static RequestMatcher[] tableApiPatterns() {
        return new RequestMatcher[]{
                new AntPathRequestMatcher("/api/tables"),
                new AntPathRequestMatcher("/api/tables/**")
        };
    }
    // endregion
}  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\controller\AuthController.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.controller;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.OtpPurpose;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.AuthDto;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.base.response.ApiResponse;
import com.omniresto.backend.service.AuthService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PutMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/auth")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AuthController {

    private final AuthService authService;

    // 1. API Đăng ký tài khoản
    @PostMapping("/register")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<String>> register(@Valid @RequestBody AuthDto.RegisterRequest request) {
        authService.register(request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.success("Đăng ký tài khoản thành công! Mã OTP đã được gửi đến email của bạn.", null));
    }

    // 2. API Xác minh OTP đăng ký tài khoản
    @PostMapping("/verify-register-otp")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<String>> verifyRegisterOtp(@Valid @RequestBody AuthDto.VerifyRegisterOtpRequest request) {
        authService.verifyRegisterOtp(request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.success("Tài khoản của bạn đã được kích hoạt thành công. Hãy đăng nhập!", null));
    }

    // 3. API Đăng nhập
    @PostMapping("/login")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<AuthDto.AuthResponse>> login(@Valid @RequestBody AuthDto.LoginRequest request) {
        AuthDto.AuthResponse response = authService.login(request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.success("Đăng nhập thành công!", response));
    }

    // 4. API Yêu cầu gửi mã OTP quên mật khẩu
    @PostMapping("/forgot-password")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<String>> forgotPassword(@Valid @RequestBody AuthDto.SendOtpRequest request) {
        authService.requestPasswordReset(request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.success("Mã OTP khôi phục mật khẩu đã được gửi về email của bạn.", null));
    }

    // 5. API Xác minh OTP quên mật khẩu
    @PostMapping("/verify-reset-otp")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<String>> verifyResetOtp(@Valid @RequestBody AuthDto.VerifyResetOtpRequest request) {
        authService.verifyResetOtp(request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.success("Mã OTP hợp lệ! Bạn có thể đặt lại mật khẩu mới.", null));
    }

    // 6. API Đặt lại mật khẩu mới
    @PostMapping("/reset-password")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<String>> resetPassword(@Valid @RequestBody AuthDto.ResetPasswordRequest request) {
        authService.resetPassword(request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.success("Đặt lại mật khẩu thành công! Bạn có thể đăng nhập ngay.", null));
    }

    // 7. API Gửi lại mã OTP
    @PostMapping("/resend-otp")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<String>> resendOtp(@RequestParam String email, @RequestParam OtpPurpose purpose) {
        authService.resendOtp(email, purpose);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.success("Mã OTP mới đã được gửi thành công!", null));
    }

    // --- ENDPOINTS QUẢN LÝ DÀNH CHO ADMIN ---

    // 8. Admin lấy danh sách thành viên/đăng ký
    @GetMapping("/admin/users")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<List<AuthDto.UserAdminDto>>> getAllUsers() {
        List<AuthDto.UserAdminDto> users = authService.getAllUsers();
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.success("Lấy danh sách thành viên thành công!", users));
    }

    // 9. Admin cập nhật trạng thái tài khoản
    @PutMapping("/admin/users/{id}/status")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<String>> updateUserStatus(
            @PathVariable UUID id,
            @RequestBody AuthDto.UpdateUserStatusRequest request) {
        authService.updateUserStatus(id, request.getStatus());
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.success("Cập nhật trạng thái người dùng thành công!", null));
    }
}

package com.omniresto.backend.controller;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.BranchStatus;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.branch.request.BranchRequest;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.branch.response.BranchResponse;
import com.omniresto.backend.service.BranchService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.List;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/branches")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@CrossOrigin(origins = "*")
public class BranchController {

    private final BranchService branchService;

    @GetMapping
    public ResponseEntity<List<BranchResponse>> getAllBranches() {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(branchService.getAllBranches());
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<BranchResponse> getBranchById(@PathVariable String id) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(branchService.getBranchById(id));
    }

    @PostMapping
    public ResponseEntity<BranchResponse> createBranch(@RequestBody BranchRequest request) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(branchService.createBranch(request));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/{id}/status")
    public ResponseEntity<BranchResponse> updateBranchStatus(
            @PathVariable String id,
            @RequestParam BranchStatus status
    ) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(branchService.updateBranchStatus(id, status));
    }
}

package com.omniresto.backend.controller;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.CustomerEntity;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.request.*;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.response.AuthResponse;
import com.omniresto.backend.service.CustomerAuthService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/auth/customer")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class CustomerAuthController {

    private final CustomerAuthService customerAuthService;

    // 1. Gửi OTP đến số điện thoại
    @PostMapping("/send-otp")
    public ResponseEntity<Map<String, String>> sendOtp(@Valid @RequestBody SendOtpRequest request) {
        customerAuthService.sendOtp(request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(Map.of("message", "Mã OTP đã được gửi đến số điện thoại thành công!"));
    }

    // 2. Đăng ký tài khoản Thành viên
    @PostMapping("/register")
    public ResponseEntity<AuthResponse> register(@Valid @RequestBody CustomerRegisterRequest request) {
        AuthResponse response = customerAuthService.register(request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
    }

    // 3. Đăng nhập Thành viên
    @PostMapping("/login")
    public ResponseEntity<AuthResponse> login(@Valid @RequestBody CustomerLoginRequest request) {
        AuthResponse response = customerAuthService.login(request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
    }

    // 4. Quên mật khẩu
    @PostMapping("/forgot-password")
    public ResponseEntity<Map<String, String>> forgotPassword(@Valid @RequestBody ForgotPasswordRequest request) {
        customerAuthService.forgotPassword(request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(Map.of("message", "Đặt lại mật khẩu thành công!"));
    }

    // 5. Lấy danh sách thành viên (Dành cho Admin/Quản lý)
    @GetMapping("/admin/customers")
    public ResponseEntity<List<CustomerEntity>> getAllCustomersForAdmin() {
        List<CustomerEntity> customers = customerAuthService.getAllCustomersForAdmin();
        return ResponseEntity.ok(customers);
    }
}

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\domain\BranchEntity.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.domain;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.base.BaseEntity;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.BranchStatus;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

@Entity
@Table(name = "branches")
@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
public class BranchEntity extends BaseEntity {

    @Column(nullable = false, unique = true, length = 20)
    private String code;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 100)
    private String name;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private String address;

    @Column(length = 20)
    private String phone;

    @Column(name = "manager_name", length = 100)
    private String managerName;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    @Builder.Default
    private BranchStatus status = BranchStatus.active;

    @Column(name = "tables_count", nullable = false)
    @Builder.Default
    private Integer tablesCount = 0;

    @Column(name = "daily_revenue", nullable = false, precision = 15, scale = 2)

package com.omniresto.backend.domain;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.CustomerStatus;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.MembershipTier;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Builder;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Setter;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.UUID;

@Entity
@Table(name = "customers",
        uniqueConstraints = {
                @UniqueConstraint(columnNames = "email"),
                @UniqueConstraint(columnNames = "phone")
        })
@Getter
@Setter
@Builder
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
public class CustomerEntity {

    @Id
    @Column(length = 20)
    private String id;

    @Column(name = "full_name", nullable = false, length = 100)
    private String fullName;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 100)
    private String email;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private String phone;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private String password; // đã mã hoá BCrypt

    @Column(name = "date_of_birth")
    private LocalDate dateOfBirth;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "membership_tier", nullable = false, length = 20)
    private MembershipTier membershipTier;

    @Column(name = "loyalty_points", nullable = false)
    private Integer loyaltyPoints;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private CustomerStatus status;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @PrePersist
    void onCreate() {
        if (this.id == null) {
            this.id = "cus-" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().substring(0, 8);
        }
        this.createdAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}

package com.omniresto.backend.domain;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.base.BaseEntity;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.OrderStatus;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.OrderType;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.PaymentMethod;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

@Entity
@Table(name = "orders")
@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
public class OrderEntity extends BaseEntity {

    @Column(name = "order_number", nullable = false, unique = true, length = 30)
    private String orderNumber;

    @Column(name = "branch_id", nullable = false, length = 36)
    private String branchId;

    @Column(name = "table_id", length = 36)
    private String tableId;

    @Column(name = "table_no", length = 30)
    private String tableNo;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "order_type", nullable = false, length = 20)
    @Builder.Default
    private OrderType orderType = OrderType.dine_in;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    @Builder.Default
    private OrderStatus status = OrderStatus.pending;

    @Column(name = "total_amount", nullable = false, precision = 15, scale = 2)
    @Builder.Default
    private BigDecimal totalAmount = BigDecimal.ZERO;

    @Column(name = "customer_name", length = 100)
    private String customerName;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "payment_method", length = 20)
    @Builder.Default
    private PaymentMethod paymentMethod = PaymentMethod.cash;

    @Column(name = "created_by_user_id", length = 36)
    private String createdByUserId;

    @OneToMany(mappedBy = "order", cascade = CascadeType.ALL, orphanRemoval = true)
    @Builder.Default
    private List<OrderItemEntity> items = new ArrayList<>();

    public void addItem(OrderItemEntity item) {
        items.add(item);
        item.setOrder(this);
    }
}

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\domain\base\BaseEntity.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.domain.base;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;
import org.springframework.data.annotation.CreatedDate;
import org.springframework.data.annotation.LastModifiedDate;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.domain.support.AuditingEntityListener;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.UUID;

@Getter
@Setter
@MappedSuperclass
@EntityListeners(AuditingEntityListener.class)
public abstract class BaseEntity {

    @Id
    @Column(length = 36, nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private String id;

    @CreatedDate
    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @LastModifiedDate
    @Column(name = "updated_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @PrePersist
    public void ensureId() {
        if (this.id == null || this.id.isBlank()) {
            this.id = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\model\AuthDto.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.model;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.RoleType;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.UserStatus;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Email;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Pattern;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;
import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Builder;
import lombok.Data;
import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.UUID;

public class AuthDto {

    // 1. Request Đăng ký tài khoản
    @Data
    @NoArgsConstructor
    @AllArgsConstructor
    @Builder
    public static class RegisterRequest {
        @NotBlank(message = "Họ và tên không được để trống")
        private String fullName;

        @NotBlank(message = "Email không được để trống")
        @Email(message = "Email không đúng định dạng")
        private String email;

        @NotBlank(message = "Số điện thoại không được để trống")
        @Pattern(regexp = "^(0|\\+84)[0-9]{9}$", message = "Số điện thoại không hợp lệ")
        private String phone;

        @NotBlank(message = "Mật khẩu không được để trống")
        @Size(min = 6, message = "Mật khẩu phải từ 6 ký tự trở lên")
        private String password;
    }

    // 2. Request Yêu cầu gửi lại mã OTP (Đăng ký hoặc Quên mật khẩu)
    @Data
    @NoArgsConstructor
    @AllArgsConstructor
    public static class SendOtpRequest {
        @NotBlank(message = "Email không được để trống")
        @Email(message = "Email không đúng định dạng")
        private String email;
    }

    // 3. Request Xác minh OTP đăng ký
    @Data
    @NoArgsConstructor
    @AllArgsConstructor
    public static class VerifyRegisterOtpRequest {
        @NotBlank(message = "Email không được để trống")
        @Email(message = "Email không đúng định dạng")
        private String email;

        @NotBlank(message = "Mã OTP không được để trống")
        @Size(min = 6, max = 6, message = "Mã OTP phải đúng 6 chữ số")
        private String otpCode;
    }

    // 4. Request Đăng nhập
    @Data
    @NoArgsConstructor
    @AllArgsConstructor
    public static class LoginRequest {
        @NotBlank(message = "Email hoặc số điện thoại không được để trống")
        private String usernameOrEmail;

        @NotBlank(message = "Mật khẩu không được để trống")
        private String password;
    }

    // 5. Request Xác minh OTP quên mật khẩu
    @Data
    @NoArgsConstructor
    @AllArgsConstructor
    public static class VerifyResetOtpRequest {
        @NotBlank(message = "Email không được để trống")
        @Email(message = "Email không đúng định dạng")
        private String email;

        @NotBlank(message = "Mã OTP không được để trống")
        @Size(min = 6, max = 6, message = "Mã OTP phải đúng 6 chữ số")
        private String otpCode;
    }

    // 6. Request Đặt lại mật khẩu mới (sau khi xác minh OTP thành công)
    @Data
    @NoArgsConstructor
    @AllArgsConstructor
    public static class ResetPasswordRequest {
        @NotBlank(message = "Email không được để trống")
        @Email(message = "Email không đúng định dạng")
        private String email;

        @NotBlank(message = "Mã OTP không được để trống")
        private String otpCode;

        @NotBlank(message = "Mật khẩu mới không được để trống")
        @Size(min = 6, message = "Mật khẩu mới phải từ 6 ký tự trở lên")
        private String newPassword;
    }

    // 7. Response Trả về thông tin Auth thành công
    @Data
    @NoArgsConstructor
    @AllArgsConstructor
    @Builder
    public static class AuthResponse {
        private String token;
        private UUID userId;
        private String fullName;
        private String email;
        private RoleType role;
        private UserStatus status;
    }

    // 8. DTO Quản lý thông tin User hiển thị ở Admin
    @Data
    @NoArgsConstructor
    @AllArgsConstructor
    @Builder
    public static class UserAdminDto {
        private UUID id;
        private String fullName;
        private String email;
        private String phone;
        private RoleType role;
        private UserStatus status;
        private LocalDateTime createdAt;
    }

    // 9. Request Cập nhật trạng thái User từ Admin (Khóa, Duyệt, Kích hoạt)
    @Data
    @NoArgsConstructor
    @AllArgsConstructor
    public static class UpdateUserStatusRequest {
        private UserStatus status;
    }
}  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\model\base\response\ApiResponse.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.model.base.response;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonRootName;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Getter
@Setter
@JsonRootName("api_response")
public class ApiResponse<T> {
    private int code;
    private String message;
    private T data;
    private LocalDateTime timestamp;

    public ApiResponse() {}

    public ApiResponse(int code, String message, T data) {
        this.code = code;
        this.message = message;
        this.data = data;
        this.timestamp = LocalDateTime.now();
    }

    public static <T> ApiResponse<T> success(T data) {
        return new ApiResponse<>(200, "Success", data);
    }

    public static <T> ApiResponse<T> success(String message, T data) {
        return new ApiResponse<>(200, message, data);
    }
}

package com.omniresto.backend.model.branch.response;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonRootName;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.BranchStatus;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Getter
@Setter
@JsonRootName("branch")
public class BranchResponse {
    private String id;
    private String code;
    private String name;
    private String address;
    private String phone;
    private String managerName;
    private BranchStatus status;

@Builder
@NoArgsConstructor

    @Size(min = 6, max = 6, message = "Mã OTP phải bao gồm đúng 6 chữ số")
    private String otp;
}

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\model\response\ApiResponse.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.model.response;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonRootName;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Getter
@Setter
@JsonRootName("api_response")
public class ApiResponse<T> {
    private int code;
    private String message;
    private T data;
    private LocalDateTime timestamp;

    public ApiResponse() {}

    public ApiResponse(int code, String message, T data) {
        this.code = code;
        this.message = message;
        this.data = data;
        this.timestamp = LocalDateTime.now();
    }

    public static <T> ApiResponse<T> success(T data) {
        return new ApiResponse<>(200, "Success", data);
    }

    public static <T> ApiResponse<T> success(String message, T data) {
        return new ApiResponse<>(200, message, data);
    }
}

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\model\user\request\CustomerRegisterRequest.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.model.user.request;

import jakarta.validation.constraints.AssertTrue;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Email;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Past;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Pattern;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;

import java.time.LocalDate;

@Getter
@Setter
public class CustomerRegisterRequest {

    @NotBlank(message = "Họ tên không được để trống")
    @Size(max = 100, message = "Họ tên tối đa 100 ký tự")
    private String fullName;

    @NotBlank(message = "Email không được để trống")
    @Email(message = "Email không hợp lệ")
    private String email;

    @NotBlank(message = "Số điện thoại không được để trống")
    @Pattern(regexp = "^(0|\\+84)\\d{9}$", message = "Số điện thoại không hợp lệ")
    private String phone;

    @NotBlank(message = "Mật khẩu không được để trống")
    @Size(min = 8, max = 64, message = "Mật khẩu phải từ 8 đến 64 ký tự")
    @Pattern(regexp = "^(?=.*[A-Za-z])(?=.*\\d).+$", message = "Mật khẩu phải có cả chữ và số")
    private String password;

    @NotBlank(message = "Vui lòng nhập lại mật khẩu")
    private String confirmPassword;

    @Past(message = "Ngày sinh phải ở quá khứ")
    private LocalDate dateOfBirth; // không bắt buộc

    @AssertTrue(message = "Mật khẩu nhập lại không khớp")
    public boolean isPasswordConfirmed() {
        return password != null && password.equals(confirmPassword);
    }
}

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\service\AuthService.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.service;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.UserEntity;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.OtpPurpose;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.RoleType;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.UserStatus;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.AuthDto;
import com.omniresto.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AuthService {

    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;
    private final OtpService otpService;

    // 1. Đăng ký tài khoản mới (Tạo user ở trạng thái PENDING_VERIFICATION và gửi OTP)
    @Transactional
    public void register(AuthDto.RegisterRequest request) {
        if (userRepository.existsByEmail(request.getEmail())) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Email đã được sử dụng trong hệ thống!");
        }

        if (userRepository.existsByPhone(request.getPhone())) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Số điện thoại đã được sử dụng trong hệ thống!");
        }

        UserEntity user = UserEntity.builder()
                .fullName(request.getFullName())
                .email(request.getEmail())
                .phone(request.getPhone())
                .password(passwordEncoder.encode(request.getPassword()))
                .role(RoleType.CUSTOMER)
                .status(UserStatus.PENDING_VERIFICATION)
                .build();

        userRepository.save(user);

        // Sinh và gửi OTP xác minh đăng ký
        otpService.generateAndSendOtp(request.getEmail(), OtpPurpose.REGISTER_VERIFICATION);
    }

    // 2. Xác minh OTP Đăng ký
    @Transactional
    public void verifyRegisterOtp(AuthDto.VerifyRegisterOtpRequest request) {
        boolean isValid = otpService.verifyOtp(
                request.getEmail(),
                request.getOtpCode(),
                OtpPurpose.REGISTER_VERIFICATION
        );

        if (!isValid) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Mã OTP không chính xác hoặc đã hết hạn!");
        }

        UserEntity user = userRepository.findByEmail(request.getEmail())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Không tìm thấy tài khoản!"));

        user.setStatus(UserStatus.ACTIVE);
        userRepository.save(user);
    }

    // 3. Đăng nhập
    public AuthDto.AuthResponse login(AuthDto.LoginRequest request) {
        UserEntity user = userRepository.findByEmailOrPhone(request.getUsernameOrEmail(), request.getUsernameOrEmail())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Tài khoản hoặc mật khẩu không chính xác!"));

        if (!passwordEncoder.matches(request.getPassword(), user.getPassword())) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Tài khoản hoặc mật khẩu không chính xác!");
        }

        if (user.getStatus() == UserStatus.PENDING_VERIFICATION) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Tài khoản chưa được xác minh OTP. Vui lòng hoàn tất xác minh!");
        }

        if (user.getStatus() == UserStatus.BLOCKED || user.getStatus() == UserStatus.INACTIVE) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Tài khoản của bạn đã bị khóa hoặc ngừng hoạt động!");
        }

        String mockJwtToken = "Bearer_Token_" + user.getId();

        return AuthDto.AuthResponse.builder()
                .token(mockJwtToken)
                .userId(UUID.fromString(user.getId()))// Truyền trực tiếp kiểu UUID
                .fullName(user.getFullName())
                .email(user.getEmail())
                .role(user.getRole())
                .status(user.getStatus())
                .build();
    }

    // 4. Yêu cầu mã OTP Quên mật khẩu
    @Transactional
    public void requestPasswordReset(AuthDto.SendOtpRequest request) {
        UserEntity user = userRepository.findByEmail(request.getEmail())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Không tìm thấy tài khoản với email này!"));

        if (user.getStatus() == UserStatus.BLOCKED) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Tài khoản hiện đang bị khóa!");
        }

        otpService.generateAndSendOtp(request.getEmail(), OtpPurpose.PASSWORD_RESET);
    }

    // 5. Xác minh OTP Quên mật khẩu
    public void verifyResetOtp(AuthDto.VerifyResetOtpRequest request) {
        boolean isValid = otpService.verifyOtp(
                request.getEmail(),
                request.getOtpCode(),
                OtpPurpose.PASSWORD_RESET
        );

        if (!isValid) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Mã OTP không chính xác hoặc đã hết hạn!");
        }
    }

    // 6. Đặt lại mật khẩu mới
    @Transactional
    public void resetPassword(AuthDto.ResetPasswordRequest request) {
        UserEntity user = userRepository.findByEmail(request.getEmail())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Không tìm thấy tài khoản!"));

        user.setPassword(passwordEncoder.encode(request.getNewPassword()));
        userRepository.save(user);
    }

    // 7. Gửi lại mã OTP (cho Đăng ký hoặc Quên mật khẩu)
    @Transactional
    public void resendOtp(String email, OtpPurpose purpose) {
        if (!userRepository.existsByEmail(email)) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Email chưa được đăng ký trong hệ thống!");
        }
        otpService.generateAndSendOtp(email, purpose);
    }

    // --- CÁC CHỨC NĂNG DÀNH CHO ADMIN QUẢN LÝ THÀNH VIÊN ---

    // 8. Admin Lấy danh sách tất cả người dùng
    public List<AuthDto.UserAdminDto> getAllUsers() {
        return userRepository.findAll().stream()
                .map(user -> AuthDto.UserAdminDto.builder()
                        .id(UUID.fromString(user.getId())) // Truyền trực tiếp kiểu UUID
                        .fullName(user.getFullName())
                        .email(user.getEmail())
                        .phone(user.getPhone())
                        .role(user.getRole())
                        .status(user.getStatus())
                        .createdAt(user.getCreatedAt())
                        .build())
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    // 9. Admin Cập nhật trạng thái tài khoản
    @Transactional
    public void updateUserStatus(UUID userId, UserStatus status) {
        // Chuyển UUID userId sang String bằng String.valueOf() hoặc userId.toString()
        UserEntity user = userRepository.findById(String.valueOf(userId))
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Không tìm thấy người dùng!"));

        user.setStatus(status);
        userRepository.save(user);
    }
}  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\service\BranchService.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.service;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.BranchEntity;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.BranchStatus;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.branch.request.BranchRequest;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.branch.response.BranchResponse;
import com.omniresto.backend.repository.BranchRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class BranchService {

    private final BranchRepository branchRepository;

    public List<BranchResponse> getAllBranches() {
        return branchRepository.findAll().stream()
                .map(this::mapToResponse)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    public BranchResponse getBranchById(String id) {
        return branchRepository.findById(id)
                .map(this::mapToResponse)
                .orElse(null);
    }

    public BranchResponse createBranch(BranchRequest request) {
        BranchEntity entity = BranchEntity.builder()
                .code(request.getCode())
                .name(request.getName())
                .address(request.getAddress())
                .phone(request.getPhone())
                .managerName(request.getManagerName())
                .status(request.getStatus() != null ? request.getStatus() : BranchStatus.active)
                .tablesCount(request.getTablesCount() != null ? request.getTablesCount() : 0)
                .dailyRevenue(request.getDailyRevenue())
                .rating(request.getRating() != null ? request.getRating() : 5.0)
                .build();
        entity.setId("br-" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().substring(0, 8));

        BranchEntity saved = branchRepository.save(entity);
        return mapToResponse(saved);
    }

    public BranchResponse updateBranchStatus(String id, BranchStatus status) {
        BranchEntity entity = branchRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Branch not found: " + id));
        entity.setStatus(status);
        BranchEntity updated = branchRepository.save(entity);
        return mapToResponse(updated);
    }

    private BranchResponse mapToResponse(BranchEntity entity) {
        BranchResponse res = new BranchResponse();
        res.setId(entity.getId());
        res.setCode(entity.getCode());
        res.setName(entity.getName());
        res.setAddress(entity.getAddress());
        res.setPhone(entity.getPhone());
        res.setManagerName(entity.getManagerName() != null ? entity.getManagerName() : "N/A");
        res.setStatus(entity.getStatus());
        res.setTablesCount(entity.getTablesCount());
        res.setActiveOrdersCount(0);
        res.setDailyRevenue(entity.getDailyRevenue());
        res.setRating(entity.getRating());
        res.setCreatedAt(entity.getCreatedAt());
        return res;
    }
}
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\service\CustomerAuthService.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.service;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.CustomerEntity;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.CustomerStatus;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.MembershipTier;
import com.omniresto.backend.exception.BadRequestException;
import com.omniresto.backend.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.request.*;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.response.AuthResponse;
import com.omniresto.backend.repository.CustomerRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class CustomerAuthService {

    private final CustomerRepository customerRepository;
    private final OtpService otpService;
    private final PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;

    // 1. Gửi OTP xác minh số điện thoại
    public void sendOtp(SendOtpRequest request) {
        otpService.generateOtp(request.getPhone());
    }

    // 2. Đăng ký tài khoản Thành viên
    public AuthResponse register(CustomerRegisterRequest request) {
        // Xác minh OTP
        otpService.validateOtp(request.getPhone(), request.getOtp());

        // Kiểm tra số điện thoại đã tồn tại chưa
        if (customerRepository.existsByPhone(request.getPhone())) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Số điện thoại này đã được đăng ký tài khoản!");
        }

        // Kiểm tra email nếu người dùng có nhập
        if (request.getEmail() != null && !request.getEmail().isBlank()) {
            if (customerRepository.existsByEmail(request.getEmail())) {
                throw new BadRequestException("Email này đã được đăng ký tài khoản!");
            }
        }

        // Tạo mới Entity Customer
        CustomerEntity customer = CustomerEntity.builder()
                .fullName(request.getFullName())
                .phone(request.getPhone())
                .email(request.getEmail())
                .password(passwordEncoder.encode(request.getPassword()))
                .membershipTier(MembershipTier.standard)
                .loyaltyPoints(0)
                .status(CustomerStatus.active)
                .build();

        CustomerEntity savedCustomer = customerRepository.save(customer);

        return AuthResponse.builder()
                .token("MOCK_JWT_TOKEN_" + savedCustomer.getId())
                .type("Bearer")
                .id(savedCustomer.getId())
                .fullName(savedCustomer.getFullName())
                .phone(savedCustomer.getPhone())
                .email(savedCustomer.getEmail())
                .customerType("MEMBER")
                .memberTier(savedCustomer.getMembershipTier().name())
                .points(savedCustomer.getLoyaltyPoints())
                .build();
    }

    // 3. Đăng nhập
    public AuthResponse login(CustomerLoginRequest request) {
        CustomerEntity customer = customerRepository.findByPhoneOrEmail(request.getUsername(), request.getUsername())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new BadRequestException("Số điện thoại/Email hoặc mật khẩu không chính xác!"));

        if (!passwordEncoder.matches(request.getPassword(), customer.getPassword())) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Số điện thoại/Email hoặc mật khẩu không chính xác!");
        }

        if (customer.getStatus() == CustomerStatus.inactive) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Tài khoản của bạn đã bị khóa!");
        }

        return AuthResponse.builder()
                .token("MOCK_JWT_TOKEN_" + customer.getId())
                .type("Bearer")
                .id(customer.getId())
                .fullName(customer.getFullName())
                .phone(customer.getPhone())
                .email(customer.getEmail())
                .customerType("MEMBER")
                .memberTier(customer.getMembershipTier().name())
                .points(customer.getLoyaltyPoints())
                .build();
    }

    // 4. Quên mật khẩu
    public void forgotPassword(ForgotPasswordRequest request) {
        // Xác minh OTP
        otpService.validateOtp(request.getPhone(), request.getOtp());

        CustomerEntity customer = customerRepository.findByPhone(request.getPhone())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("Không tìm thấy tài khoản với số điện thoại này!"));

        customer.setPassword(passwordEncoder.encode(request.getNewPassword()));
        customerRepository.save(customer);
    }

    // 5. Dành cho Admin: Quản lý danh sách thành viên
    public List<CustomerEntity> getAllCustomersForAdmin() {
        return customerRepository.findAllByOrderByCreatedAtDesc();
    }
}  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\service\CustomerService.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.service;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.CustomerEntity;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.CustomerStatus;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.MembershipTier;
import com.omniresto.backend.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.user.request.CustomerRegisterRequest;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.user.response.CustomerResponse;
import com.omniresto.backend.repository.CustomerRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class CustomerService {

    private final CustomerRepository customerRepository;
    private final PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;

    @Transactional
    public CustomerResponse register(CustomerRegisterRequest request) {
        String email = request.getEmail().trim().toLowerCase();
        String phone = request.getPhone().trim();

        if (customerRepository.existsByEmail(email)) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Email đã được sử dụng");
        }
        if (customerRepository.existsByPhone(phone)) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Số điện thoại đã được sử dụng");
        }

        CustomerEntity entity = CustomerEntity.builder()
                .fullName(request.getFullName().trim())
                .email(email)
                .phone(phone)
                .password(passwordEncoder.encode(request.getPassword()))
                .dateOfBirth(request.getDateOfBirth())
                .membershipTier(MembershipTier.standard)
                .loyaltyPoints(0)
                .status(CustomerStatus.active)
                .build();

        return mapToResponse(customerRepository.save(entity));
    }

    // [THÊM] Lấy danh sách khách hàng cho trang quản lý admin
    public List<CustomerResponse> getAllCustomers() {
        return customerRepository.findAllByOrderByCreatedAtDesc().stream()
                .map(this::mapToResponse)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    // [THÊM] Khóa / mở khóa tài khoản khách hàng
    @Transactional
    public CustomerResponse updateStatus(String id, CustomerStatus status) {
        CustomerEntity entity = customerRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("Không tìm thấy khách hàng: " + id));
        entity.setStatus(status);
        return mapToResponse(customerRepository.save(entity));
    }

    private CustomerResponse mapToResponse(CustomerEntity entity) {
        CustomerResponse res = new CustomerResponse();
        res.setId(entity.getId());
        res.setFullName(entity.getFullName());
        res.setEmail(entity.getEmail());
        res.setPhone(entity.getPhone());
        res.setDateOfBirth(entity.getDateOfBirth());
        res.setMembershipTier(entity.getMembershipTier());
        res.setLoyaltyPoints(entity.getLoyaltyPoints());
        res.setStatus(entity.getStatus());
        res.setCreatedAt(entity.getCreatedAt());
        return res;
    }
}  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\service\EmailService.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.service;

import jakarta.mail.MessagingException;
import jakarta.mail.internet.MimeMessage;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.mail.javamail.JavaMailSender;
import org.springframework.mail.javamail.MimeMessageHelper;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class EmailService {

    private final JavaMailSender mailSender;

    public void sendOtpEmail(String toEmail, String otpCode, String purposeText) {
        try {
            MimeMessage message = mailSender.createMimeMessage();
            MimeMessageHelper helper = new MimeMessageHelper(message, true, "UTF-8");

            helper.setTo(toEmail);
            helper.setSubject("OmniResto - Mã xác minh OTP (" + purposeText + ")");

            String htmlContent = "<div style=\"font-family: Arial, sans-serif; max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 20px; border: 1px solid #e0e0e0; border-radius: 8px;\">" +
                    "<h2 style=\"color: #e65100; text-align: center;\">OmniResto Restaurant</h2>" +
                    "<hr style=\"border: none; border-top: 1px solid #eee;\"/>" +
                    "<p>Xin chào,</p>" +
                    "<p>Bạn vừa yêu cầu mã xác minh cho mục đích: <strong>" + purposeText + "</strong>.</p>" +
                    "<div style=\"background-color: #f5f5f5; padding: 15px; text-align: center; border-radius: 6px; margin: 20px 0;\">" +
                    "<span style=\"font-size: 32px; font-weight: bold; letter-spacing: 5px; color: #d84315;\">" + otpCode + "</span>" +
                    "</div>" +
                    "<p style=\"color: #666; font-size: 14px;\">Mã OTP này có hiệu lực trong <strong>5 phút</strong>. Vui lòng không chia sẻ mã này với bất kỳ ai.</p>" +
                    "<br/>" +
                    "<p style=\"color: #999; font-size: 12px; text-align: center;\">Đây là email tự động, vui lòng không phản hồi email này.</p>" +
                    "</div>";

            helper.setText(htmlContent, true);
            mailSender.send(message);
            log.info("Đã gửi email OTP thành công tới: {}", toEmail);
        } catch (MessagingException e) {
            log.error("Lỗi khi gửi email OTP tới: {}", toEmail, e);
            throw new RuntimeException("Không thể gửi email OTP, vui lòng thử lại sau!");
        }
    }
}  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\service\MenuItemService.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.service;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.CategoryEntity;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.MenuItemEntity;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.MenuStatus;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.menu.request.MenuItemRequest;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.menu.response.MenuItemResponse;
import com.omniresto.backend.repository.CategoryRepository;
import com.omniresto.backend.repository.MenuItemRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class MenuItemService {

    private final MenuItemRepository menuItemRepository;
    private final CategoryRepository categoryRepository;

    public List<MenuItemResponse> getAllMenuItems() {
        return menuItemRepository.findAll().stream()
                .map(this::mapToResponse)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    public MenuItemResponse getMenuItemById(String id) {
        return menuItemRepository.findById(id)
                .map(this::mapToResponse)
                .orElse(null);
    }

    public MenuItemResponse createMenuItem(MenuItemRequest request) {
        CategoryEntity category = categoryRepository.findById(request.getCategoryId() != null ? request.getCategoryId() : "cat-01")
                .orElse(null);

        MenuItemEntity entity = MenuItemEntity.builder()
                .code(request.getCode())
                .name(request.getName())
                .category(category)
                .basePrice(request.getBasePrice())
                .description(request.getDescription())
                .status(request.getStatus() != null ? request.getStatus() : MenuStatus.available)
                .isBestSeller(request.getIsBestSeller() != null ? request.getIsBestSeller() : false)
                .imageUrl(request.getImageUrl() != null ? request.getImageUrl() : request.getImage())
                .build();
        entity.setId("m-" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().substring(0, 8));

        MenuItemEntity saved = menuItemRepository.save(entity);
        return mapToResponse(saved);
    }

    public MenuItemResponse updateMenuItemStatus(String id, MenuStatus status) {
        MenuItemEntity entity = menuItemRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Menu item not found: " + id));
        entity.setStatus(status);
        MenuItemEntity updated = menuItemRepository.save(entity);
        return mapToResponse(updated);
    }

    private MenuItemResponse mapToResponse(MenuItemEntity entity) {
        MenuItemResponse res = new MenuItemResponse();
        res.setId(entity.getId());
        res.setCode(entity.getCode());
        res.setName(entity.getName());
        res.setCategoryId(entity.getCategory() != null ? entity.getCategory().getId() : "cat-01");
        res.setCategory(entity.getCategory() != null ? entity.getCategory().getName() : "Phở & Bún");
        res.setBasePrice(entity.getBasePrice());
        res.setDescription(entity.getDescription());
        res.setStatus(entity.getStatus());
        res.setIsBestSeller(entity.getIsBestSeller());
        res.setImage(entity.getImageUrl());
        res.setBranchesCount(3);
        res.setCreatedAt(entity.getCreatedAt());
        return res;
    }
}
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\service\OrderService.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.service;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.OrderEntity;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.OrderItemEntity;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.OrderStatus;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.order.request.OrderRequest;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.order.response.OrderItemResponse;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.order.response.OrderResponse;
import com.omniresto.backend.repository.OrderItemRepository;
import com.omniresto.backend.repository.OrderRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class OrderService {

    private final OrderRepository orderRepository;
    private final OrderItemRepository orderItemRepository;

    public List<OrderResponse> getAllOrders(String branchId, OrderStatus status) {
        List<OrderEntity> orders;
        if (branchId != null && !branchId.equals("ALL")) {
            orders = orderRepository.findByBranchId(branchId);
        } else {
            orders = orderRepository.findAll();
        }

        if (status != null) {
            orders = orders.stream()
                    .filter(o -> o.getStatus() == status)
                    .collect(Collectors.toList());
        }

        return orders.stream()
                .map(this::mapToResponse)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    @Transactional
    public OrderResponse createOrder(OrderRequest request) {
        String orderId = "ord-" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().substring(0, 8);
        String orderNum = "ORD-" + (System.currentTimeMillis() % 100000);

        OrderEntity entity = OrderEntity.builder()
                .orderNumber(orderNum)
                .branchId(request.getBranchId() != null ? request.getBranchId() : "br-01")
                .orderType(request.getOrderType())
                .tableNo(request.getTableNo())
                .status(request.getStatus() != null ? request.getStatus() : OrderStatus.pending)
                .totalAmount(request.getTotalAmount())
                .paymentMethod(request.getPaymentMethod())
                .customerName(request.getCustomerName())
                .build();
        entity.setId(orderId);

        OrderEntity saved = orderRepository.save(entity);

        List<OrderItemEntity> itemEntities = new ArrayList<>();
        if (request.getItems() != null) {
            request.getItems().forEach(itemReq -> {
                OrderItemEntity item = OrderItemEntity.builder()
                        .order(saved)
                        .menuItemId(itemReq.getMenuItemId())
                        .itemName(itemReq.getName() != null ? itemReq.getName() : "Món Ăn Special")
                        .quantity(itemReq.getQuantity())
                        .unitPrice(itemReq.getPrice())
                        .notes(itemReq.getNotes())
                        .build();
                item.setId("item-" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().substring(0, 8));
                itemEntities.add(orderItemRepository.save(item));
            });
        }

        return mapToResponseWithItems(saved, itemEntities);
    }

    public OrderResponse updateOrderStatus(String id, OrderStatus status) {
        OrderEntity entity = orderRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Order not found: " + id));
        entity.setStatus(status);
        OrderEntity updated = orderRepository.save(entity);
        return mapToResponse(updated);
    }

    private OrderResponse mapToResponse(OrderEntity entity) {
        List<OrderItemEntity> items = orderItemRepository.findByOrderId(entity.getId());
        return mapToResponseWithItems(entity, items);
    }

    private OrderResponse mapToResponseWithItems(OrderEntity entity, List<OrderItemEntity> items) {
        List<OrderItemResponse> itemResponses = items.stream().map(i -> {
            OrderItemResponse res = new OrderItemResponse();
            res.setId(i.getId());
            res.setMenuItemId(i.getMenuItemId());
            res.setName(i.getItemName());
            res.setQuantity(i.getQuantity());
            res.setPrice(i.getUnitPrice());
            res.setNotes(i.getNotes());
            return res;
        }).collect(Collectors.toList());

        OrderResponse res = new OrderResponse();
        res.setId(entity.getId());
        res.setOrderNumber(entity.getOrderNumber());
        res.setBranchId(entity.getBranchId());
        res.setBranchName("OmniResto - Hoàn Kiếm");
        res.setOrderType(entity.getOrderType());
        res.setTableNo(entity.getTableNo());
        res.setStatus(entity.getStatus());
        res.setTotalAmount(entity.getTotalAmount());
        res.setPaymentMethod(entity.getPaymentMethod());
        res.setCustomerName(entity.getCustomerName());
        res.setItems(itemResponses);
        res.setCreatedAt(entity.getCreatedAt() != null ? entity.getCreatedAt() : LocalDateTime.now());
        return res;
    }
}
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\service\OtpService.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.service;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.OtpVerificationEntity;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.OtpPurpose;
import com.omniresto.backend.exception.BadRequestException;
import com.omniresto.backend.repository.OtpVerificationRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class OtpService {

    private final OtpVerificationRepository otpVerificationRepository;
    private final EmailService emailService;
    private static final SecureRandom random = new SecureRandom();

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // 1. LUỒNG XÁC THỰC QUA SỐ ĐIỆN THOẠI (Dùng bộ nhớ RAM tạm)
    // Phục vụ cho CustomerAuthService (Khách hàng vãng lai / Hội viên)
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private final Map<String, OtpData> otpStorage = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private static final long OTP_VALID_DURATION = 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5 phút

    private static class OtpData {
        String code;
        long expiryTime;

        OtpData(String code, long expiryTime) {
            this.code = code;
            this.expiryTime = expiryTime;
        }
    }

    public String generateOtp(String phone) {
        String otp = String.format("%06d", random.nextInt(900000) + 100000);
        long expiryTime = System.currentTimeMillis() + OTP_VALID_DURATION;
        otpStorage.put(phone, new OtpData(otp, expiryTime));

        // Mô phỏng log gửi SMS console
        log.info("====== [OMNI RESTO OTP SMS] ======");
        log.info("Gửi OTP tới SĐT: {}", phone);
        log.info("Mã OTP của bạn là: {}", otp);
        log.info("===================================");

        return otp;
    }

    public boolean validateOtp(String phone, String inputOtp) {
        OtpData otpData = otpStorage.get(phone);

        if (otpData == null) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Mã OTP chưa được gửi hoặc đã hết hạn!");
        }

        if (System.currentTimeMillis() > otpData.expiryTime) {
            otpStorage.remove(phone);
            throw new BadRequestException("Mã OTP đã hết hạn, vui lòng yêu cầu mã mới!");
        }

        if (!otpData.code.equals(inputOtp)) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Mã OTP không chính xác!");
        }

        otpStorage.remove(phone);
        return true;
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // 2. LUỒNG XÁC THỰC QUA EMAIL (Lưu Database & Gửi JavaMailSender)
    // Phục vụ cho AuthService (Tài khoản người dùng / Đăng ký / Quên MK)
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    // Tạo mã OTP 6 chữ số ngẫu nhiên
    public String generateOtpCode() {
        int number = random.nextInt(900000) + 100000;
        return String.valueOf(number);
    }

    // Tạo và gửi mã OTP qua Email
    @Transactional
    public void generateAndSendOtp(String email, OtpPurpose purpose) {
        String otpCode = generateOtpCode();
        LocalDateTime expiredAt = LocalDateTime.now().plusMinutes(5);

        OtpVerificationEntity otpEntity = OtpVerificationEntity.builder()
                .email(email)
                .otpCode(otpCode)
                .purpose(purpose)
                .expiredAt(expiredAt)
                .isUsed(false)
                .build();

        otpVerificationRepository.save(otpEntity);

        String purposeText = purpose == OtpPurpose.REGISTER_VERIFICATION
                ? "Xác minh đăng ký tài khoản"
                : "Đặt lại mật khẩu";

        emailService.sendOtpEmail(email, otpCode, purposeText);
    }

    // Xác minh mã OTP Email từ Database
    @Transactional
    public boolean verifyOtp(String email, String otpCode, OtpPurpose purpose) {
        var otpOpt = otpVerificationRepository
                .findFirstByEmailAndOtpCodeAndPurposeAndIsUsedFalseAndExpiredAtAfter(
                        email, otpCode, purpose, LocalDateTime.now()
                );

        if (otpOpt.isPresent()) {
            OtpVerificationEntity otpEntity = otpOpt.get();
            otpEntity.setIsUsed(true);
            otpVerificationRepository.save(otpEntity);
            return true;
        }

        return false;
    }
}  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\service\TableService.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.service;

import com.omniresto.backend.domain.TableEntity;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.TableStatus;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.table.request.TableRequest;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.table.response.TableResponse;
import com.omniresto.backend.repository.TableRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class TableService {

    private final TableRepository tableRepository;

    public List<TableResponse> getTablesByBranch(String branchId, TableStatus status) {
        List<TableEntity> tables = tableRepository.findByBranchId(branchId);
        if (status != null) {
            tables = tables.stream().filter(t -> t.getStatus() == status).collect(Collectors.toList());
        }
        return tables.stream().map(this::mapToResponse).collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    public TableResponse updateTableStatus(String id, TableStatus status) {
        TableEntity entity = tableRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Table not found: " + id));
        entity.setStatus(status);
        TableEntity updated = tableRepository.save(entity);
        return mapToResponse(updated);
    }

    private TableResponse mapToResponse(TableEntity entity) {
        TableResponse res = new TableResponse();
        res.setId(entity.getId());
        res.setTableNo(entity.getTableNo());
        res.setBranchId(entity.getBranchId());
        res.setCapacity(entity.getCapacity());
        res.setStatus(entity.getStatus());
        res.setCurrentOrderAmount(BigDecimal.valueOf(150000));
        res.setOccupiedSince("15 phút trước");
        return res;
    }
}
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\backend\src\main\java\com\omniresto\backend\service\UserService.java =====  
package com.omniresto.backend.service;

import com.omniresto.backend.model.response.UserResponse;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.UserEntity;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.RoleType;
import com.omniresto.backend.domain.enums.UserStatus;
import com.omniresto.backend.model.user.request.UserRequest;
import com.omniresto.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class UserService {

    private final UserRepository userRepository;

    // Helper map từ Entity sang UserResponse DTO
    private UserResponse mapToUserResponse(UserEntity user) {
        return UserResponse.builder()
                .id(user.getId())
                .fullName(user.getFullName())
                .email(user.getEmail())
                .phone(user.getPhone())
                .role(user.getRole())
                .branchId(user.getBranchId())
                .status(user.getStatus())
                .createdAt(user.getCreatedAt())
                .build();
    }

    // 1. Lấy tất cả người dùng cho UserController
    public List<UserResponse> getAllUsers() {
        return userRepository.findAll().stream()
                .map(this::mapToUserResponse)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    // 2. Lấy thông tin user theo ID dạng DTO
    public UserResponse getUserById(String id) {
        UserEntity user = userRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Không tìm thấy người dùng với ID: " + id));
        return mapToUserResponse(user);
    }

    // 3. Tạo người dùng mới
    @Transactional
    public UserResponse createUser(UserRequest request) {
        if (userRepository.existsByEmail(request.getEmail())) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Email đã tồn tại!");
        }

        UserEntity user = UserEntity.builder()
                .fullName(request.getFullName())
                .email(request.getEmail())
                .phone(request.getPhone())
                .role(request.getRole() != null ? request.getRole() : RoleType.CUSTOMER)
                .status(UserStatus.ACTIVE)
                .build();

        UserEntity savedUser = userRepository.save(user);
        return mapToUserResponse(savedUser);
    }

    // 4. Cập nhật trạng thái người dùng
    @Transactional
    public UserResponse updateUserStatus(String id, UserStatus status) {
        UserEntity user = userRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Không tìm thấy người dùng với ID: " + id));

        user.setStatus(status);
        UserEntity updatedUser = userRepository.save(user);
        return mapToUserResponse(updatedUser);
    }

    // Các hàm bổ trợ nâng cao
    public List<UserEntity> getUsersByBranch(String branchId) {
        return userRepository.findByBranchId(branchId);
    }

    public List<UserEntity> getStoreManagers() {
        return userRepository.findByRole(RoleType.STORE_MANAGER);
    }

    public List<UserEntity> getUsersByRole(RoleType role) {
        return userRepository.findByRole(role);
    }

    @Transactional
    public void deleteUser(String id) {
        if (!userRepository.existsById(id)) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Không tìm thấy người dùng để xóa!");
        }
        userRepository.deleteById(id);
    }
}

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\README.md =====  
# OmniResto Frontend

**OmniResto Frontend** is the user web application for the OmniResto Vietnamese Restaurant Chain Management Platform. Built for high performance, ease of use, and visual appeal, it empowers restaurant managers, floor staff, waiters, and kitchen personnel to seamlessly coordinate daily dining room and kitchen operations.

---

## 🌟 Key Application Features

- **Branch Switcher & Multi-Location Context:** Easily toggle between different restaurant branches to view and manage location-specific operations.
- **Interactive Floor Plan & Table Manager:** Real-time visual floor map displaying live table statuses (Available, Occupied, Reserved, Cleaning).
- **Intuitive Point of Sale (POS):** Fast order entry interface tailored for Vietnamese dining, supporting category filters, special preparation notes (e.g., "Không hành", "Nhiều bánh"), and extra toppings.
- **Kitchen Display System (KDS):** Live digital ticket dashboard for kitchen staff to track preparing items and signal order readiness.
- **Billing & Receipt Management:** Integrated payment checkout flow supporting multiple payment methods and invoice preview.
- **Executive Analytics Dashboard:** Visual charts and metrics for daily sales, revenue breakdown by branch, and best-selling dishes.

---

## 🛠 Technology Stack

- **Core Library:** React 19 & TypeScript
- **Build & HMR Tool:** Vite
- **Router:** React Router DOM (v7)
- **HTTP Client:** Axios (with automated JWT interceptors)
- **Data Fetching & Caching:** TanStack Query (React Query)
- **State Management:** Zustand & React Context
- **Design & Styling:** Vanilla CSS with Design Tokens (Vietnamese culinary warm amber palette)

---

## 🚀 Getting Started

### Prerequisites
- **Node.js:** 18.x or higher
- **Package Manager:** npm 9.x+

### Local Setup & Execution

```bash
# Navigate to frontend directory
cd frontend

# Install dependencies
npm install

# Start local development server
npm run dev

# Run TypeScript type check
npx tsc -b

# Build production bundle
npm run build
```

The application will run locally at `http://localhost:5173`.

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Bell, Store, Calendar, ChevronDown, User, Package, LogOut, HelpCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAdminStore } from '../store/adminStore';
import { Modal } from '../../shared/components/Modal';
import './Header.css';

interface HeaderProps {
  onQuickAction?: () => void;
}

export const Header: React.FC<HeaderProps> = () => {
  const { branches, selectedBranchId, setSelectedBranchId } = useAdminStore();
  const [showNotifications, setShowNotifications] = useState(false);
  const [showProfileDropdown, setShowProfileDropdown] = useState(false);
  const [showProfileModal, setShowProfileModal] = useState(false);

  const todayStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString('vi-VN', {
    weekday: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    day: 'numeric',
  });

  const handleLogout = () => {
    setShowProfileDropdown(false);
    window.location.reload();
  };

  return (
    <>
      <header className="header">
        {/* Left: Branch Selector Only */}
        <div className="header-left">
          <div className="branch-selector-wrapper">
            <Store size={16} color="var(--primary)" />
            <span className="branch-selector-label">Chi nhánh:</span>
            <select
              className="branch-selector"
              value={selectedBranchId}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedBranchId(e.target.value)}
            >
              <option value="ALL">Tất cả Chi Nhánh (Trụ Sở HQ)</option>
              {branches.map((b) => (
                <option key={b.id} value={b.id}>
                  {b.name} ({b.code})
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <ChevronDown size={14} color="var(--primary)" />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right: Date, Notifications & User Profile Dropdown */}
        <div className="header-right">
          <div className="date-badge">
            <Calendar size={15} color="var(--primary)" />
            <span>{todayStr}</span>
          </div>

          {/* Notifications */}
          <div className="icon-btn-wrapper">
            <button
              className="btn btn-secondary btn-icon"
              onClick={() => {
                setShowNotifications(!showNotifications);
                setShowProfileDropdown(false);
              }}
              title="Thông báo hệ thống"
            >
              <Bell size={18} />
            </button>
            <span className="notification-dot" />

            {showNotifications && (
              <div
                className="card animate-fade-in"
                style={{
                  position: 'absolute',
                  top: '48px',
                  right: '0',
                  width: '320px',
                  zIndex: 200,
                  boxShadow: 'var(--shadow-xl)',
                  padding: '16px',
                }}
              >
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: '12px' }}>
                  <strong style={{ fontSize: '0.9rem' }}>Thông báo mới (2)</strong>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: '0.75rem', color: 'var(--primary)', cursor: 'pointer' }}>Đã đọc tất cả</span>
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '10px' }}>
                  <div
                    style={{
                      padding: '8px 10px',
                      backgroundColor: 'var(--primary-subtle)',
                      borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
