import React from 'react';
import { NavLink } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  Store,
  UtensilsCrossed,
  ShoppingBag,
  Grid2x2,
  Users,
  BarChart3,
  Settings,
  Flame,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useAdminStore } from '../store/adminStore';
import './Sidebar.css';

export const Sidebar: React.FC = () => {
  const { orders } = useAdminStore();
  const pendingOrdersCount = orders.filter((o) => o.status === 'pending').length;

  const navItems = [
    {
      section: 'VẬN HÀNH CHUỖI',
      items: [
        { label: 'Tổng Quan Hệ Thống', path: '/admin', icon: LayoutDashboard },
        { label: 'Quản Lý Chi Nhánh', path: '/admin/branches', icon: Store },
        { label: 'Master Menu & Món', path: '/admin/menu', icon: UtensilsCrossed },
        {
          label: 'Đơn Hàng Chuỗi',
          path: '/admin/orders',
          icon: ShoppingBag,
          badge: pendingOrdersCount > 0 ? pendingOrdersCount : undefined,
        },
        { label: 'Sơ Đồ Bàn & Đặt Bàn', path: '/admin/tables', icon: Grid2x2 },
      ],
    },
    {
      section: 'QUẢN TRỊ & PHÂN TÍCH',
      items: [
        { label: 'Nhân Sự & Phân Quyền', path: '/admin/staff', icon: Users },
        { label: 'Quản Lý Khách Hàng', path: '/admin/customers', icon: Users },
        { label: 'Báo Cáo Doanh Thu', path: '/admin/reports', icon: BarChart3 },
        { label: 'Cấu Hình Hệ Thống', path: '/admin/settings', icon: Settings },
      ],
    },
  ];

  return (
    <aside className="sidebar">
      {/* Brand Header */}
      <div className="sidebar-header">
        <div className="sidebar-logo-icon">
          <Flame size={24} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <span className="sidebar-brand-title">OmniResto</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Nav Menu */}
      <nav className="sidebar-nav">
        {navItems.map((group, idx) => (
          <div key={idx}>
            <div className="sidebar-section-title">{group.section}</div>
            {group.items.map((item) => {
              const Icon = item.icon;
              return (
                <NavLink
                  key={item.path}
                  to={item.path}
                  end={item.path === '/admin'}
                  className={({ isActive }) => `sidebar-item ${isActive ? 'active' : ''}`}
                >
                  <Icon size={19} />
                  <span style={{ flex: 1 }}>{item.label}</span>
                  {item.badge !== undefined && (
                    <span
                      style={{
                        backgroundColor: 'var(--danger)',
                        color: '#ffffff',
                        fontSize: '0.725rem',
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        padding: '1px 7px',
                        borderRadius: 'var(--radius-full)',
                      }}
                    >
                      {item.badge}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </NavLink>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        ))}
      </nav>
    </aside>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type { LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import { TrendingUp, TrendingDown } from 'lucide-react';

interface StatCardProps {
  title: string;
  value: string | number;
  change?: string;
  isPositive?: boolean;
  icon: LucideIcon;
  iconBgColor?: string;
  iconColor?: string;
  subtitle?: string;
}

export const StatCard: React.FC<StatCardProps> = ({
  title,
  value,
  change,
  isPositive = true,
  icon: Icon,
  iconBgColor = 'var(--primary-subtle)',
  iconColor = 'var(--primary)',
  subtitle,
}) => {
  return (
    <div className="card animate-fade-in" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px' }}>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
        <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>
          {title}
        </span>
        <div
          style={{
            width: '42px',
            height: '42px',
            borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
            backgroundColor: iconBgColor,
            color: iconColor,
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
          }}
        >
          <Icon size={22} />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'baseline', gap: '10px' }}>
        <span style={{ fontSize: '1.75rem', fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--text-main)', letterSpacing: '-0.5px' }}>
          {value}
        </span>
        {change && (
          <span
            style={{
              display: 'inline-flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              gap: '3px',
              fontSize: '0.8125rem',
              fontWeight: 700,
              color: isPositive ? 'var(--success)' : 'var(--danger)',
              backgroundColor: isPositive ? 'var(--success-bg)' : 'var(--danger-bg)',
              padding: '2px 8px',
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-full)',
            }}
          >
            {isPositive ? <TrendingUp size={14} /> : <TrendingDown size={14} />}
            {change}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      {subtitle && (
        <span style={{ fontSize: '0.78125rem', color: 'var(--text-light)' }}>
          {subtitle}
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\components\StatusBadge.tsx =====  
import React from 'react';

type StatusType =
  | 'ACTIVE'
  | 'INACTIVE'
  | 'MAINTENANCE'
  | 'CLOSED'
  | 'AVAILABLE'
  | 'OUT_OF_STOCK'
  | 'DISCONTINUED'
  | 'PENDING'
  | 'PREPARING'
  | 'READY'
  | 'COMPLETED'
  | 'CANCELLED'
  | 'OCCUPIED'
  | 'RESERVED'
  | 'CLEANING'
  | 'active'
  | 'inactive'
  | 'maintenance'
  | 'closed'
  | 'available'
  | 'out_of_stock'
  | 'discontinued'
  | 'pending'
  | 'preparing'
  | 'ready'
  | 'completed'
  | 'cancelled'
  | 'occupied'
  | 'reserved'
  | 'cleaning';

interface StatusBadgeProps {
  status: StatusType | string;
  label?: string;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md';
}

const statusMap: Record<string, { label: string; bg: string; color: string; border: string }> = {
  ACTIVE: { label: 'Đang hoạt động', bg: 'var(--success-bg)', color: 'var(--success)', border: 'var(--success-border)' },
  INACTIVE: { label: 'Tạm khóa', bg: 'var(--danger-bg)', color: 'var(--danger)', border: 'var(--danger-border)' },
  MAINTENANCE: { label: 'Bảo trì', bg: 'var(--warning-bg)', color: 'var(--warning)', border: 'var(--warning-border)' },
  CLOSED: { label: 'Đóng cửa', bg: 'var(--danger-bg)', color: 'var(--danger)', border: 'var(--danger-border)' },

  AVAILABLE: { label: 'Còn món', bg: 'var(--success-bg)', color: 'var(--success)', border: 'var(--success-border)' },
  OUT_OF_STOCK: { label: 'Hết món', bg: 'var(--warning-bg)', color: 'var(--warning)', border: 'var(--warning-border)' },
  DISCONTINUED: { label: 'Ngừng bán', bg: 'var(--danger-bg)', color: 'var(--danger)', border: 'var(--danger-border)' },

  PENDING: { label: 'Mới đặt', bg: 'var(--info-bg)', color: 'var(--info)', border: 'var(--info-border)' },
  PREPARING: { label: 'Đang làm', bg: 'var(--warning-bg)', color: 'var(--warning)', border: 'var(--warning-border)' },
  READY: { label: 'Chờ giao', bg: 'var(--purple-bg)', color: 'var(--purple)', border: '#ddd6fe' },
  COMPLETED: { label: 'Hoàn tất', bg: 'var(--success-bg)', color: 'var(--success)', border: 'var(--success-border)' },
  CANCELLED: { label: 'Đã hủy', bg: 'var(--danger-bg)', color: 'var(--danger)', border: 'var(--danger-border)' },

  OCCUPIED: { label: 'Có khách', bg: 'var(--danger-bg)', color: 'var(--danger)', border: 'var(--danger-border)' },
  RESERVED: { label: 'Đã đặt bàn', bg: 'var(--info-bg)', color: 'var(--info)', border: 'var(--info-border)' },
  CLEANING: { label: 'Cần dọn', bg: 'var(--warning-bg)', color: 'var(--warning)', border: 'var(--warning-border)' },
};

export const StatusBadge: React.FC<StatusBadgeProps> = ({ status, label, size = 'md' }) => {
  const normalizedKey = status ? String(status).toUpperCase() : '';
  const config = statusMap[normalizedKey] || {
    label: status,
    bg: 'var(--border-subtle)',
    color: 'var(--text-muted)',
    border: 'var(--border-color)',
  };

  const displayText = label || config.label;

  return (
    <span
      style={{
        display: 'inline-flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        gap: '6px',
        padding: size === 'sm' ? '2px 8px' : '4px 12px',
        fontSize: size === 'sm' ? '0.75rem' : '0.8125rem',
        fontWeight: 600,
        borderRadius: 'var(--radius-full)',
        backgroundColor: config.bg,
        color: config.color,
        border: `1px solid ${config.border}`,
        whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
      }}
    >
      <span
        style={{
          width: size === 'sm' ? '5px' : '6px',
          height: size === 'sm' ? '5px' : '6px',
          borderRadius: '50%',
          backgroundColor: config.color,
        }}
      />
      {displayText}
    </span>
  );
};

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\pages\auth\userApi.ts =====  
import { apiClient, transformAxiosErrorResponse } from '../../../shared/api/client';
import type { StaffUser, UserResponse, UserStatus } from '../../../shared/types/admin';

export const userApi = {
  getUsers: async (): Promise<StaffUser[]> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<StaffUser[] | UserResponse>('/users');
      if (Array.isArray(res.data)) return res.data;
      return res.data.users || [];
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },

  getUser: async (id: string): Promise<StaffUser> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<StaffUser | UserResponse>(`/users/${id}`);
      if ('user' in res.data && res.data.user) return res.data.user;
      return res.data as StaffUser;
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },

  createUser: async (request: Partial<StaffUser>): Promise<StaffUser> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.post<StaffUser | UserResponse>('/users', {
        user: request,
      });
      if ('user' in res.data && res.data.user) return res.data.user;
      return res.data as StaffUser;
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },

  updateUserStatus: async (id: string, status: UserStatus): Promise<StaffUser> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.patch<StaffUser | UserResponse>(`/users/${id}/status`, null, { params: { status } });
      if ('user' in res.data && res.data.user) return res.data.user;
      return res.data as StaffUser;
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },
};
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\pages\auth\validate.ts =====  
import * as yup from 'yup';
import type { UserFormData } from './types';

export const getValidateUserSchema = () => {
  return yup.object().shape({
    fullName: yup
      .string()
      .trim()
      .nullable()
      .required('Họ tên nhân viên không được để trống')
      .max(100, 'Họ tên không được vượt quá 100 ký tự'),
    email: yup
      .string()
      .trim()
      .nullable()
      .required('Email không được để trống')
      .email('Email không hợp lệ'),
    phone: yup
      .string()
      .trim()
      .nullable()
      .required('Số điện thoại không được để trống')
      .test('phone-format', 'Số điện thoại không hợp lệ', (val) => !val || /^\+?\d{9,15}$/.test(val)),
    role: yup
      .string()
      .trim()
      .nullable()
      .required('Vai trò nhân viên không được để trống'),
  });
};

export const validateUser = (data: Partial<UserFormData>) => {
  try {
    getValidateUserSchema().validateSync(data, { abortEarly: false });
    return { isValid: true, errors: {} };
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const errors: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (err instanceof yup.ValidationError) {
      err.inner.forEach((e) => {
        if (e.path) errors[e.path] = e.message;
      });
    }
    return { isValid: false, errors };
  }
};

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\pages\auth\StaffManagement.tsx =====  
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Plus, Search, Shield, CheckCircle, UserX, Crown } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAdminStore } from '../../store/adminStore';
import { StatusBadge } from '../../components/StatusBadge';
import { Modal } from '../../../shared/components/Modal';
import type { RoleType, UserStatus } from '../../../shared/types/admin';

const ROLE_MAP: Record<string, { label: string; bg: string; color: string; isMaster?: boolean }> = {
  admin: { label: 'Admin Chuỗi (Quyền cao nhất)', bg: 'var(--primary-light)', color: 'var(--primary)', isMaster: true },
  store_manager: { label: 'Quản Lý Cửa Hàng', bg: 'var(--purple-bg)', color: 'var(--purple)' },
  cashier: { label: 'Thu Ngân Quầy', bg: 'var(--info-bg)', color: 'var(--info)' },
  waiter: { label: 'Phục Vụ / Order Bàn', bg: 'var(--success-bg)', color: 'var(--success)' },
  chef: { label: 'Bếp / Bar Chế Biến', bg: 'var(--warning-bg)', color: 'var(--warning)' },
};

export const StaffManagement: React.FC = () => {
  const { staffList, branches, addStaff, toggleStaffStatus } = useAdminStore();
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [roleFilter, setRoleFilter] = useState<string>('ALL');

  // Modal State
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    fullName: '',
    email: '',
    phone: '',
    role: 'store_manager' as RoleType,
    branchId: 'br-01',
    avatar: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1534528741775-53994a69daeb?w=150&auto=format&fit=crop',
  });

  const filteredStaff = staffList.filter((usr) => {
    const matchesSearch =
      usr.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      usr.email.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      usr.phone.includes(searchQuery);
    const matchesRole = roleFilter === 'ALL' || usr.role === roleFilter;

    return matchesSearch && matchesRole;
  });

  const handleSubmitNewStaff = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!formData.fullName || !formData.email) return;

    const assignedBranch = branches.find((b) => b.id === formData.branchId);

    addStaff({
      fullName: formData.fullName,
      email: formData.email,
      phone: formData.phone,
      role: formData.role,
      branchId: formData.branchId,
      branchName: assignedBranch ? assignedBranch.name : 'Trụ Sở HQ',
      status: 'active',
      avatar: formData.avatar,
    });

    setIsModalOpen(false);
    setFormData({
      fullName: '',
      email: '',
      phone: '',
      role: 'store_manager',
      branchId: 'br-01',
      avatar: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1534528741775-53994a69daeb?w=150&auto=format&fit=crop',
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="animate-fade-in" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '32px' }}>
      {/* Header */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '20px' }}>
        <div>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: '1.75rem', fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
            Quản Lý Nhân Sự & Phân Quyền Vị Trí ({staffList.length})
          </h2>
          <p style={{ fontSize: '0.925rem', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginTop: '4px' }}>
            Phân quyền gán trực tiếp theo từng tài khoản vị trí công việc. Tài khoản đăng nhập chỉ được truy cập các tính năng thuộc quyền hạn của mình.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(true)}>
          <Plus size={19} />
          Cấp Tài Khoản Mới
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Filter Bar */}
      <div className="card" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: '20px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <div className="search-bar" style={{ width: '380px' }}>
          <Search size={18} color="var(--text-muted)" />
          <input
            type="text"
            className="search-input"
            placeholder="Tìm theo tên, email, sdt nhân viên..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' }}>
          <span style={{ fontSize: '0.9rem', fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Lọc vị trí:</span>
          <select
            className="form-select"
            style={{ width: '230px' }}
            value={roleFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setRoleFilter(e.target.value)}
          >
            <option value="ALL">Tất cả vị trí</option>
            <option value="admin">Admin / Chủ Chuỗi (Mọi quyền)</option>
            <option value="store_manager">Quản Lý Cửa Hàng</option>
            <option value="cashier">Thu Ngân Quầy</option>
            <option value="waiter">Phục Vụ / Order Bàn</option>
            <option value="chef">Bếp / Bar Chế Biến</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Staff Table Card - Spacious */}
      <div className="card" style={{ padding: '0', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
        <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', textAlign: 'left', fontSize: '0.925rem' }}>
          <thead>
            <tr style={{ backgroundColor: 'var(--bg-app)', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border-color)', color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: '0.8125rem', fontWeight: 800, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.6px' }}>
              <th style={{ padding: '18px 24px' }}>NHÂN VIÊN</th>
              <th style={{ padding: '18px 24px' }}>VỊ TRÍ & QUYỀN HẠN</th>
              <th style={{ padding: '18px 24px' }}>CHI NHÁNH PHỤ TRÁCH</th>
              <th style={{ padding: '18px 24px' }}>LIÊN HỆ</th>
              <th style={{ padding: '18px 24px' }}>TRẠNG THÁI</th>
              <th style={{ padding: '18px 24px', textAlign: 'right' }}>THAO TÁC</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {filteredStaff.map((usr) => {
              const roleInfo = ROLE_MAP[usr.role] || { label: usr.role, bg: '#eee', color: '#333' };
              return (
                <tr key={usr.id} style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border-subtle)', transition: 'background 0.15s' }}>
                  <td style={{ padding: '18px 24px' }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '14px' }}>
                      <img
                        src={usr.avatar}
                        alt={usr.fullName}
                        style={{ width: '44px', height: '44px', borderRadius: '50%', objectFit: 'cover' }}
                      />
                      <div>
                        <strong style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)', fontSize: '0.95rem' }}>
                          {usr.fullName}
                        </strong>
                        <div style={{ fontSize: '0.8125rem', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginTop: '2px' }}>
                          ID: {usr.id}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '18px 24px' }}>
                    <span
                      style={{
                        padding: '6px 14px',
                        borderRadius: 'var(--radius-full)',
                        fontSize: '0.8125rem',
                        fontWeight: 800,
                        backgroundColor: roleInfo.bg,
                        color: roleInfo.color,
                        display: 'inline-flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: '6px',
                      }}
                    >
                      {roleInfo.isMaster ? <Crown size={14} /> : <Shield size={14} />} {roleInfo.label}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '18px 24px', fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--text-body)' }}>
                    {usr.branchName}
                  </td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '18px 24px', color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: '0.85rem' }}>
                    <div>{usr.email}</div>
                    <div style={{ marginTop: '2px' }}>{usr.phone}</div>
                  </td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '18px 24px' }}>
                    <StatusBadge status={usr.status} />
                  </td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '18px 24px', textAlign: 'right' }}>
                    {usr.role === 'admin' ? (
                      <span style={{ fontSize: '0.8125rem', fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--primary)' }}>Quyền Hệ Thống</span>
                    ) : (
                      <button
                        className="btn btn-secondary btn-sm"
                        onClick={() => {
                          const nextStatus: UserStatus = usr.status === 'active' ? 'inactive' : 'active';
                          toggleStaffStatus(usr.id, nextStatus);
                        }}
                      >
                        {usr.status === 'active' ? <UserX size={15} /> : <CheckCircle size={15} />}
                        {usr.status === 'active' ? 'Tạm Khóa' : 'Mở Khóa'}
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              );
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      {/* Modal Add Staff */}
      <Modal isOpen={isModalOpen} onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)} title="Cấp Tài Khoản Nhân Viên & Phân Vị Trí">
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmitNewStaff}>
          <div className="form-group">
            <label className="form-label">Họ Và Tên Nhân Viên*</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              className="form-input"
              placeholder="VD: Nguyễn Văn Anh"
              required
              value={formData.fullName}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, fullName: e.target.value })}
            />
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '20px' }}>
            <div className="form-group">

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useAdminStore } from '../../../store/adminStore';

export const useUser = () => {
  const { staffList, branches, addStaff, toggleStaffStatus } = useAdminStore();
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [roleFilter, setRoleFilter] = useState<string>('ALL');

  const filteredStaff = staffList.filter((user) => {
    const matchesSearch =
      user.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      user.email.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      user.phone.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());
    const matchesRole = roleFilter === 'ALL' || user.role === roleFilter;
    return matchesSearch && matchesRole;
  });

  return { staffList: filteredStaff, branches, searchQuery, setSearchQuery, roleFilter, setRoleFilter, addStaff, toggleStaffStatus };
};
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\pages\branch\branchApi.ts =====  
import { apiClient, transformAxiosErrorResponse } from '../../../shared/api/client';
import type { Branch, BranchResponse, BranchStatus } from '../../../shared/types/admin';

export const branchApi = {
  getBranches: async (): Promise<Branch[]> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<Branch[] | BranchResponse>('/branches');
      if (Array.isArray(res.data)) return res.data;
      return res.data.branches || [];
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },

  getBranch: async (id: string): Promise<Branch> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<Branch | BranchResponse>(`/branches/${id}`);
      if ('branch' in res.data && res.data.branch) return res.data.branch;
      return res.data as Branch;
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },

  createBranch: async (request: Partial<Branch>): Promise<Branch> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.post<Branch | BranchResponse>('/branches', {
        branch: request,
      });
      if ('branch' in res.data && res.data.branch) return res.data.branch;
      return res.data as Branch;
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },

  updateBranchStatus: async (id: string, status: BranchStatus): Promise<Branch> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.patch<Branch | BranchResponse>(`/branches/${id}/status`, null, { params: { status } });
      if ('branch' in res.data && res.data.branch) return res.data.branch;
      return res.data as Branch;
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },
};

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\pages\branch\validate.ts =====  
import * as yup from 'yup';
import type { BranchFormData } from './types';

export const getValidateBranchSchema = () => {
  return yup.object().shape({
    code: yup
      .string()
      .trim()
      .nullable()
      .required('Mã chi nhánh không được để trống')
      .max(50, 'Mã chi nhánh không được vượt quá 50 ký tự'),
    name: yup
      .string()
      .trim()
      .nullable()
      .required('Tên chi nhánh không được để trống')
      .max(255, 'Tên chi nhánh không được vượt quá 255 ký tự'),
    address: yup
      .string()
      .trim()
      .nullable()
      .required('Địa chỉ không được để trống')
      .max(500, 'Địa chỉ không được vượt quá 500 ký tự'),
    phone: yup
      .string()
      .trim()
      .nullable()
      .test('phone-format', 'Số điện thoại không hợp lệ', (val) => !val || /^\+?\d{9,15}$/.test(val)),
  });
};

export const validateBranch = (data: Partial<BranchFormData>) => {
  try {
    getValidateBranchSchema().validateSync(data, { abortEarly: false });
    return { isValid: true, errors: {} };
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const errors: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (err instanceof yup.ValidationError) {
      err.inner.forEach((e) => {
        if (e.path) errors[e.path] = e.message;
      });
    }
    return { isValid: false, errors };
  }
};

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\pages\customers\index.tsx =====  
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { fetchCustomers, updateCustomerStatus } from "../../../shared/api/customerApi";
import type { CustomerResponse } from "../../../shared/types/customer";
import "./customers.css";

const TIER_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  standard: "Tiêu chuẩn",
  silver: "Bạc",
  gold: "Vàng",
  platinum: "Bạch kim",
};

const initials = (name: string) =>
  name.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(-2).map((w) => w[0]?.toUpperCase()).join("");

export default function CustomersPage() {
  const [customers, setCustomers] = useState<CustomerResponse[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [tier, setTier] = useState("all");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("all");
  const [busyId, setBusyId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchCustomers()
      .then(setCustomers)
      .catch((e: Error) => setError(e.message))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return customers.filter((c) => {
      const matchQuery =
        !q ||
        c.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        c.email.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        c.phone.includes(q) ||
        c.id.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      return matchQuery && (tier === "all" || c.membershipTier === tier) && (status === "all" || c.status === status);
    });
  }, [customers, query, tier, status]);

  const toggleStatus = async (c: CustomerResponse) => {
    const next = c.status === "active" ? "inactive" : "active";
    setBusyId(c.id);
    setError("");
    try {
      const updated = await updateCustomerStatus(c.id, next);
      setCustomers((list) => list.map((x) => (x.id === c.id ? updated : x)));
    } catch (e) {
      setError((e as Error).message);
    } finally {
      setBusyId(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="cm-wrap">
      <header className="cm-head">
        <div>
          <h1>Quản Lý Khách Hàng Thành Viên ({customers.length})</h1>
          <p>Danh sách khách hàng đã đăng ký thành viên. Bạn có thể tìm kiếm, lọc theo hạng và khóa hoặc mở khóa tài khoản.</p>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="cm-filters">
        <input
          className="cm-search"
          placeholder="Tìm theo tên, email, sđt, mã khách hàng..."
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          aria-label="Tìm khách hàng"
        />
        <label>
          Hạng:
          <select value={tier} onChange={(e) => setTier(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="all">Tất cả hạng</option>
            {Object.entries(TIER_LABEL).map(([k, v]) => (
              <option key={k} value={k}>{v}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
        <label>
          Trạng thái:
          <select value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="all">Tất cả</option>
            <option value="active">Đang hoạt động</option>
            <option value="inactive">Đã khóa</option>
          </select>
        </label>
      </div>

      {error && <div className="cm-alert" role="alert">{error}</div>}

      <div className="cm-table">
        <div className="cm-tr cm-th">
          <span>Khách hàng</span>
          <span>Hạng & điểm</span>
          <span>Liên hệ</span>
          <span>Ngày đăng ký</span>
          <span>Trạng thái</span>
          <span className="cm-right">Thao tác</span>
        </div>

        {loading && <p className="cm-empty">Đang tải danh sách...</p>}
        {!loading && filtered.length === 0 && (
          <p className="cm-empty">
            {customers.length === 0 ? "Chưa có khách hàng nào đăng ký." : "Không có khách hàng phù hợp với bộ lọc."}
          </p>
        )}

        {filtered.map((c) => (
          <div className="cm-tr" key={c.id}>
            <span className="cm-person">
              <span className="cm-avatar" aria-hidden="true">{initials(c.fullName)}</span>
              <span>
                <strong>{c.fullName}</strong>
                <small>ID: {c.id}</small>
              </span>
            </span>
            <span>
              <em className={`cm-tier cm-tier-${c.membershipTier}`}>{TIER_LABEL[c.membershipTier] ?? c.membershipTier}</em>
              <small className="cm-block">{c.loyaltyPoints} điểm</small>
            </span>
            <span className="cm-contact">
              {c.email}
              <small className="cm-block">{c.phone}</small>
            </span>
            <span>{new Date(c.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("vi-VN")}</span>
            <span>
              <em className={c.status === "active" ? "cm-badge cm-on" : "cm-badge cm-off"}>
                {c.status === "active" ? "Đang hoạt động" : "Đã khóa"}
              </em>
            </span>
            <span className="cm-right">
              <button className="cm-btn" disabled={busyId === c.id} onClick={() => toggleStatus(c)}>
                {busyId === c.id ? "Đang xử lý..." : c.status === "active" ? "Tạm Khóa" : "Mở Khóa"}
              </button>
            </span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import * as yup from 'yup';

export const getValidateDashboardFilterSchema = () => {
  return yup.object().shape({
    period: yup.string().trim().required('Kỳ báo cáo không được để trống'),
    branchId: yup.string().trim().nullable(),
  });
};

export const validateDashboardPeriod = (period: string) => {
  return period.length > 0;
};

};

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\pages\menu\menuApi.ts =====  
import { apiClient, transformAxiosErrorResponse } from '../../../shared/api/client';
import type { MenuItem, MenuItemResponse, MenuStatus } from '../../../shared/types/admin';

export const menuApi = {
  getMenuItems: async (): Promise<MenuItem[]> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<MenuItem[] | MenuItemResponse>('/menu-items');
      if (Array.isArray(res.data)) return res.data;
      return res.data.menu_items || [];
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },

  getMenuItem: async (id: string): Promise<MenuItem> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<MenuItem | MenuItemResponse>(`/menu-items/${id}`);
      if ('menu_item' in res.data && res.data.menu_item) return res.data.menu_item;
      return res.data as MenuItem;
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },

  createMenuItem: async (request: Partial<MenuItem>): Promise<MenuItem> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.post<MenuItem | MenuItemResponse>('/menu-items', {
        menu_item: request,
      });
      if ('menu_item' in res.data && res.data.menu_item) return res.data.menu_item;
      return res.data as MenuItem;
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },

  updateMenuItemStatus: async (id: string, status: MenuStatus): Promise<MenuItem> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.patch<MenuItem | MenuItemResponse>(`/menu-items/${id}/status`, null, { params: { status } });
      if ('menu_item' in res.data && res.data.menu_item) return res.data.menu_item;
      return res.data as MenuItem;
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },
};
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\pages\menu\types.ts =====  
export type MenuCategory = 'Phở & Bún' | 'Cơm & Món Chính' | 'Món Ăn Kèm' | 'Đồ Uống' | 'Tráng Miệng';
export type MenuStatus = 'available' | 'out_of_stock' | 'discontinued' | 'AVAILABLE' | 'OUT_OF_STOCK' | 'DISCONTINUED';

export interface MenuItemFormData {
  code: string;
  name: string;
  category: MenuCategory;
  basePrice: number;
  description: string;
  isBestSeller: boolean;
  image: string;
}
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\pages\menu\validate.ts =====  
import * as yup from 'yup';
import type { MenuItemFormData } from './types';

export const getValidateMenuItemSchema = () => {
  return yup.object().shape({
    code: yup
      .string()
      .trim()
      .nullable()
      .required('Mã món không được để trống')
      .max(50, 'Mã món không được vượt quá 50 ký tự'),
    name: yup
      .string()
      .trim()
      .nullable()
      .required('Tên món không được để trống')
      .max(255, 'Tên món không được vượt quá 255 ký tự'),
    basePrice: yup
      .number()
      .required('Giá bán không được để trống')
      .min(1000, 'Giá bán phải lớn hơn 1,000 đ'),
    description: yup
      .string()
      .trim()
      .nullable()
      .max(500, 'Mô tả không được vượt quá 500 ký tự'),
  });
};

export const validateMenuItem = (data: Partial<MenuItemFormData>) => {
  try {
    getValidateMenuItemSchema().validateSync(data, { abortEarly: false });
    return { isValid: true, errors: {} };
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const errors: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (err instanceof yup.ValidationError) {
      err.inner.forEach((e) => {
        if (e.path) errors[e.path] = e.message;
      });
    }
    return { isValid: false, errors };
  }
};

    category: 'Phở & Bún' as MenuCategory,
    basePrice: 79000,
    description: '',
    isBestSeller: false,
    image: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1546069901-ba9599a7e63c?w=500&auto=format&fit=crop',

      item.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      item.code.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());
    const matchesCategory = activeCategory === 'ALL' || item.category === activeCategory;
    return matchesSearch && matchesCategory;
  });

  const handleSubmitNewDish = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!formData.name || !formData.code) return;

    addMenuItem({
      code: formData.code,
      name: formData.name,
      category: formData.category,
      basePrice: Number(formData.basePrice),
      description: formData.description,

      isBestSeller: formData.isBestSeller,
      image: formData.image || 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1546069901-ba9599a7e63c?w=500&auto=format&fit=crop',
    });

    setIsModalOpen(false);
    setFormData({
      code: '',
      name: '',
      category: 'Phở & Bún',
      basePrice: 79000,
      description: '',
      isBestSeller: false,
      image: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1546069901-ba9599a7e63c?w=500&auto=format&fit=crop',
    });

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\pages\order\orderApi.ts =====  
import { apiClient, transformAxiosErrorResponse, toQueryString } from '../../../shared/api/client';
import type { Order, OrderResponse, OrderStatus } from '../../../shared/types/admin';

export const orderApi = {
  getOrders: async (branchId?: string, status?: OrderStatus): Promise<Order[]> => {
    try {
      const q = toQueryString({ branchId, status });
      const res = await apiClient.get<Order[] | OrderResponse>(`/orders${q}`);
      if (Array.isArray(res.data)) return res.data;
      return res.data.orders || [];
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },

  getOrder: async (id: string): Promise<Order> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<Order | OrderResponse>(`/orders/${id}`);
      if ('order' in res.data && res.data.order) return res.data.order;
      return res.data as Order;
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },

  createOrder: async (request: Partial<Order>): Promise<Order> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.post<Order | OrderResponse>('/orders', {
        order: request,
      });
      if ('order' in res.data && res.data.order) return res.data.order;
      return res.data as Order;
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },

  updateOrderStatus: async (id: string, status: OrderStatus): Promise<Order> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.patch<Order | OrderResponse>(`/orders/${id}/status`, null, { params: { status } });
      if ('order' in res.data && res.data.order) return res.data.order;
      return res.data as Order;
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },
};

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\pages\order\validate.ts =====  
import * as yup from 'yup';

export const getValidateOrderCreateSchema = () => {
  return yup.object().shape({
    branchId: yup
      .string()
      .trim()
      .nullable()
      .required('Chi nhánh không được để trống'),
    items: yup
      .array()
      .min(1, 'Đơn hàng phải có ít nhất 1 món ăn')
      .of(
        yup.object().shape({
          id: yup.string().required(),
          quantity: yup.number().min(1, 'Số lượng phải lớn hơn 0'),
        })
      ),
    customerName: yup
      .string()
      .trim()
      .nullable()
      .max(100, 'Tên khách hàng không được vượt quá 100 ký tự'),
  });
};

export const validateOrderSearch = (query: string) => {
  return query.trim().length >= 0;
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ShoppingBag, Search, CreditCard } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAdminStore } from '../../store/adminStore';
import { StatusBadge } from '../../components/StatusBadge';

export const ChainOrdersManagement: React.FC = () => {
  const { orders, selectedBranchId, updateOrderStatus } = useAdminStore();
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [statusTab, setStatusTab] = useState<string>('ALL');

  const filteredOrders = orders.filter((ord) => {
    const matchesBranch = selectedBranchId === 'ALL' || ord.branchId === selectedBranchId;
    const matchesStatus = statusTab === 'ALL' || ord.status === statusTab;
    const matchesSearch =
      ord.orderNumber.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      ord.branchName.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      (ord.customerName && ord.customerName.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()));

    return matchesBranch && matchesStatus && matchesSearch;
  });

  const getStatusCount = (status: string) => {
    if (status === 'ALL') return orders.length;
    return orders.filter((o) => o.status === status).length;
  };

  return (
    <div className="animate-fade-in" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '24px' }}>
      {/* Header */}
      <div>
        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '1.5rem', fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
          Quản Lý Đơn Hàng Live Toàn Chuỗi ({filteredOrders.length})
        </h2>
        <p style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>
          Theo dõi luồng chế biến, giao hàng và thanh toán trực tiếp từ các điểm bán
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Tabs & Filters */}
      <div className="card" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: '16px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          <div className="search-bar" style={{ width: '320px' }}>
            <Search size={16} color="var(--text-muted)" />
            <input
              type="text"
              className="search-input"
              placeholder="Tìm mã đơn (ORD-HN-8841), tên khách..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Status Tabs */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', overflowX: 'auto', paddingBottom: '4px' }}>
          {[
            { id: 'ALL', label: 'Tất Cả Đơn' },
            { id: 'PENDING', label: 'Mới Đặt (Cần Duyệt)' },
            { id: 'PREPARING', label: 'Đang Nấu Bếp' },
            { id: 'READY', label: 'Sẵn Sàng Giao' },
            { id: 'COMPLETED', label: 'Hoàn Tất' },
          ].map((tab) => {
            const count = getStatusCount(tab.id);
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                className={`btn btn-sm ${statusTab === tab.id ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-secondary'}`}
                onClick={() => setStatusTab(tab.id)}
              >
                {tab.label} ({count})
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Orders List View */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
        {filteredOrders.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="card" style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '40px', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>
            Không tìm thấy đơn hàng nào phù hợp với bộ lọc hiện tại.
          </div>
        ) : (
          filteredOrders.map((ord) => (
            <div
              key={ord.id}
              className="card"
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column',
                gap: '14px',
                borderLeft: `4px solid ${
                  ord.status === 'PENDING'
                    ? 'var(--info)'
                    : ord.status === 'PREPARING'
                    ? 'var(--warning)'
                    : ord.status === 'READY'
                    ? 'var(--purple)'
                    : 'var(--success)'
                }`,
              }}
            >
              {/* Top Bar of Order */}
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '12px' }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' }}>
                  <div
                    style={{
                      width: '38px',
                      height: '38px',
                      borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
                      backgroundColor: 'var(--primary-subtle)',
                      color: 'var(--primary)',
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      justifyContent: 'center',
                      fontWeight: 800,
                    }}
                  >
                    <ShoppingBag size={20} />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                      <strong style={{ fontSize: '1rem', color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
                        {ord.orderNumber}
                      </strong>
                      <span
                        style={{
                          fontSize: '0.725rem',
                          fontWeight: 700,
                          padding: '2px 8px',
                          borderRadius: 'var(--radius-full)',
                          backgroundColor:
                            ord.orderType === 'DINE_IN'
                              ? 'var(--info-bg)'
                              : ord.orderType === 'DELIVERY'
                              ? 'var(--purple-bg)'
                              : 'var(--warning-bg)',
                          color:
                            ord.orderType === 'DINE_IN'
                              ? 'var(--info)'
                              : ord.orderType === 'DELIVERY'
                              ? 'var(--purple)'
                              : 'var(--warning)',
                        }}
                      >
                        {ord.orderType === 'DINE_IN'
                          ? `Ăn Tại Bàn (${ord.tableNo || 'Chưa xếp'})`
                          : ord.orderType === 'DELIVERY'
                          ? 'Giao Hàng (Online)'
                          : 'Mang Về (Takeaway)'}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ fontSize: '0.8125rem', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginTop: '2px' }}>
                      {ord.branchName} • Tạo lúc: <strong>{ord.createdAt}</strong> {ord.customerName ? `• Khách: ${ord.customerName}` : ''}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '16px' }}>
                  <div style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '0.75rem', color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontWeight: 600 }}>TỔNG TIỀN</div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '1.15rem', fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--primary)' }}>
                      {ord.totalAmount.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} đ
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <StatusBadge status={ord.status} />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Items Breakdown Table */}
              <div
                style={{
                  backgroundColor: 'var(--bg-app)',
                  borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
                  padding: '12px 16px',
                  display: 'flex',
                  flexDirection: 'column',
                  gap: '8px',
                }}
              >
                {ord.items.map((item) => (
                  <div
                    key={item.id}
                    style={{
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      justifyContent: 'space-between',
                      fontSize: '0.84rem',
                    }}
                  >
                    <div>
                      <strong style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
                        {item.quantity}x {item.name}
                      </strong>
                      {item.notes && (
                        <span style={{ marginLeft: '8px', color: 'var(--danger)', fontSize: '0.78125rem', fontStyle: 'italic' }}>
                          ({item.notes})
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>
                      {(item.price * item.quantity).toLocaleString('vi-VN')} đ
                    </span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              {/* Order Footer & Action Controls */}
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', paddingTop: '6px' }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px', fontSize: '0.8125rem', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>
                  <CreditCard size={15} />
                  <span>Thanh toán: <strong>{ord.paymentMethod}</strong></span>
                </div>

                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
                  {ord.status === 'PENDING' && (
                    <button
                      className="btn btn-primary btn-sm"
                      onClick={() => updateOrderStatus(ord.id, 'PREPARING')}
                    >
                      Duyệt & Gửi Bếp Nấu
                    </button>
                  )}
                  {ord.status === 'PREPARING' && (
                    <button
                      className="btn btn-primary btn-sm"
                      style={{ backgroundColor: 'var(--purple)', borderColor: 'var(--purple)' }}
                      onClick={() => updateOrderStatus(ord.id, 'READY')}
                    >
                      Báo Đã Xong Món
                    </button>
                  )}
                  {ord.status === 'READY' && (
                    <button
                      className="btn btn-primary btn-sm"
                      style={{ backgroundColor: 'var(--success)', borderColor: 'var(--success)' }}
                      onClick={() => updateOrderStatus(ord.id, 'COMPLETED')}
                    >
                      Xác Nhận Hoàn Tất
                    </button>
                  )}
                  {ord.status !== 'COMPLETED' && ord.status !== 'CANCELLED' && (
                    <button
                      className="btn btn-danger btn-sm"
                      onClick={() => updateOrderStatus(ord.id, 'CANCELLED')}
                    >
                      Hủy Đơn
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\pages\order\hooks\useOrder.ts =====  
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useAdminStore } from '../../../store/adminStore';

export const useOrder = () => {
  const { orders, selectedBranchId, updateOrderStatus } = useAdminStore();
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [statusTab, setStatusTab] = useState<string>('ALL');

  const filteredOrders = orders.filter((ord) => {
    const matchesBranch = selectedBranchId === 'ALL' || ord.branchId === selectedBranchId;
    const matchesStatus = statusTab === 'ALL' || ord.status === statusTab;
    const matchesSearch =
      ord.orderNumber.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      ord.branchName.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      (ord.customerName && ord.customerName.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()));

    return matchesBranch && matchesStatus && matchesSearch;
  });

  return { orders: filteredOrders, searchQuery, setSearchQuery, statusTab, setStatusTab, updateOrderStatus };
};

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\pages\table\tableApi.ts =====  
import { apiClient, transformAxiosErrorResponse, toQueryString } from '../../../shared/api/client';
import type { TableInfo, TableResponse, TableStatus } from '../../../shared/types/admin';

export const tableApi = {
  getTables: async (branchId: string, status?: TableStatus): Promise<TableInfo[]> => {
    try {
      const q = toQueryString({ branchId, status });
      const res = await apiClient.get<TableInfo[] | TableResponse>(`/tables${q}`);
      if (Array.isArray(res.data)) return res.data;
      return res.data.tables || [];
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },

  updateTableStatus: async (id: string, status: TableStatus): Promise<TableInfo> => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.patch<TableInfo | TableResponse>(`/tables/${id}/status`, null, { params: { status } });
      if ('table' in res.data && res.data.table) return res.data.table;
      return res.data as TableInfo;
    } catch (error) {
      throw transformAxiosErrorResponse(error);
    }
  },
};

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\pages\table\validate.ts =====  
import * as yup from 'yup';

export const getValidateTableSchema = () => {
  return yup.object().shape({
    tableNo: yup
      .string()
      .trim()
      .nullable()
      .required('Số bàn không được để trống')
      .max(20, 'Số bàn không được vượt quá 20 ký tự'),
    capacity: yup
      .number()
      .required('Sức chứa bàn không được để trống')
      .min(1, 'Sức chứa tối thiểu 1 người'),
  });
};

export const validateTableNumber = (tableNo: string) => {
  return tableNo.trim().length > 0;
};

export const TableManagement: React.FC = () => {
  const { tables, branches, selectedBranchId } = useAdminStore();
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState<string>('ALL');

  const filteredTables = tables.filter((t) => {
    return filterStatus === 'ALL' || t.status === filterStatus;
  });

  const availableCount = tables.filter((t) => t.status === 'AVAILABLE').length;
  const occupiedCount = tables.filter((t) => t.status === 'OCCUPIED').length;
  const reservedCount = tables.filter((t) => t.status === 'RESERVED').length;
  const cleaningCount = tables.filter((t) => t.status === 'CLEANING').length;

          </p>

            <Search size={16} color="var(--text-muted)" />
            <input type="text" className="search-input" placeholder="Tìm kiếm bàn, vị trí..." />
          </div>

            <Plus size={16} />
            <span>Tạo Đơn Hàng Mới</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Summary KPI Badges & Controls */}
      <div className="card" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '16px' }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '12px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          <button

            Tất Cả ({tables.length} Bàn)

            onClick={() => setFilterStatus('RESERVED')}
          >

          </button>
          <button
            className={`btn btn-sm ${filterStatus === 'CLEANING' ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-secondary'}`}

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\admin\store\adminStore.ts =====  
import { create } from 'zustand';
import type { Branch, MenuItem, Order, StaffUser, TableInfo } from '../../shared/types/admin';

interface AdminState {
  // Global Filters
  selectedBranchId: string; // 'ALL' or specific branchId
  setSelectedBranchId: (id: string) => void;

  // Data Collections
  branches: Branch[];
  menuItems: MenuItem[];
  orders: Order[];
  staffList: StaffUser[];
  tables: TableInfo[];

  // Bulk Setters
  setBranches: (branches: Branch[]) => void;
  setMenuItems: (items: MenuItem[]) => void;
  setOrders: (orders: Order[]) => void;
  setStaffList: (staff: StaffUser[]) => void;
  setTables: (tables: TableInfo[]) => void;

  // Actions - Branch
  addBranch: (branch: Omit<Branch, 'id' | 'dailyRevenue' | 'activeOrdersCount'>) => void;
  updateBranchStatus: (id: string, status: Branch['status']) => void;

  // Actions - Menu
  addMenuItem: (item: Omit<MenuItem, 'id' | 'branchesCount'>) => void;
  toggleMenuItemStatus: (id: string, status: MenuItem['status']) => void;

  // Actions - Orders
  updateOrderStatus: (id: string, status: Order['status']) => void;

  // Actions - Staff
  addStaff: (staff: Omit<StaffUser, 'id' | 'createdAt'>) => void;
  toggleStaffStatus: (id: string, status: StaffUser['status']) => void;
}

const INITIAL_BRANCHES: Branch[] = [
  {
    id: 'br-01',
    code: 'HN-HK',
    name: 'OmniResto - Hoàn Kiếm',
    address: '42 Tràng Tiền, Q. Hoàn Kiếm, Hà Nội',

const INITIAL_TABLES: TableInfo[] = Array.from({ length: 12 }).map((_, i) => ({
  id: `tbl-${i + 1}`,
  tableNo: `Bàn ${i + 1 < 10 ? '0' + (i + 1) : i + 1}`,
  branchId: 'br-01',
  capacity: (i % 3 === 0) ? 6 : (i % 2 === 0 ? 4 : 2),
  status: i === 1 || i === 4 || i === 7 ? 'OCCUPIED' : (i === 2 ? 'RESERVED' : (i === 9 ? 'CLEANING' : 'AVAILABLE')),
  currentOrderAmount: (i === 1 || i === 4 || i === 7) ? (i * 95000 + 120000) : undefined,
  occupiedSince: (i === 1 || i === 4 || i === 7) ? `${15 + i * 5} phút trước` : undefined,
}));

export const useAdminStore = create<AdminState>((set) => ({
  selectedBranchId: 'ALL',
  setSelectedBranchId: (id) => set({ selectedBranchId: id }),

  branches: INITIAL_BRANCHES,
  menuItems: INITIAL_MENU,
  orders: INITIAL_ORDERS,
  staffList: INITIAL_STAFF,
  tables: INITIAL_TABLES,

  setBranches: (branches) => set({ branches }),
  setMenuItems: (menuItems) => set({ menuItems }),
  setOrders: (orders) => set({ orders }),
  setStaffList: (staffList) => set({ staffList }),
  setTables: (tables) => set({ tables }),

  addBranch: (newBranchData) =>
    set((state) => ({
      branches: [
        ...state.branches,
        {
          ...newBranchData,
          id: `br-${Date.now()}`,
          dailyRevenue: 0,
          activeOrdersCount: 0,
        },
      ],
    })),

  updateBranchStatus: (id, status) =>
    set((state) => ({
      branches: state.branches.map((b) => (b.id === id ? { ...b, status } : b)),
    })),

  addMenuItem: (newItemData) =>
    set((state) => ({
      menuItems: [
        ...state.menuItems,
        {
          ...newItemData,
          id: `m-${Date.now()}`,
          branchesCount: state.branches.length,
        },
      ],
    })),

  toggleMenuItemStatus: (id, status) =>
    set((state) => ({
      menuItems: state.menuItems.map((item) => (item.id === id ? { ...item, status } : item)),
    })),

  updateOrderStatus: (id, status) =>
    set((state) => ({
      orders: state.orders.map((o) => (o.id === id ? { ...o, status } : o)),
    })),

  addStaff: (newStaffData) =>
    set((state) => ({
      staffList: [
        ...state.staffList,
        {
          ...newStaffData,
          id: `usr-${Date.now()}`,
          createdAt: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
        },
      ],
    })),

  toggleStaffStatus: (id, status) =>
    set((state) => ({
      staffList: state.staffList.map((usr) => (usr.id === id ? { ...usr, status } : usr)),
    })),
}));
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\client\components\CartDrawer.tsx =====  
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, Minus, Plus, Trash2, ShoppingBag, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useClientStore, selectTotalItems, selectTotalAmount } from '../store/clientStore';

interface CartDrawerProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

/**
 * CartDrawer — Slide-in cart panel from right
 *
 * UX Principles:
 * - Figure-Ground: Backdrop blur + dim separates drawer from content
 * - Fitts's Law: +/- buttons 36px, CTA 52px full-width
 * - Anchoring: Total always visible in sticky footer
 * - Progressive Disclosure: Drawer → Cart page → Checkout
 * - Gestalt Proximity: Item info grouped, price right-aligned
 */
export const CartDrawer: React.FC<CartDrawerProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const navigate    = useNavigate();
  const { cart, updateQuantity, removeFromCart, selectedBranchName } = useClientStore();
  const totalItems  = useClientStore(selectTotalItems);
  const totalAmount = useClientStore(selectTotalAmount);

  // Lock body scroll when drawer is open
  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.style.overflow = isOpen ? 'hidden' : '';
    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = ''; };
  }, [isOpen]);

  // Close on Escape key
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose(); };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKey);
  }, [onClose]);

  const handleCheckout = () => {
    onClose();
    navigate('/client/cart');
  };

  return (
    <>
      {/* ── Backdrop — Figure-Ground principle ── */}
      <div
        className="cart-backdrop"
        style={{
          position: 'fixed', inset: 0, zIndex: 300,
          background: 'rgba(24, 16, 10, 0.45)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
          opacity: isOpen ? 1 : 0,
          pointerEvents: isOpen ? 'auto' : 'none',
          transition: 'opacity 0.3s ease',
        }}
        onClick={onClose}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />

      {/* ── Drawer Panel ── */}
      <aside
        role="dialog"
        aria-label="Giỏ hàng của bạn"
        aria-modal="true"
        style={{
          position: 'fixed',
          top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0,
          width: 'min(420px, 100vw)',
          zIndex: 301,
          background: 'var(--c-surface)',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          boxShadow: '-8px 0 40px rgba(100, 50, 20, 0.18)',
          transform: isOpen ? 'translateX(0)' : 'translateX(100%)',
          transition: 'transform 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)',
          fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)',
        }}
      >
        {/* ── Drawer Header ── */}
        <div style={{
          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between',
          padding: '18px 20px', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--c-border-light)',
          flexShrink: 0,
        }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
            <ShoppingBag size={20} color="var(--c-primary)" strokeWidth={2.2} />
            <div>
              <strong style={{ fontSize: '1rem', color: 'var(--c-text-main)' }}>
                Giỏ hàng
              </strong>
              {totalItems > 0 && (
                <span style={{
                  marginLeft: 8, fontSize: '0.75rem', fontWeight: 700,
                  background: 'var(--c-primary-light)', color: 'var(--c-primary)',
                  padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-full)',
                }}>
                  {totalItems} món
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Đóng giỏ hàng"
            style={{
              width: 36, height: 36, borderRadius: '50%', border: '1px solid var(--c-border)',
              background: 'var(--c-surface-warm)', cursor: 'pointer', display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', transition: 'all var(--c-t-fast)',
              color: 'var(--c-text-muted)',
            }}
            onMouseEnter={e => {
              (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = 'var(--c-error-bg)';
              (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.color = 'var(--c-error)';
              (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.borderColor = 'var(--c-error)';
            }}
            onMouseLeave={e => {
              (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = 'var(--c-surface-warm)';
              (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.color = 'var(--c-text-muted)';
              (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.borderColor = 'var(--c-border)';
            }}
          >
            <X size={16} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Branch context */}
        {selectedBranchName && (
          <div style={{
            padding: '10px 20px', background: 'var(--c-surface-warm)',
            borderBottom: '1px solid var(--c-border-light)',
            fontSize: '0.8125rem', color: 'var(--c-text-muted)',
          }}>
            📍 Chi nhánh: <strong style={{ color: 'var(--c-text-body)' }}>{selectedBranchName}</strong>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ── Items List ── */}
        <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: 'auto', padding: '12px 0' }}>
          {cart.length === 0 ? (
            /* Empty state */
            <div style={{
              display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'center', height: '100%', gap: 16,
              padding: '40px 24px', textAlign: 'center',
            }}>
              <div style={{
                width: 80, height: 80, borderRadius: '50%',
                background: 'var(--c-surface-warm)', border: '2px dashed var(--c-border)',
                display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
              }}>
                <ShoppingBag size={32} color="var(--c-text-light)" strokeWidth={1.5} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <strong style={{ color: 'var(--c-text-main)', display: 'block', marginBottom: 6 }}>
                  Giỏ hàng trống
                </strong>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', color: 'var(--c-text-muted)' }}>
                  Thêm món ăn yêu thích vào giỏ nhé!
                </span>
              </div>
              <button
                className="c-btn c-btn-outline c-btn-sm"
                onClick={() => { onClose(); navigate('/client/menu'); }}
              >
                Xem thực đơn
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            cart.map((item) => (
              <div
                key={item.menuItemId}
                style={{
                  display: 'flex', gap: 12, padding: '12px 20px',
                  borderBottom: '1px solid var(--c-border-light)',
                  transition: 'background var(--c-t-fast)',
                }}
              >
                {/* Item image */}
                <img
                  src={item.image}
                  alt={item.name}
                  style={{
                    width: 60, height: 60, borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-md)',
                    objectFit: 'cover', flexShrink: 0,
                    border: '1px solid var(--c-border-light)',
                  }}
                />

                {/* Item info — Gestalt Proximity: name+price together */}
                <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
                  <span style={{
                    fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: 700,
                    color: 'var(--c-text-main)', lineHeight: 1.3,
                    display: '-webkit-box', WebkitLineClamp: 2,
                    WebkitBoxOrient: 'vertical', overflow: 'hidden',
                  }}>
                    {item.name}
                  </span>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--c-primary)' }}>
                    {(item.price * item.quantity).toLocaleString('vi-VN')}đ
                  </span>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: '0.75rem', color: 'var(--c-text-muted)' }}>
                    {item.price.toLocaleString('vi-VN')}đ / món
                  </span>
                </div>

                {/* Controls */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-end', gap: 8, flexShrink: 0 }}>
                  {/* Remove */}
                  <button
                    onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.menuItemId)}
                    aria-label={`Xóa ${item.name}`}
                    style={{
                      width: 28, height: 28, borderRadius: 6,
                      border: '1px solid var(--c-border)', background: 'transparent',
                      display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                      cursor: 'pointer', color: 'var(--c-text-muted)',
                      transition: 'all var(--c-t-fast)',
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={e => {
                      (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.color = 'var(--c-error)';
                      (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.borderColor = 'var(--c-error)';
                      (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = 'var(--c-error-bg)';
                    }}
                    onMouseLeave={e => {
                      (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.color = 'var(--c-text-muted)';
                      (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.borderColor = 'var(--c-border)';
                      (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).style.background = 'transparent';
                    }}
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={13} />
                  </button>

                  {/* Quantity stepper — Fitts's Law: min 32px each */}
                  <div style={{
                    display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center',
                    border: '1.5px solid var(--c-primary)', borderRadius: 8, overflow: 'hidden',
                  }}>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.menuItemId, -1)}
                      aria-label="Giảm"
                      style={{
                        width: 30, height: 30, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center', background: 'transparent', border: 'none',
                        cursor: 'pointer', color: 'var(--c-primary)',
                      }}
                    >
                      <Minus size={13} strokeWidth={2.5} />
                    </button>
                    <span style={{
                      width: 28, textAlign: 'center', fontSize: '0.875rem',
                      fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--c-primary)', fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)',
                    }}>
                      {item.quantity}
                    </span>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.menuItemId, +1)}
                      aria-label="Tăng"
                      style={{
                        width: 30, height: 30, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center', background: 'var(--c-primary)', border: 'none',
                        cursor: 'pointer', color: '#fff',
                      }}
                    >
                      <Plus size={13} strokeWidth={2.5} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>

        {/* ── Sticky Footer — Anchoring: total always visible ── */}
        {cart.length > 0 && (
          <div style={{
            padding: '16px 20px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--c-border)',
            background: 'var(--c-surface)', flexShrink: 0,
            boxShadow: '0 -4px 20px rgba(100,50,20,0.08)',
          }}>
            {/* Summary row */}
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: 14 }}>

                Tạm tính ({totalItems} món)
              </span>
              <strong style={{ fontSize: '1.125rem', color: 'var(--c-primary)' }}>
                {totalAmount.toLocaleString('vi-VN')}đ
              </strong>
            </div>

            {/* Checkout CTA — Fitts's Law: full-width, 52px tall */}
            <button
              id="cart-checkout-btn"
              className="c-btn c-btn-primary c-btn-lg c-btn-block"
              onClick={handleCheckout}
            >
              Đặt hàng ngay
              <ArrowRight size={18} strokeWidth={2.2} />
            </button>
            <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: '0.75rem', color: 'var(--c-text-muted)', marginTop: 10 }}>
              Chưa bao gồm phí giao hàng
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </aside>
    </>
  );
};

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\client\components\MenuCard.tsx =====  
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Plus, Minus, Flame } from 'lucide-react';
import type { MenuItem } from '../../shared/types/menu';
import { useClientStore } from '../store/clientStore';

interface MenuCardProps {
  item: MenuItem;
  /** Staggered animation delay (ms) */
  animationDelay?: number;
}

/**
 * MenuCard — Client menu item card
 *
 * UX Principles applied:
 * - Visual Hierarchy: Image → Name → Description → Price/CTA (F-pattern reading)
 * - Fitts's Law: Add button min 44px, stepper buttons equally large
 * - Gestalt Proximity: Price and CTA grouped together at card bottom
 * - Miller's Law: Description clamped to 2 lines (prevent info overload)
 * - Social Proof: "Bán chạy" badge prominently shown
 * - Feedback: Scale + shadow on hover, animation on add
 */
export const MenuCard: React.FC<MenuCardProps> = ({ item, animationDelay = 0 }) => {
  const { cart, addToCart, updateQuantity } = useClientStore();
  const [isAdding, setIsAdding] = useState(false);

  const cartItem = cart.find((c) => c.menuItemId === item.id);
  const qty = cartItem?.quantity ?? 0;
  const isAvailable = item.status === 'AVAILABLE' || item.status === 'available';

  const handleAdd = () => {
    if (!isAvailable) return;
    setIsAdding(true);
    addToCart({
      menuItemId: item.id,
      name: item.name,
      category: item.category,
      price: item.basePrice,
      image: item.image,
    });
    setTimeout(() => setIsAdding(false), 400);
  };

  return (
    <article
      className="menu-card"
      style={{ animationDelay: `${animationDelay}ms` }}
      aria-label={item.name}
    >
      {/* ── Food Image — Visual anchor (top of card, draws eye first) ── */}
      <div className="menu-card__img-wrap">
        <img
          src={item.image}
          alt={item.name}
          className="menu-card__img"
          loading="lazy"
          onError={(e) => {
            (e.target as HTMLImageElement).src =
              'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1546069901-ba9599a7e63c?w=400&auto=format&fit=crop';
          }}
        />

        {/* Out-of-stock overlay — Figure-Ground principle */}
        {!isAvailable && (
          <div className="menu-card__oos-overlay">
            <span>Tạm hết</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Social proof badge — Serial Position: shown on image for max visibility */}
        {item.isBestSeller && (
          <div className="menu-card__badge-bestseller">
            <Flame size={11} strokeWidth={2.5} />
            Bán chạy
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* ── Card Body ── */}
      <div className="menu-card__body">
        {/* Category label — small, muted (de-emphasised per hierarchy) */}
        <span className="menu-card__category">{item.category}</span>

        {/* Name — largest text in body, dark for max contrast */}
        <h3 className="menu-card__name c-clamp-2">{item.name}</h3>

        {/* Description — 2-line clamp (Miller's Law: prevent overload) */}
        <p className="menu-card__desc c-clamp-2">{item.description}</p>

        {/* ── Footer: Price + CTA — Gestalt Proximity: grouped together ── */}
        <div className="menu-card__footer">
          <div className="menu-card__price">
            <span className="menu-card__price-value">
              {item.basePrice.toLocaleString('vi-VN')}
            </span>
            <span className="menu-card__price-unit">đ</span>
          </div>

          {/* CTA — Fitts's Law: min 44px, distinct orange color */}
          {qty === 0 ? (
            <button
              id={`add-to-cart-${item.id}`}
              className={`menu-card__add-btn${isAdding ? ' menu-card__add-btn--adding' : ''}${!isAvailable ? ' menu-card__add-btn--disabled' : ''}`}
              onClick={handleAdd}
              disabled={!isAvailable}
              aria-label={`Thêm ${item.name} vào giỏ hàng`}
            >
              <Plus size={18} strokeWidth={2.5} />
              {isAvailable ? 'Thêm' : 'Hết'}
            </button>
          ) : (
            /* Quantity stepper — shown after first add */
            <div className="menu-card__stepper" role="group" aria-label={`Số lượng ${item.name}`}>
              <button
                className="menu-card__step-btn"
                onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.id, -1)}
                aria-label="Giảm số lượng"
              >
                <Minus size={14} strokeWidth={2.5} />
              </button>
              <span className="menu-card__qty">{qty}</span>
              <button
                className="menu-card__step-btn menu-card__step-btn--plus"
                onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.id, +1)}
                aria-label="Tăng số lượng"
              >
                <Plus size={14} strokeWidth={2.5} />
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <style>{`
        .menu-card {
          background: var(--c-surface);
          border: 1px solid var(--c-border-light);
          border-radius: var(--c-r-lg);
          overflow: hidden;
          display: flex;
          flex-direction: column;
          box-shadow: var(--c-shadow-sm);
          transition: box-shadow var(--c-t-fast), transform var(--c-t-fast);
          animation: c-fade-up 0.4s var(--c-t-normal) both;
          cursor: default;
        }
        .menu-card:hover {
          box-shadow: var(--c-shadow-md);
          transform: translateY(-3px);
        }

        /* Image */
        .menu-card__img-wrap {
          position: relative;
          width: 100%;
          aspect-ratio: 4 / 3;
          overflow: hidden;
          background: var(--c-border-light);
          flex-shrink: 0;
        }
        .menu-card__img {
          width: 100%;
          height: 100%;
          object-fit: cover;
          transition: transform var(--c-t-slow);
        }
        .menu-card:hover .menu-card__img {
          transform: scale(1.05);
        }
        .menu-card__oos-overlay {
          position: absolute;
          inset: 0;
          background: rgba(20, 10, 5, 0.52);
          display: flex;
          align-items: center;
          justify-content: center;
        }
        .menu-card__oos-overlay span {
          background: rgba(255,255,255,0.92);
          color: var(--c-text-main);
          font-size: 0.8125rem;
          font-weight: 700;
          font-family: var(--c-font);
          padding: 6px 16px;
          border-radius: var(--c-r-full);
        }
        .menu-card__badge-bestseller {
          position: absolute;
          top: 10px;
          left: 10px;
          background: var(--c-primary);
          color: #fff;
          font-size: 0.6875rem;
          font-weight: 800;
          font-family: var(--c-font);
          padding: 3px 10px 3px 7px;
          border-radius: var(--c-r-full);
          display: flex;
          align-items: center;
          gap: 4px;
          box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(232,93,38,0.4);
        }

        /* Body */
        .menu-card__body {
          padding: 14px 16px 16px;
          display: flex;
          flex-direction: column;
          gap: 6px;
          flex: 1;
        }
        .menu-card__category {
          font-size: 0.6875rem;
          font-weight: 600;
          color: var(--c-primary);
          text-transform: uppercase;
          letter-spacing: 0.06em;
        }
        .menu-card__name {
          font-size: 0.9375rem;
          font-weight: 700;
          color: var(--c-text-main);
          line-height: 1.35;
          margin: 0;

        .menu-card__desc {
          font-size: 0.8125rem;
