===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\client\components\OrderStatusStepper.tsx =====  
import React from 'react';
import { Check, Clock, ChefHat, Bell } from 'lucide-react';
import type { PlacedOrderStatus } from '../../shared/types/client';

interface Step {
  key: PlacedOrderStatus;
  label: string;
  subLabel: string;
  Icon: React.FC<{ size?: number; strokeWidth?: number }>;
}

const STEPS: Step[] = [
  { key: 'PENDING',    label: 'Đã nhận đơn', subLabel: 'Bếp đã xác nhận', Icon: Clock },
  { key: 'PREPARING',  label: 'Đang nấu',     subLabel: 'Đang chế biến',   Icon: ChefHat },
  { key: 'READY',      label: 'Sẵn sàng',     subLabel: 'Món đã sẵn sàng', Icon: Bell },
  { key: 'COMPLETED',  label: 'Hoàn tất',     subLabel: 'Chúc ngon miệng!',Icon: Check },
];

interface Props {
  status?: PlacedOrderStatus;
  currentStatus?: PlacedOrderStatus;
  estimatedMinutes?: number;
}

export const OrderStatusStepper: React.FC<Props> = ({ status, currentStatus, estimatedMinutes }) => {
  const activeStatus = currentStatus || status || 'PENDING';
  const currentIdx = STEPS.findIndex(s => s.key === activeStatus);
  const progressPercent = currentIdx === -1 ? 0 : (currentIdx / (STEPS.length - 1)) * 100;

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: '24px 0' }}>
      {estimatedMinutes && activeStatus !== 'COMPLETED' && (
        <div style={{
          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8,
          marginBottom: 20, padding: '10px 16px',
          background: 'var(--c-accent-light, #fffbe6)', borderRadius: '8px',
          border: '1px solid #fde68a',
        }}>
          <Clock size={16} color="#b45309" strokeWidth={2} />
          <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: 600, color: '#92400e' }}>
            Thời gian dự kiến: khoảng <strong>{estimatedMinutes} phút</strong>
          </span>
        </div>
      )}
      {/* Progress track top */}
      <div style={{
        height: 4,
        backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0',
        borderRadius: 2,
        marginBottom: 20,
        position: 'relative',
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }}>
        <div style={{
          height: '100%',
          width: `${progressPercent}%`,
          backgroundColor: '#FF6B00',
          borderRadius: 2,
          transition: 'width 0.4s ease',
        }} />
      </div>

      {/* Steps */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: 0 }}>
        {STEPS.map((step, idx) => {
          const isCompleted = idx < currentIdx;
          const isActive    = idx === currentIdx;

          return (
            <React.Fragment key={step.key}>
              {/* Step node */}
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                {/* Circle icon */}
                <div style={{
                  width: 48, height: 48, borderRadius: '50%',
                  display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                  transition: 'all 0.4s ease',
                  flexShrink: 0,
                  ...(isCompleted ? {
                    background: 'var(--c-success)',
                    boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(22,163,74,0.3)',
                  } : isActive ? {
                    background: 'var(--c-primary)',
                    boxShadow: 'var(--c-shadow-orange)',
                    animation: 'c-badge-pop 1.5s ease-in-out infinite',
                  } : {
                    background: 'var(--c-border-light)',
                    border: '2px dashed var(--c-border)',
                  }),
                }}>
                  {isCompleted ? (
                    <Check size={20} color="#fff" strokeWidth={3} />
                  ) : (
                    <step.Icon
                      size={20}
                      strokeWidth={2}
                    />
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Labels — Gestalt Proximity: label directly under icon */}
                <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 8, padding: '0 4px' }}>
                  <div style={{
                    fontSize: '0.75rem', fontWeight: 700, lineHeight: 1.3,
                    color: isCompleted ? 'var(--c-success)' : isActive ? 'var(--c-primary)' : 'var(--c-text-muted)',
                  }}>
                    {step.label}
                  </div>
                  {isActive && (
                    <div style={{
                      fontSize: '0.6875rem', color: 'var(--c-text-muted)',
                      marginTop: 2, lineHeight: 1.3,
                    }}>
                      {step.subLabel}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Connector line — Gestalt Continuity */}
              {idx < STEPS.length - 1 && (
                <div style={{
                  height: 3, flex: 0.5, marginTop: 22, borderRadius: 2,
                  background: idx < currentIdx ? 'var(--c-success)' : 'var(--c-border)',
                  transition: 'background 0.6s ease',
                }} />
              )}
            </React.Fragment>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\client\components\OtpAuthModal.tsx =====  
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { customerApi } from '../../shared/api/customerApi';

interface OtpAuthModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess: (phone: string) => void;
}

export const OtpAuthModal: React.FC<OtpAuthModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, onSuccess }) => {
  const [step, setStep] = useState<'PHONE' | 'OTP'>('PHONE');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleSendOtp = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!phone) {
      setError('Vui lòng nhập số điện thoại');
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      await customerApi.sendOtp(phone);
      setStep('OTP');
    } catch {
      setError('Gửi mã OTP thất bại. Vui lòng thử lại.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleVerifyOtp = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!otp) {
      setError('Vui lòng nhập mã OTP');
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const res = await customerApi.verifyOtp(phone, otp);
      if (res.success) {
        onSuccess(phone);
        onClose();
      } else {
        setError(res.message || 'Mã OTP không đúng');
      }
    } catch {
      setError('Xác thực thất bại. Vui lòng kiểm tra lại.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 flex items-center justify-center z-50 p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl max-w-md w-full p-6 shadow-xl relative">
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 font-bold text-xl"
        >
          ✕
        </button>

        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800 text-center mb-6">
          {step === 'PHONE' ? 'Đăng nhập / Đặt món' : 'Xác thực OTP'}
        </h2>

        {error && (
          <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 text-red-600 rounded-lg text-sm text-center">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 'PHONE' ? (
          <form onSubmit={handleSendOtp} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                Số điện thoại
              </label>
              <input
                type="tel"
                value={phone}
                onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Ví dụ: 0912345678"
                className="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-orange-500 focus:outline-none"
              />
            </div>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full py-3 bg-orange-500 hover:bg-orange-600 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg shadow transition duration-200 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {loading ? 'Đang gửi...' : 'Tiếp tục'}
            </button>
          </form>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleVerifyOtp} className="space-y-4">
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 text-center">
              Mã OTP đã được gửi tới số <span className="font-semibold text-gray-800">{phone}</span>
            </p>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                Mã xác thực OTP
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                maxLength={6}
                value={otp}
                onChange={(e) => setOtp(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Nhập 6 số OTP"
                className="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 text-center tracking-widest text-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-orange-500 focus:outline-none"
              />
            </div>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full py-3 bg-orange-500 hover:bg-orange-600 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg shadow transition duration-200 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {loading ? 'Đang xác thực...' : 'Xác nhận'}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setStep('PHONE')}
              className="w-full text-center text-sm text-gray-500 hover:underline mt-2"
            >
              Đổi số điện thoại khác
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\client\layouts\ClientLayout.tsx =====  
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Outlet, Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ShoppingCart, MapPin, UtensilsCrossed, User, LogOut, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';
import { useClientStore, selectTotalItems, selectTotalAmount } from '../store/clientStore';
import { CartDrawer } from '../components/CartDrawer';
import './ClientLayout.css';

interface ClientUser {
  id?: string;
  fullName?: string;
  name?: string;
  email?: string;
  phone?: string;
  role?: string;
}

export const ClientLayout: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { selectedBranchName, isCartOpen, openCart, closeCart } = useClientStore();
  const totalItems = useClientStore(selectTotalItems);
  const totalAmount = useClientStore(selectTotalAmount);

  // Đọc thông tin phiên đăng nhập từ localStorage
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<ClientUser | null>(() => {
    try {
      const savedUser = localStorage.getItem('user');
      return savedUser ? (JSON.parse(savedUser) as ClientUser) : null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  });

  const [isDropdownOpen, setIsDropdownOpen] = useState(false);
  const dropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Đóng dropdown khi click ra ngoài
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (dropdownRef.current && !dropdownRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setIsDropdownOpen(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  // Xử lý Đăng xuất
  const handleLogout = () => {
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('user');
    setCurrentUser(null);
    setIsDropdownOpen(false);
    navigate('/client');
  };

  const displayName = currentUser?.fullName || currentUser?.name || currentUser?.phone || 'Khách hàng';

  return (
    <div className="client-layout">
      {/* ── Sticky Header ── */}
      <header className="client-header">
        <div className="client-header-inner">
          {/* Cụm Trái: Logo + Chi nhánh */}
          <div className="client-header-left">
            <Link to="/client" className="client-logo">
              <span className="client-logo-icon">
                <UtensilsCrossed size={20} strokeWidth={2.4} />
              </span>
              <span className="client-logo-text">OmniResto</span>
            </Link>

            {selectedBranchName && (
              <div className="client-branch-pill" title={selectedBranchName}>
                <MapPin size={13} />
                <span>{selectedBranchName}</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Cụm Phải: Giỏ hàng + Tài khoản */}
          <div className="client-header-right">
            {/* Nút Giỏ Hàng */}
            <button
              type="button"
              className={`client-cart-pill ${totalItems > 0 ? 'client-cart-pill--active' : ''}`}
              onClick={openCart}
            >
              <ShoppingCart size={18} strokeWidth={2.2} />
              {totalItems > 0 ? (
                <>
                  <span className="client-cart-price">{totalAmount.toLocaleString('vi-VN')}đ</span>
                  <span className="client-cart-badge">{totalItems}</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <span className="client-cart-label">Giỏ hàng</span>
              )}
            </button>

            {/* Tài Khoản / Nút Đăng nhập */}
            {currentUser ? (
              <div className="client-user-wrapper" ref={dropdownRef}>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className={`client-user-trigger ${isDropdownOpen ? 'client-user-trigger--active' : ''}`}
                  onClick={() => setIsDropdownOpen(!isDropdownOpen)}
                >
                  <div className="client-user-avatar">
                    <User size={15} strokeWidth={2.4} />
                  </div>
                  <span className="client-user-label">{displayName}</span>
                  <ChevronDown
                    size={14}
                    className={`client-chevron ${isDropdownOpen ? 'client-chevron--open' : ''}`}
                  />
                </button>

                {/* Dropdown Menu */}
                {isDropdownOpen && (
                  <div className="client-menu-dropdown">
                    <div className="client-menu-header">
                      <p className="client-menu-name">{displayName}</p>
                      <p className="client-menu-sub">
                        {currentUser.email || currentUser.phone || 'Hội viên OmniResto'}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="client-menu-divider" />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="client-menu-logout"
                      onClick={handleLogout}
                    >
                      <LogOut size={15} />
                      <span>Đăng xuất</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <Link to="/login" className="client-auth-link">
                <User size={16} strokeWidth={2.2} />
                <span>Đăng ký / Đăng nhập</span>
              </Link>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* ── Nội Dung Trang ── */}
      <main className="client-main">
        <Outlet />
      </main>

      {/* ── Chân Trang ── */}
      <footer className="client-footer">
        <div className="client-footer-inner">
          <div className="client-footer-brand">
            <UtensilsCrossed size={16} />
            <span>OmniResto</span>
          </div>
          <p>© 2026 OmniResto · Hệ thống F&B Ẩm Thực Việt</p>
        </div>
      </footer>

      {/* Giỏ Hàng Trượt (Cart Drawer) */}
      <CartDrawer isOpen={isCartOpen} onClose={closeCart} />
    </div>
  );
};

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\client\pages\auth\AuthPage.tsx =====  
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { authApi } from '../../../shared/api/authService';
import { OtpModal } from '../../../shared/components/OtpModal';
import './AuthPage.css';

type AuthMode = 'LOGIN' | 'REGISTER' | 'FORGOT_PASSWORD' | 'RESET_PASSWORD';

interface ApiErrorResponse {
  response?: {
    data?: {
      message?: string;
    };
  };
}

export const AuthPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<AuthMode>('LOGIN');

  // Form States
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState('');
  const [verifiedOtpCode, setVerifiedOtpCode] = useState('');

  // UI / Modal States
  const [isOtpModalOpen, setIsOtpModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<{ type: 'success' | 'error'; text: string } | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const resetForm = () => {
    setEmail('');
    setPassword('');
    setFullName('');
    setPhone('');
    setNewPassword('');
    setMessage(null);
  };

  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setMessage(null);
      const res = await authApi.login({ usernameOrEmail: email, password });
      
      // 1. Lưu phiên đăng nhập người dùng vào localStorage
      localStorage.setItem('token', res.data.token);
      localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(res.data));

      setMessage({ type: 'success', text: 'Đăng nhập thành công!' });
      
      // 2. Chuyển hướng thẳng về trang /client (không vào Admin)
      setTimeout(() => {
        window.location.href = '/client';
      }, 700);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const apiErr = err as ApiErrorResponse;
      setMessage({ type: 'error', text: apiErr.response?.data?.message || 'Đăng nhập thất bại' });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRegister = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setMessage(null);
      await authApi.register({ fullName, email, phone, password });
      setIsOtpModalOpen(true);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const apiErr = err as ApiErrorResponse;
      setMessage({ type: 'error', text: apiErr.response?.data?.message || 'Đăng ký thất bại' });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleForgotPassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setMessage(null);
      await authApi.forgotPassword({ email });
      setIsOtpModalOpen(true);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const apiErr = err as ApiErrorResponse;
      setMessage({ type: 'error', text: apiErr.response?.data?.message || 'Không thể gửi yêu cầu' });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleResetPassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setMessage(null);
      await authApi.resetPassword({ email, otpCode: verifiedOtpCode, newPassword });
      setMessage({ type: 'success', text: 'Mật khẩu đã được cập nhật! Vui lòng đăng nhập.' });
      setTimeout(() => {
        resetForm();
        setMode('LOGIN');
      }, 1500);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const apiErr = err as ApiErrorResponse;
      setMessage({ type: 'error', text: apiErr.response?.data?.message || 'Đặt lại mật khẩu thất bại' });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleOtpSuccess = (otpCode: string) => {
    setIsOtpModalOpen(false);
    if (mode === 'REGISTER') {
      setMessage({ type: 'success', text: 'Kích hoạt tài khoản thành công! Vui lòng đăng nhập.' });
      resetForm();
      setMode('LOGIN');
    } else if (mode === 'FORGOT_PASSWORD') {
      setVerifiedOtpCode(otpCode);
      setMode('RESET_PASSWORD');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="auth-container">
      <div className="auth-card">
        <h2 className="auth-title">
          {mode === 'LOGIN' && 'Đăng Nhập OmniResto'}
          {mode === 'REGISTER' && 'Đăng Ký Tài Khoản'}
          {mode === 'FORGOT_PASSWORD' && 'Quên Mật Khẩu'}
          {mode === 'RESET_PASSWORD' && 'Đặt Lại Mật Khẩu Mới'}
        </h2>

        {message && (
          <div className={`auth-alert ${message.type === 'success' ? 'auth-alert-success' : 'auth-alert-error'}`}>
            {message.text}
          </div>
        )}

        {mode === 'LOGIN' && (
          <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="auth-form">
            <div className="form-group">
              <label className="form-label">Email / Số điện thoại</label>
              <input type="text" required value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} className="form-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="form-group">
              <label className="form-label">Mật khẩu</label>
              <input type="password" required value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} className="form-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="text-right">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { resetForm(); setMode('FORGOT_PASSWORD'); }} className="auth-btn-link">
                Quên mật khẩu?
              </button>
            </div>
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="auth-btn-submit">
              {loading ? 'Đang xử lý...' : 'Đăng Nhập'}
            </button>
            <p className="auth-footer-text">
              Chưa có tài khoản?{' '}
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { resetForm(); setMode('REGISTER'); }} className="auth-btn-link">
                Đăng ký ngay
              </button>
            </p>
          </form>
        )}

        {mode === 'REGISTER' && (
          <form onSubmit={handleRegister} className="auth-form">
            <div className="form-group">
              <label className="form-label">Họ và Tên</label>
              <input type="text" required value={fullName} onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)} className="form-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="form-group">
              <label className="form-label">Email</label>
              <input type="email" required value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} className="form-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="form-group">
              <label className="form-label">Số Điện Thoại</label>
              <input type="text" required value={phone} onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)} className="form-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="form-group">
              <label className="form-label">Mật Khẩu</label>
              <input type="password" required minLength={6} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} className="form-input" />
            </div>
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="auth-btn-submit">
              {loading ? 'Đang tạo tài khoản...' : 'Đăng Ký'}
            </button>
            <p className="auth-footer-text">
              Đã có tài khoản?{' '}
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { resetForm(); setMode('LOGIN'); }} className="auth-btn-link">
                Đăng nhập
              </button>
            </p>
          </form>
        )}

        {mode === 'FORGOT_PASSWORD' && (
          <form onSubmit={handleForgotPassword} className="auth-form">
            <div className="form-group">
              <label className="form-label">Nhập Email Đã Đăng Ký</label>
              <input type="email" required value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} className="form-input" />
            </div>
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="auth-btn-submit">
              {loading ? 'Đang gửi...' : 'Gửi Mã Xác Nhận OTP'}
            </button>
            <div className="auth-footer-text">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { resetForm(); setMode('LOGIN'); }} className="auth-btn-link">
                Quay lại đăng nhập
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}

        {mode === 'RESET_PASSWORD' && (
          <form onSubmit={handleResetPassword} className="auth-form">
            <div className="form-group">
              <label className="form-label">Mật Khẩu Mới</label>
              <input type="password" required minLength={6} value={newPassword} onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)} className="form-input" />
            </div>
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="auth-btn-submit">
              {loading ? 'Đang cập nhật...' : 'Cập Nhật Mật Khẩu'}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}

        <OtpModal
          isOpen={isOtpModalOpen}
          email={email}
          purpose={mode === 'REGISTER' ? 'REGISTER_VERIFICATION' : 'PASSWORD_RESET'}
          onClose={() => setIsOtpModalOpen(false)}
          onSuccess={handleOtpSuccess}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\client\pages\cart\ClientCart.tsx =====  
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Check, Utensils, ShoppingBag, Bike, QrCode, Banknote, CreditCard } from 'lucide-react';
import { useClientStore, selectTotalAmount } from '../../store/clientStore';
import type { OrderMode, ClientPaymentMethod } from '../../../shared/types/client';

/**
 * ClientCart — Multi-step checkout
 *
 * UX Principles:
 * - Hick's Law: 3 steps instead of one long form (chunk complexity)
 * - Miller's Law: Each step shows only relevant info
 * - Progressive Disclosure: Step 1 → 2 → 3 (info revealed incrementally)
 * - Visual Hierarchy: Step indicator at top (orientation/navigation aid)
 * - Fitts's Law: Mode selector buttons are large (min 80px tall)
 * - Anchoring: Order summary always visible on right (desktop)
 */
const ClientCart: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const {
    cart, updateQuantity, removeFromCart,
    orderMode, setOrderMode,
    selectedBranchId, selectedBranchName,
    placeOrder,
  } = useClientStore();
  const totalAmount = useClientStore(selectTotalAmount);

  const [step, setStep]                   = useState<1 | 2 | 3>(1);
  const [customerName, setCustomerName]   = useState('');
  const [customerPhone, setCustomerPhone] = useState('');
  const [tableNo, setTableNo]             = useState('');
  const [deliveryAddr, setDeliveryAddr]   = useState('');
  const [notes, setNotes]                 = useState('');
  const [payMethod, setPayMethod]         = useState<ClientPaymentMethod>('QR_CODE');

  const totalItems = cart.reduce((s, i) => s + i.quantity, 0);
  const isEmpty    = cart.length === 0;

  const ORDER_MODES: Array<{ mode: OrderMode; icon: typeof Utensils; label: string; desc: string; emoji: string }> = [
    { mode: 'DINE_IN',  icon: Utensils,  label: 'Tại bàn',  desc: 'Dùng bữa ngay tại nhà hàng', emoji: '🍽️' },
    { mode: 'TAKEAWAY', icon: ShoppingBag, label: 'Mang về', desc: 'Đóng gói mang đi, nhận tại quầy', emoji: '📦' },
    { mode: 'DELIVERY', icon: Bike,      label: 'Giao hàng', desc: 'Giao đến tận địa chỉ của bạn', emoji: '🚴' },
  ];

  const PAY_METHODS: Array<{ key: ClientPaymentMethod; label: string; icon: typeof QrCode }> = [
    { key: 'QR_CODE', label: 'Quét QR', icon: QrCode },
    { key: 'CASH',    label: 'Tiền mặt', icon: Banknote },
    { key: 'CARD',    label: 'Thẻ ngân hàng', icon: CreditCard },
  ];

  const handlePlaceOrder = () => {
    const placed = placeOrder({
      branchId:        selectedBranchId || 'br-01',
      branchName:      selectedBranchName || 'OmniResto',
      orderMode,
      tableNo:         orderMode === 'DINE_IN' ? tableNo : undefined,
      customerName:    customerName || undefined,
      customerPhone:   customerPhone || undefined,
      deliveryAddress: orderMode === 'DELIVERY' ? deliveryAddr : undefined,
      notes:           notes || undefined,
      paymentMethod:   payMethod,
    });
    navigate(`/client/order/${placed.id}`);
  };

  const canGoStep2 = cart.length > 0;
  const canGoStep3 = orderMode !== undefined;
  const canConfirm = orderMode !== 'DINE_IN' || tableNo.trim() !== '';

  /* Step progress indicator */
  const StepIndicator = () => (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 0, marginBottom: 32 }}>
      {(['Giỏ hàng', 'Hình thức', 'Thông tin'] as const).map((label, i) => {
        const stepNum = (i + 1) as 1 | 2 | 3;
        const done    = step > stepNum;
        const active  = step === stepNum;
        return (
          <React.Fragment key={label}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
              <div style={{
                width: 36, height: 36, borderRadius: '50%', display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                fontWeight: 700, fontSize: '0.875rem', fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)',
                transition: 'all 0.3s ease',
                ...(done ? {
                  background: 'var(--c-success)', color: '#fff',
                } : active ? {
                  background: 'var(--c-primary)', color: '#fff',
                  boxShadow: 'var(--c-shadow-orange)',
                } : {
                  background: 'var(--c-border)', color: 'var(--c-text-muted)',
                }),
              }}>
                {done ? <Check size={16} strokeWidth={3} /> : stepNum}
              </div>
              <span style={{
                fontSize: '0.75rem', fontWeight: active ? 700 : 500,
                color: active ? 'var(--c-primary)' : done ? 'var(--c-success)' : 'var(--c-text-muted)',
                whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
              }}>
                {label}
              </span>
            </div>
            {i < 2 && (
              <div style={{
                flex: 1, height: 2, margin: '0 8px', marginBottom: 22,
                background: done ? 'var(--c-success)' : 'var(--c-border)',
                transition: 'background 0.4s ease',
              }} />
            )}
          </React.Fragment>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );

  /* Order summary sidebar (always visible — Anchoring) */
  const OrderSummary = () => (
    <div style={{
      background: 'var(--c-surface)', border: '1px solid var(--c-border)',
      borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-lg)', padding: '20px', boxShadow: 'var(--c-shadow-sm)',
      position: 'sticky', top: 'calc(var(--c-header-h) + 20px)',
    }}>
      <h3 style={{ fontSize: '0.9375rem', fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--c-text-main)', margin: '0 0 16px' }}>
        Tóm tắt đơn hàng
      </h3>
      {cart.map((item) => (
        <div key={item.menuItemId} style={{
          display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start',
          gap: 8, marginBottom: 10,
        }}>
          <span style={{ fontSize: '0.8125rem', color: 'var(--c-text-body)', lineHeight: 1.4, flex: 1 }}>
            {item.name} <span style={{ color: 'var(--c-text-muted)' }}>×{item.quantity}</span>
          </span>
          <span style={{ fontSize: '0.8125rem', fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--c-text-main)', flexShrink: 0 }}>
            {(item.price * item.quantity).toLocaleString('vi-VN')}đ
          </span>
        </div>
      ))}
      <div style={{ height: 1, background: 'var(--c-border-light)', margin: '12px 0' }} />
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--c-text-body)' }}>
          Tổng cộng
        </span>
        <span style={{ fontSize: '1.125rem', fontWeight: 900, color: 'var(--c-primary)' }}>
          {totalAmount.toLocaleString('vi-VN')}đ
        </span>
      </div>
      <p style={{ fontSize: '0.75rem', color: 'var(--c-text-muted)', margin: '8px 0 0', textAlign: 'center' }}>
        * Chưa bao gồm phí dịch vụ và phí giao hàng
      </p>
    </div>
  );

  if (isEmpty) {
    return (
      <div style={{
        fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)', minHeight: '70vh',
        display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center', gap: 20, padding: 40, textAlign: 'center',
      }}>
        <span style={{ fontSize: '4rem' }}>🛒</span>
        <h2 style={{ color: 'var(--c-text-main)', margin: 0, fontSize: '1.25rem' }}>Giỏ hàng trống</h2>
        <p style={{ color: 'var(--c-text-muted)', margin: 0 }}>Hãy chọn món ăn yêu thích trước nhé!</p>
        <button
          className="c-btn c-btn-primary"
          onClick={() => navigate('/client/menu')}
        >
          🍜 Xem thực đơn
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)', background: 'var(--c-bg)', minHeight: '100%', padding: '32px 24px 64px' }}>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 960, margin: '0 auto' }}>

        {/* Page header */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, marginBottom: 28 }}>
          <button
            className="c-btn c-btn-ghost c-btn-sm"
            onClick={() => step === 1 ? navigate('/client/menu') : setStep((s) => (s - 1) as 1 | 2 | 3)}
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Quay lại
          </button>
          <h1 style={{ fontSize: '1.25rem', fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--c-text-main)', margin: 0 }}>
            Xác nhận đặt hàng
          </h1>
        </div>

        <StepIndicator />

        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 300px', gap: 24, alignItems: 'start' }}>
          {/* ── Main Content (Left) ── */}
          <div>

            {/* ─ STEP 1: Review Cart ─ */}
            {step === 1 && (
              <div className="c-animate-fade-up" style={{
                background: 'var(--c-surface)', border: '1px solid var(--c-border)',
                borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-lg)', overflow: 'hidden', boxShadow: 'var(--c-shadow-sm)',
              }}>
                <div style={{ padding: '18px 20px', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--c-border-light)' }}>
                  <h2 style={{ fontSize: '1rem', fontWeight: 700, margin: 0, color: 'var(--c-text-main)' }}>
                    🛒 Giỏ hàng ({totalItems} món)
                  </h2>
                </div>
                {cart.map((item) => (
                  <div key={item.menuItemId} style={{
                    display: 'flex', gap: 14, padding: '16px 20px',
                    borderBottom: '1px solid var(--c-border-light)',
                  }}>
                    <img src={item.image} alt={item.name} style={{
                      width: 72, height: 72, borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-md)', objectFit: 'cover', flexShrink: 0,
                    }} />
                    <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                      <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--c-text-main)', marginBottom: 4 }}>{item.name}</div>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', color: 'var(--c-primary)', fontWeight: 800 }}>
                        {item.price.toLocaleString('vi-VN')}đ / món
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 0, flexShrink: 0 }}>
                      <div style={{
                        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center',
                        border: '1.5px solid var(--c-primary)', borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-md)', overflow: 'hidden',
                      }}>
                        <button
                          onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.menuItemId)}
                          style={{ width: 36, height: 36, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', background: 'transparent', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', color: item.quantity === 1 ? 'var(--c-error)' : 'var(--c-primary)', fontSize: '1.1rem' }}
                        >
                          {item.quantity === 1 ? '🗑' : '−'}
                        </button>
                        <span style={{ width: 32, textAlign: 'center', fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--c-primary)', fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)' }}>
                          {item.quantity}
                        </span>
                        <button
                          onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.menuItemId, +1)}
                          style={{ width: 36, height: 36, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', background: 'var(--c-primary)', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', color: '#fff', fontSize: '1.1rem' }}
                        >
                          +
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
                <div style={{ padding: 20 }}>
                  <button
                    id="cart-step2-btn"
                    className="c-btn c-btn-primary c-btn-lg c-btn-block"
                    onClick={() => setStep(2)}
                    disabled={!canGoStep2}
                  >
                    Chọn hình thức đặt →
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* ─ STEP 2: Order Mode — Hick's Law: 3 big choices ─ */}
            {step === 2 && (
              <div className="c-animate-fade-up">
                <div style={{
                  background: 'var(--c-surface)', border: '1px solid var(--c-border)',
                  borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-lg)', padding: 24, boxShadow: 'var(--c-shadow-sm)', marginBottom: 20,
                }}>
                  <h2 style={{ fontSize: '1rem', fontWeight: 700, margin: '0 0 20px', color: 'var(--c-text-main)' }}>
                    Bạn muốn dùng bữa như thế nào?
                  </h2>
                  {/* 3 big buttons (Fitts's Law + Hick's Law) */}
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
                    {ORDER_MODES.map(({ mode, label, desc, emoji }) => {
                      const active = orderMode === mode;
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={mode}
                          id={`order-mode-${mode}`}
                          onClick={() => setOrderMode(mode)}
                          style={{
                            display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 16,
                            padding: '18px 20px', borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-lg)',
                            border: `2px solid ${active ? 'var(--c-primary)' : 'var(--c-border)'}`,
                            background: active ? 'var(--c-primary-light)' : 'var(--c-surface-warm)',
                            cursor: 'pointer', textAlign: 'left', transition: 'all var(--c-t-fast)',
                            fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)',
                          }}
                        >
                          <span style={{ fontSize: '2rem', flexShrink: 0 }}>{emoji}</span>
                          <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                            <div style={{ fontSize: '1rem', fontWeight: 700, color: active ? 'var(--c-primary)' : 'var(--c-text-main)', marginBottom: 2 }}>
                              {label}
                            </div>
                            <div style={{ fontSize: '0.8125rem', color: 'var(--c-text-muted)' }}>
                              {desc}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                          {active && (
                            <div style={{
                              width: 24, height: 24, borderRadius: '50%',
                              background: 'var(--c-primary)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', flexShrink: 0,
                            }}>
                              <Check size={14} color="#fff" strokeWidth={3} />
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </button>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Payment method */}
                <div style={{
                  background: 'var(--c-surface)', border: '1px solid var(--c-border)',
                  borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-lg)', padding: 24, boxShadow: 'var(--c-shadow-sm)', marginBottom: 20,
                }}>
                  <h2 style={{ fontSize: '1rem', fontWeight: 700, margin: '0 0 16px', color: 'var(--c-text-main)' }}>
                    Phương thức thanh toán
                  </h2>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10 }}>
                    {PAY_METHODS.map(({ key, label, icon: Icon }) => {
                      const active = payMethod === key;
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={key}
                          id={`pay-method-${key}`}
                          onClick={() => setPayMethod(key)}
                          style={{
                            flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6,
                            padding: '14px 8px', borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-md)',
                            border: `2px solid ${active ? 'var(--c-primary)' : 'var(--c-border)'}`,
                            background: active ? 'var(--c-primary-light)' : 'var(--c-surface-warm)',
                            cursor: 'pointer', fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)', transition: 'all var(--c-t-fast)',
                          }}
                        >
                          <Icon size={20} color={active ? 'var(--c-primary)' : 'var(--c-text-muted)'} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                          <span style={{ fontSize: '0.75rem', fontWeight: active ? 700 : 500, color: active ? 'var(--c-primary)' : 'var(--c-text-body)' }}>
                            {label}
                          </span>
                        </button>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  id="cart-step3-btn"
                  className="c-btn c-btn-primary c-btn-lg c-btn-block"
                  onClick={() => setStep(3)}
                  disabled={!canGoStep3}
                >
                  Nhập thông tin →
                </button>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* ─ STEP 3: Info Form — minimal fields ─ */}
            {step === 3 && (
              <div className="c-animate-fade-up" style={{
                background: 'var(--c-surface)', border: '1px solid var(--c-border)',
                borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-lg)', padding: 24, boxShadow: 'var(--c-shadow-sm)',
              }}>
                <h2 style={{ fontSize: '1rem', fontWeight: 700, margin: '0 0 20px', color: 'var(--c-text-main)' }}>
                  Thông tin đặt hàng
                </h2>

                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>
                  {/* Conditional fields based on order mode */}
                  {orderMode === 'DINE_IN' && (
                    <label style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6 }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--c-text-body)' }}>
                        Số bàn <span style={{ color: 'var(--c-error)' }}>*</span>
                      </span>
                      <input
                        id="table-no-input"
                        className="form-control"
                        style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)', borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-md)', borderColor: 'var(--c-border)', padding: '12px 16px' }}
                        placeholder="VD: Bàn 05"
                        value={tableNo}
                        onChange={(e) => setTableNo(e.target.value)}
                      />
                    </label>
                  )}

                  {orderMode === 'DELIVERY' && (
                    <label style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6 }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--c-text-body)' }}>
                        Địa chỉ giao hàng <span style={{ color: 'var(--c-error)' }}>*</span>
                      </span>
                      <input
                        id="delivery-address-input"
                        className="form-control"
                        style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)', borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-md)', borderColor: 'var(--c-border)', padding: '12px 16px' }}
                        placeholder="Số nhà, tên đường, phường, quận..."
                        value={deliveryAddr}
                        onChange={(e) => setDeliveryAddr(e.target.value)}
                      />
                    </label>
                  )}

                  <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 16 }}>
                    <label style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6 }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--c-text-body)' }}>Tên khách hàng</span>
                      <input
                        id="customer-name-input"
                        className="form-control"
                        style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)', borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-md)', borderColor: 'var(--c-border)', padding: '12px 16px' }}
                        placeholder="Không bắt buộc"
                        value={customerName}
                        onChange={(e) => setCustomerName(e.target.value)}
                      />
                    </label>
                    <label style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6 }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--c-text-body)' }}>Số điện thoại</span>
                      <input
                        id="customer-phone-input"
                        className="form-control"
                        style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)', borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-md)', borderColor: 'var(--c-border)', padding: '12px 16px' }}
                        placeholder="Không bắt buộc"
                        value={customerPhone}
                        onChange={(e) => setCustomerPhone(e.target.value)}
                      />
                    </label>
                  </div>

                  <label style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6 }}>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--c-text-body)' }}>Ghi chú cho bếp</span>
                    <textarea
                      id="order-notes-input"
                      className="form-control"
                      style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)', borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-md)', borderColor: 'var(--c-border)', padding: '12px 16px', resize: 'none', minHeight: 80 }}
                      placeholder="Dị ứng thực phẩm, ít cay, không hành..."
                      value={notes}
                      onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
                    />
                  </label>

                  {/* Confirm button — Fitts's Law: full-width, 52px */}
                  <button
                    id="place-order-btn"
                    className="c-btn c-btn-primary c-btn-lg c-btn-block"
                    style={{ marginTop: 8 }}
                    onClick={handlePlaceOrder}
                    disabled={!canConfirm}
                  >
                    ✅ Xác nhận đặt hàng · {totalAmount.toLocaleString('vi-VN')}đ
                  </button>
                  <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: '0.75rem', color: 'var(--c-text-muted)', margin: 0 }}>
                    Bằng cách đặt hàng, bạn đồng ý với điều khoản dịch vụ của OmniResto
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

          </div>

          {/* ── Order Summary Sidebar (Anchoring) ── */}
          <div style={{ display: 'none' }}
            ref={(el) => { if (el) el.style.display = window.innerWidth >= 768 ? 'block' : 'none'; }}>
            <OrderSummary />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <style>{`
        @media (min-width: 768px) {
          #cart-summary-sidebar { display: block !important; }
        }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ClientCart;
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\client\pages\home\ClientHome.tsx =====  
import React from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { MapPin, Star, ArrowRight, Clock, Users, ChevronRight, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAdminStore } from '../../../admin/store/adminStore';
import { useClientStore } from '../../store/clientStore';

/**
 * ClientHome — Landing page
 *
 * Layout: Z-Pattern scanning
 *   Zone 1 (Top): Hero — Logo → tagline → CTAs → social proof
 *   Zone 2 (Mid-left): Branch selection cards
 *   Zone 3 (Mid-right): Category shortcuts
 *   Zone 4 (Bottom): Best sellers preview (Serial Position Effect)
 *
 * UX Principles:
 * - Z-Pattern: Logo top-left → CTA top-right → branches → best sellers
 * - Color Psychology: Warm orange hero → appetite stimulation
 * - Social Proof: Rating + review count in hero
 * - Progressive Disclosure: Home → select branch → menu
 * - Fitts's Law: Branch cards large enough to tap; CTA buttons prominent
 * - Serial Position Effect: Best sellers at end of page (memorable)
 */
const ClientHome: React.FC = () => {

    setSelectedBranch(id, name);
    navigate('/client/menu');

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\client\pages\login\index.tsx =====  
import { useState, type ChangeEvent, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { ApiRequestError, loginCustomer } from "../../services/authService";
import "../register/register.css"; // dùng lại giao diện của trang đăng ký

export default function LoginPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ identifier: "", password: "" });
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<{ identifier?: string; password?: string }>({});
  const [showPw, setShowPw] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [serverMsg, setServerMsg] = useState("");

  const set = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const { name, value } = e.target;
    setForm({ ...form, [name]: value });
    setErrors({ ...errors, [name]: undefined });
  };

  const submit = async (ev: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    ev.preventDefault();
    setServerMsg("");

    const v: typeof errors = {};
    if (!form.identifier.trim()) v.identifier = "Vui lòng nhập email hoặc số điện thoại";
    if (!form.password) v.password = "Vui lòng nhập mật khẩu";
    setErrors(v);
    if (Object.keys(v).length) return;

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const customer = await loginCustomer({ identifier: form.identifier.trim(), password: form.password });
      localStorage.setItem("customer", JSON.stringify(customer)); // lưu tạm thông tin khách đã đăng nhập
      navigate("/client");
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof ApiRequestError) {
        setServerMsg(
          err.status === 401 || err.status === 400
            ? "Email/số điện thoại hoặc mật khẩu không đúng"
            : err.message
        );
      } else {
        setServerMsg("Không kết nối được máy chủ. Kiểm tra backend đã chạy ở cổng 8080 chưa.");
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="rg-page">
      <aside className="rg-brand">
        <div className="rg-logo">OmniResto</div>
        <h2>Chào mừng bạn quay lại.</h2>
        <p>Đăng nhập để xem điểm tích lũy và nhận ưu đãi dành cho thành viên.</p>
      </aside>

      <section className="rg-formwrap">
        <form className="rg-card" onSubmit={submit} noValidate>
          <h1>Đăng nhập</h1>
          <p className="rg-sub">Dùng email hoặc số điện thoại bạn đã đăng ký.</p>

          {serverMsg && <div className="rg-alert" role="alert">{serverMsg}</div>}

          <div className={"rg-field" + (errors.identifier ? " has-error" : "")}>
            <label htmlFor="identifier">Email hoặc số điện thoại</label>
            <input id="identifier" name="identifier" value={form.identifier} onChange={set}
              autoComplete="username" placeholder="ban@example.com" />
            {errors.identifier && <p className="rg-error">{errors.identifier}</p>}
          </div>

          <div className={"rg-field" + (errors.password ? " has-error" : "")}>
            <label htmlFor="password">Mật khẩu</label>
            <div className="rg-pw">
              <input id="password" name="password" type={showPw ? "text" : "password"}
                value={form.password} onChange={set} autoComplete="current-password" />
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPw(!showPw)} aria-pressed={showPw}>
                {showPw ? "Ẩn" : "Hiện"}
              </button>
            </div>
            {errors.password && <p className="rg-error">{errors.password}</p>}
          </div>

          <button className="rg-btn" disabled={loading} style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
            {loading ? "Đang đăng nhập..." : "Đăng nhập"}
          </button>

          <p className="rg-hint" style={{ textAlign: "center", margin: "18px 0 0" }}>
            Chưa có tài khoản?{" "}
            <Link to="/register" style={{ color: "#2563eb", fontWeight: 600 }}>Đăng ký thành viên</Link>
          </p>
        </form>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\client\pages\menu\ClientMenu.tsx =====  
import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Search, ShoppingCart } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAdminStore } from '../../../admin/store/adminStore';
import { useClientStore, selectTotalItems, selectTotalAmount } from '../../store/clientStore';
import { MenuCard } from '../../components/MenuCard';

const ALL_CATEGORY = 'Tất cả';

/**
 * ClientMenu — Browsing + ordering interface
 */
export const ClientMenu: React.FC = () => {
  const { menuItems } = useAdminStore();
  const { selectedBranchName, openCart } = useClientStore();
  const totalItems  = useClientStore(selectTotalItems);
  const totalAmount = useClientStore(selectTotalAmount);

  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState<string>(ALL_CATEGORY);
  const [searchQuery,    setSearchQuery]    = useState('');

  // Build category list (Hick's Law: limit choice set)
  const categories: string[] = useMemo(() => {
    const cats = Array.from(new Set(menuItems.map((m) => m.category))) as string[];
    return [ALL_CATEGORY, ...cats];
  }, [menuItems]);

  // Filter + sort items (Serial Position: best sellers first)
  const filteredItems = useMemo(() => {
    let items = menuItems;
    if (activeCategory !== ALL_CATEGORY) {
      items = items.filter((m) => m.category === activeCategory);
    }
    if (searchQuery.trim()) {
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      items = items.filter(
        (m) => m.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || m.description.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
    }
    // Best sellers first (Serial Position Effect)
    return [...items].sort((a, b) => (b.isBestSeller ? 1 : 0) - (a.isBestSeller ? 1 : 0));
  }, [menuItems, activeCategory, searchQuery]);

  const availableCount   = filteredItems.filter((m) => m.status === 'AVAILABLE' || m.status === 'available').length;
  const unavailableCount = filteredItems.length - availableCount;

  return (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)', background: 'var(--c-bg)', minHeight: '100%' }}>

      {/* ── Context + Search Bar (F-Pattern: top row) ── */}
      <div style={{
        background: 'var(--c-surface)',
        borderBottom: '1px solid var(--c-border-light)',
        padding: '16px 24px',
      }}>
        <div style={{
          maxWidth: 'var(--c-max-w)', margin: '0 auto',
          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 16, flexWrap: 'wrap',
        }}>
          {/* Context label */}
          <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 200 }}>
            <h1 style={{ fontSize: '1.125rem', fontWeight: 800, color: 'var(--c-text-main)', margin: 0 }}>
              Thực Đơn
            </h1>
            {selectedBranchName && (
              <span style={{ fontSize: '0.8125rem', color: 'var(--c-text-muted)' }}>
                📍 {selectedBranchName}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Search — Hick's Law: narrow down choices fast */}
          <div style={{
            display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10,
            background: 'var(--c-surface-warm)', border: '1.5px solid var(--c-border)',
            borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-lg)', padding: '8px 16px',
            flex: '0 1 320px', transition: 'border-color var(--c-t-fast)',
          }}
            onFocusCapture={e => { (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).style.borderColor = 'var(--c-primary)'; }}
            onBlurCapture={e  => { (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).style.borderColor = 'var(--c-border)'; }}
          >
            <Search size={16} color="var(--c-text-muted)" strokeWidth={2} />
            <input
              id="menu-search-input"
              type="text"
              placeholder="Tìm món ăn..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              style={{
                border: 'none', background: 'transparent', outline: 'none',
                font: '0.875rem/1 var(--c-font)', color: 'var(--c-text-main)',
                width: '100%',
              }}
            />
            {searchQuery && (
              <button
                onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
                style={{
                  background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer',
                  color: 'var(--c-text-muted)', padding: 0, lineHeight: 1,
                }}
              >
                ✕
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Sticky Category Filter (Miller's Law: max 7 visible) ── */}
      <div style={{
        position: 'sticky', top: 'var(--c-header-h)', zIndex: 100,
        background: 'rgba(255,252,249,0.95)', backdropFilter: 'blur(10px)',
        borderBottom: '1px solid var(--c-border-light)',
        overflowX: 'auto',
        /* Hide scrollbar while allowing scroll */
        scrollbarWidth: 'none',
      }}>
        <div style={{
          maxWidth: 'var(--c-max-w)', margin: '0 auto',
          padding: '10px 24px',
          display: 'flex', gap: 8, alignItems: 'center',
          width: 'max-content', minWidth: '100%',
        }}>
          {categories.map((cat) => {
            const isActive = cat === activeCategory;
            return (
              <button
                key={cat}
                id={`category-filter-${cat}`}
                className="c-btn"
                style={{
                  minHeight: 38, padding: '6px 18px', borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-full)',
                  fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: isActive ? 700 : 500,
                  whiteSpace: 'nowrap', flexShrink: 0,
                  background: isActive ? 'var(--c-primary)' : 'var(--c-surface)',
                  color: isActive ? '#fff' : 'var(--c-text-body)',
                  border: `1.5px solid ${isActive ? 'var(--c-primary)' : 'var(--c-border)'}`,
                  boxShadow: isActive ? 'var(--c-shadow-orange)' : 'none',
                  transition: 'all var(--c-t-fast)',
                }}
                onClick={() => setActiveCategory(cat)}
                aria-pressed={isActive}
              >
                {cat}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Menu Grid + Floating Cart ── */}
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 'var(--c-max-w)', margin: '0 auto', padding: '28px 24px 80px' }}>

        {/* Results summary */}
        {filteredItems.length > 0 && (
          <div style={{
            display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, marginBottom: 24,
            flexWrap: 'wrap',
          }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', color: 'var(--c-text-muted)' }}>
              <strong style={{ color: 'var(--c-text-body)' }}>{filteredItems.length}</strong> món
              {activeCategory !== ALL_CATEGORY && ` · ${activeCategory}`}
            </span>
            {availableCount < filteredItems.length && (
              <span className="c-badge c-badge-gray">
                {unavailableCount} tạm hết
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Empty state */}
        {filteredItems.length === 0 && (
          <div style={{
            display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center',
            gap: 16, padding: '64px 24px', textAlign: 'center',
          }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '3rem' }}>🍽️</span>
            <div>
              <strong style={{ color: 'var(--c-text-main)', display: 'block', marginBottom: 6 }}>
                Không tìm thấy món
              </strong>
              <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', color: 'var(--c-text-muted)' }}>
                Thử từ khóa khác hoặc chọn danh mục khác
              </span>
            </div>
            <button
              className="c-btn c-btn-outline c-btn-sm"
              onClick={() => { setSearchQuery(''); setActiveCategory(ALL_CATEGORY); }}
            >
              Xem tất cả món
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* 2-column card grid (F-Pattern: scan left first) */}
        <div style={{
          display: 'grid',
          gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(260px, 1fr))',
          gap: 20,
        }}>
          {filteredItems.map((item, idx) => (
            <MenuCard
              key={item.id}
              item={item}
              animationDelay={Math.min(idx * 50, 400)}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Floating Cart CTA — Fitts's Law: large, always visible ── */}
      {totalItems > 0 && (
        <div style={{
          position: 'fixed', bottom: 24, left: '50%', transform: 'translateX(-50%)',
          zIndex: 150,
          animation: 'c-fade-up 0.3s var(--c-t-fast) both',
        }}>
          <button
            id="floating-cart-btn"
            onClick={openCart}
            style={{
              display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12,
              padding: '14px 24px', borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-full)',
              background: 'var(--c-primary)', color: '#fff',
              border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer',
              font: '700 0.9375rem var(--c-font)',
              boxShadow: '0 8px 32px rgba(232,93,38,0.45)',
              transition: 'transform var(--c-t-fast), box-shadow var(--c-t-fast)',
              whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
            }}
            onMouseEnter={e => {
              (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).style.transform = 'translateX(-50%) translateY(-2px)';
              (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).style.boxShadow = '0 12px 40px rgba(232,93,38,0.55)';
            }}
            onMouseLeave={e => {
              (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).style.transform = 'translateX(-50%)';
              (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).style.boxShadow = '0 8px 32px rgba(232,93,38,0.45)';
            }}
            aria-label={`Xem giỏ hàng: ${totalItems} món, ${totalAmount.toLocaleString('vi-VN')}đ`}
          >
            <div style={{
              background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.25)', borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-full)',
              width: 28, height: 28, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
              fontSize: '0.75rem', fontWeight: 900,
            }}>
              {totalItems}
            </div>
            <ShoppingCart size={18} strokeWidth={2.2} />
            Xem giỏ hàng · {totalAmount.toLocaleString('vi-VN')}đ
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default ClientMenu;
