import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Receipt } from 'lucide-react';
import { useClientStore } from '../../store/clientStore';
import { OrderStatusStepper } from '../../components/OrderStatusStepper';
import type { PlacedOrderStatus } from '../../../shared/types/client';

const ORDER_MODE_LABELS = {
  DINE_IN:  { label: 'Dùng tại bàn',  emoji: '🍽️' },
  TAKEAWAY: { label: 'Mang về',       emoji: '📦' },
  DELIVERY: { label: 'Giao hàng',     emoji: '🚴' },
};

const PAY_LABELS = {
  QR_CODE: 'Quét mã QR',
  CASH:    'Tiền mặt',
  CARD:    'Thẻ ngân hàng',
};

/**
 * ClientOrderTracking — Real-time-like order status page
 *
 * UX Principles:
 * - Progressive Disclosure: Stepper shows current stage clearly
 * - Feedback: Status updates with smooth visual transition
 * - Anchoring: Order details summary keeps context visible
 * - Color Psychology: Green = success, Orange = active, Gray = future
 * - Gestalt Proximity: Order items grouped together
 */
const ClientOrderTracking: React.FC = () => {
  const { orderId } = useParams<{ orderId: string }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { placedOrder } = useClientStore();

  // Simulate progressive status update (demo purpose)
  const [simulatedStatus, setSimulatedStatus] = useState<PlacedOrderStatus>(
    placedOrder?.status ?? 'PENDING'
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!placedOrder) return;
    setSimulatedStatus(placedOrder.status);

    // Auto-advance demo: PENDING → PREPARING after 4s
    const t1 = setTimeout(() => setSimulatedStatus('PREPARING'), 4000);
    // → READY after 12s
    const t2 = setTimeout(() => setSimulatedStatus('READY'), 12000);

    return () => { clearTimeout(t1); clearTimeout(t2); };
  }, [placedOrder]);

  if (!placedOrder || placedOrder.id !== orderId) {
    return (
      <div style={{
        fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)', minHeight: '70vh',
        display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center', gap: 20, padding: 40, textAlign: 'center',
      }}>
        <span style={{ fontSize: '4rem' }}>🔍</span>
        <h2 style={{ color: 'var(--c-text-main)', margin: 0 }}>Không tìm thấy đơn hàng</h2>
        <p style={{ color: 'var(--c-text-muted)', margin: 0 }}>
          Mã đơn: <code style={{ background: 'var(--c-surface-warm)', padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: 4 }}>{orderId}</code>
        </p>
        <button className="c-btn c-btn-primary" onClick={() => navigate('/client/menu')}>
          🍜 Đặt món mới
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const modeInfo = ORDER_MODE_LABELS[placedOrder.orderMode];
  const isCompleted = simulatedStatus === 'COMPLETED';

  return (
    <div style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--c-font)', background: 'var(--c-bg)', minHeight: '100%', padding: '32px 24px 64px' }}>
      <div style={{ maxWidth: 640, margin: '0 auto' }}>

        {/* Header */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, marginBottom: 28 }}>
          <button
            className="c-btn c-btn-ghost c-btn-sm"
            onClick={() => navigate('/client/menu')}
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Đặt thêm
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Success banner or active banner */}
        <div style={{
          borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-xl)', padding: '24px',
          marginBottom: 24,
          background: isCompleted
            ? 'linear-gradient(135deg, #16a34a 0%, #15803d 100%)'
            : 'linear-gradient(135deg, var(--c-primary) 0%, var(--c-primary-dark) 100%)',
          color: '#fff', textAlign: 'center',
          boxShadow: isCompleted
            ? '0 8px 32px rgba(22,163,74,0.35)'
            : 'var(--c-shadow-orange)',
          transition: 'all 0.6s ease',
        }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: '2.5rem', marginBottom: 8 }}>
            {isCompleted ? '🎉' : '⏳'}
          </div>
          <h1 style={{ fontSize: '1.25rem', fontWeight: 800, margin: '0 0 4px' }}>
            {isCompleted ? 'Đơn hàng hoàn thành!' : 'Đơn hàng đã được tiếp nhận'}
          </h1>
          <p style={{ opacity: 0.88, margin: '0 0 12px', fontSize: '0.9375rem' }}>
            {isCompleted
              ? 'Chúc bạn ngon miệng! Cảm ơn đã tin tùng OmniResto.'
              : `Đang xử lý · Dự kiến ${placedOrder.estimatedMinutes} phút`}
          </p>
          <div style={{
            display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6,
            background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)', borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-full)',
            padding: '5px 16px', fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: 700,
          }}>
            <Receipt size={14} />
            {placedOrder.orderNumber}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Order Status Stepper */}
        <div style={{
          background: 'var(--c-surface)', border: '1px solid var(--c-border)',
          borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-lg)', padding: '24px',
          boxShadow: 'var(--c-shadow-sm)', marginBottom: 20,
        }}>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: '0.9375rem', fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--c-text-main)', margin: '0 0 20px' }}>
            Trạng thái đơn hàng
          </h2>
          <OrderStatusStepper
            status={simulatedStatus}
            estimatedMinutes={isCompleted ? undefined : placedOrder.estimatedMinutes}
          />
        </div>

        {/* Order details */}
        <div style={{
          background: 'var(--c-surface)', border: '1px solid var(--c-border)',
          borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-lg)', overflow: 'hidden',
          boxShadow: 'var(--c-shadow-sm)', marginBottom: 20,
        }}>
          {/* Info strip */}
          <div style={{
            display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 0,
            borderBottom: '1px solid var(--c-border-light)',
          }}>
            {[
              { label: 'Hình thức', value: `${modeInfo.emoji} ${modeInfo.label}` },
              { label: 'Chi nhánh', value: `📍 ${placedOrder.branchName}` },
              placedOrder.tableNo   ? { label: 'Bàn',          value: `🪑 ${placedOrder.tableNo}` } : null,
              placedOrder.customerName ? { label: 'Khách hàng', value: `👤 ${placedOrder.customerName}` } : null,
              { label: 'Thanh toán', value: PAY_LABELS[placedOrder.paymentMethod] },
              { label: 'Đặt lúc', value: `🕐 ${placedOrder.placedAt}` },
            ].filter(Boolean).map((info) => (
              <div key={info!.label} style={{
                flex: '1 1 200px', padding: '14px 20px',
                borderRight: '1px solid var(--c-border-light)',
                borderBottom: '1px solid var(--c-border-light)',
              }}>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '0.6875rem', color: 'var(--c-text-muted)', fontWeight: 600, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.06em', marginBottom: 3 }}>
                  {info!.label}
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--c-text-body)' }}>
                  {info!.value}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Items list — Gestalt Proximity: items grouped */}
          <div style={{ padding: '16px 20px' }}>
            <h3 style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--c-text-main)', margin: '0 0 12px' }}>
              Món đã đặt
            </h3>
            {placedOrder.items.map((item) => (
              <div key={item.menuItemId} style={{
                display: 'flex', gap: 12, alignItems: 'center',
                padding: '8px 0', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--c-border-light)',
              }}>
                <img
                  src={item.image}
                  alt={item.name}
                  style={{ width: 44, height: 44, borderRadius: 'var(--c-r-sm)', objectFit: 'cover', flexShrink: 0 }}
                />
                <span style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: '0.875rem', color: 'var(--c-text-body)', fontWeight: 500 }}>
                  {item.name}
                  <span style={{ color: 'var(--c-text-muted)', marginLeft: 4 }}>×{item.quantity}</span>
                </span>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--c-text-main)', flexShrink: 0 }}>
                  {(item.price * item.quantity).toLocaleString('vi-VN')}đ
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}

            {/* Total */}
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginTop: 14, paddingTop: 12 }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: '0.9375rem', fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--c-text-body)' }}>Tổng cộng</span>
              <span style={{ fontSize: '1.25rem', fontWeight: 900, color: 'var(--c-primary)' }}>
                {placedOrder.totalAmount.toLocaleString('vi-VN')}đ
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* CTA buttons */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12 }}>
          <button
            className="c-btn c-btn-outline c-btn-block"
            onClick={() => navigate('/client/menu')}
          >
            🍜 Đặt thêm món
          </button>
          <button
            className="c-btn c-btn-ghost c-btn-block"
            onClick={() => navigate('/client')}
          >
            🏠 Trang chủ
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ClientOrderTracking;
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\client\pages\register\index.tsx =====  
import { useState, type ChangeEvent, type FormEvent, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { ApiRequestError, registerCustomer } from "../../services/authService";
import type { CustomerResponse } from "../../../shared/types/customer";
import "./register.css";

interface FormState {
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  dateOfBirth: string;
  password: string;
  confirmPassword: string;
}

type Errors = Record<string, string | undefined>;

const TIERS = [
  { name: "Tiêu chuẩn", note: "Bạn bắt đầu ở đây" },
  { name: "Bạc", note: "Tích điểm để lên hạng" },
  { name: "Vàng", note: "Nhiều ưu đãi hơn" },
  { name: "Bạch kim", note: "Hạng cao nhất" },
];

const EMPTY: FormState = { fullName: "", email: "", phone: "", dateOfBirth: "", password: "", confirmPassword: "" };

function validate(f: FormState): Errors {
  const e: Errors = {};
  if (!f.fullName.trim()) e.fullName = "Vui lòng nhập họ tên";
  if (!f.email.trim()) e.email = "Vui lòng nhập email";
  else if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(f.email)) e.email = "Email không hợp lệ";
  if (!f.phone.trim()) e.phone = "Vui lòng nhập số điện thoại";
  else if (!/^(0|\+84)\d{9}$/.test(f.phone)) e.phone = "Số điện thoại không hợp lệ (ví dụ 0912345678)";
  if (f.dateOfBirth && new Date(f.dateOfBirth) >= new Date()) e.dateOfBirth = "Ngày sinh phải ở quá khứ";
  if (f.password.length < 8) e.password = "Mật khẩu cần ít nhất 8 ký tự";
  else if (!/[A-Za-z]/.test(f.password) || !/\d/.test(f.password)) e.password = "Mật khẩu cần có cả chữ và số";
  if (f.confirmPassword !== f.password) e.confirmPassword = "Mật khẩu nhập lại không khớp";
  return e;
}

interface FieldProps {
  label: string;
  name: string;
  error?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}

function Field({ label, name, error, children }: FieldProps) {
  return (
    <div className={"rg-field" + (error ? " has-error" : "")}>
      <label htmlFor={name}>{label}</label>
      {children}
      {error && <p className="rg-error" id={name + "-err"}>{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function RegisterPage() {
  const [form, setForm] = useState<FormState>(EMPTY);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Errors>({});
  const [showPw, setShowPw] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [serverMsg, setServerMsg] = useState("");
  const [done, setDone] = useState<CustomerResponse | null>(null);

  const set = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const { name, value } = e.target;
    setForm({ ...form, [name]: value });
    if (errors[name]) setErrors({ ...errors, [name]: undefined });
  };

  const submit = async (ev: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    ev.preventDefault();
    setServerMsg("");
    const v = validate(form);
    setErrors(v);
    if (Object.keys(v).length) return;

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      setDone(await registerCustomer({ ...form, dateOfBirth: form.dateOfBirth || null }));
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof ApiRequestError) {
        if (err.status === 400 && err.fieldErrors) {
          const mapped: Errors = { ...err.fieldErrors };
          if (mapped.passwordConfirmed) mapped.confirmPassword = mapped.passwordConfirmed;
          return setErrors(mapped);
        }
        if (err.status === 409) {
          if (/điện thoại/i.test(err.message)) return setErrors({ phone: err.message });
          if (/email/i.test(err.message)) return setErrors({ email: err.message });
        }
        return setServerMsg(err.message);
      }
      setServerMsg("Không kết nối được máy chủ. Kiểm tra backend đã chạy ở cổng 8080 chưa.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (done) {
    return (
      <main className="rg-page">
        <section className="rg-done">
          <div className="rg-check" aria-hidden="true">✓</div>
          <h1>Chào mừng, {done.fullName}</h1>
          <p>Tài khoản thành viên của bạn đã sẵn sàng. Mã thành viên: <strong>{done.id}</strong></p>
          <dl>
            <div><dt>Hạng hiện tại</dt><dd>Tiêu chuẩn</dd></div>
            <div><dt>Điểm tích lũy</dt><dd>{done.loyaltyPoints}</dd></div>
          </dl>
          <button className="rg-btn" onClick={() => { setDone(null); setForm(EMPTY); }}>
            Đăng ký tài khoản khác
          </button>
        </section>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="rg-page">
      <aside className="rg-brand">
        <div className="rg-logo">OmniResto</div>
        <h2>Ăn ở chi nhánh nào cũng được tích điểm.</h2>
        <p>Một tài khoản dùng cho toàn bộ chuỗi. Điểm của bạn được cộng sau mỗi bữa ăn.</p>
        <ol className="rg-tiers">
          {TIERS.map((t, i) => (
            <li key={t.name} className={i === 0 ? "is-start" : ""}>
              <span className="rg-dot" />
              <span><strong>{t.name}</strong><small>{t.note}</small></span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      </aside>

      <section className="rg-formwrap">
        <form className="rg-card" onSubmit={submit} noValidate>
          <h1>Đăng ký thành viên</h1>
          <p className="rg-sub">Chỉ mất một phút. Bạn có thể bỏ trống ngày sinh.</p>

          {serverMsg && <div className="rg-alert" role="alert">{serverMsg}</div>}

          <Field label="Họ và tên" name="fullName" error={errors.fullName}>
            <input id="fullName" name="fullName" value={form.fullName} onChange={set}
              autoComplete="name" placeholder="Nguyễn Văn A" aria-describedby="fullName-err" />
          </Field>

          <div className="rg-row">
            <Field label="Email" name="email" error={errors.email}>
              <input id="email" name="email" type="email" value={form.email} onChange={set}
                autoComplete="email" placeholder="ban@example.com" />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Số điện thoại" name="phone" error={errors.phone}>
              <input id="phone" name="phone" type="tel" value={form.phone} onChange={set}
                autoComplete="tel" placeholder="0912345678" />
            </Field>
          </div>

          <Field label="Ngày sinh " name="dateOfBirth" error={errors.dateOfBirth}>
            <input id="dateOfBirth" name="dateOfBirth" type="date" value={form.dateOfBirth} onChange={set} />
          </Field>

          <div className="rg-row">
            <Field label="Mật khẩu" name="password" error={errors.password}>
              <div className="rg-pw">
                <input id="password" name="password" type={showPw ? "text" : "password"}
                  value={form.password} onChange={set} autoComplete="new-password" placeholder="Ít nhất 8 ký tự" />
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPw(!showPw)} aria-pressed={showPw}>
                  {showPw ? "Ẩn" : "Hiện"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </Field>
            <Field label="Nhập lại mật khẩu" name="confirmPassword" error={errors.confirmPassword}>
              <input id="confirmPassword" name="confirmPassword" type={showPw ? "text" : "password"}
                value={form.confirmPassword} onChange={set} autoComplete="new-password" />
            </Field>
          </div>
          <p className="rg-hint">Mật khẩu cần có cả chữ và số.</p>

          <button className="rg-btn" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? "Đang tạo tài khoản..." : "Tạo tài khoản"}
          </button>
        </form>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\client\pages\users\UserManagementPage.tsx =====  
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { authApi, type UserAdminDto, type UserStatus } from '../../../shared/api/authService';
import './UserManagementPage.css';

export const UserManagementPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [users, setUsers] = useState<UserAdminDto[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string>('');

  const fetchUsers = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const res = await authApi.getAllUsers();
      setUsers(res.data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.message || 'Không thể tải danh sách người dùng');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchUsers();
  }, []);

  const handleStatusChange = async (userId: string, newStatus: UserStatus) => {
    try {
      await authApi.updateUserStatus(userId, newStatus);
      setUsers((prevUsers) =>
        prevUsers.map((u) => (u.id === userId ? { ...u, status: newStatus } : u))
      );
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || 'Cập nhật trạng thái thất bại!');
    }
  };

  const getStatusBadge = (status: UserStatus) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'ACTIVE':
        return <span className="badge badge-active">Hoạt động</span>;
      case 'PENDING_VERIFICATION':
        return <span className="badge badge-pending">Chờ xác minh</span>;
      case 'BLOCKED':
        return <span className="badge badge-blocked">Đã bị khóa</span>;
      default:
        return <span className="badge badge-default">{status}</span>;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="admin-users-container">
      <div className="admin-users-wrapper">
        <h1 className="admin-users-title">Quản Lý Đăng Ký Thành Viên</h1>

        {error && <div className="admin-users-error">{error}</div>}

        {loading ? (
          <div className="admin-users-loading">Đang tải dữ liệu...</div>
        ) : (
          <div className="admin-users-card">
            <table className="admin-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Họ và Tên</th>
                  <th>Email</th>
                  <th>Số Điện Thoại</th>
                  <th>Vai Trò</th>
                  <th>Trạng Thái</th>
                  <th className="text-right">Hành Động</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {users.map((user) => (
                  <tr key={user.id}>
                    <td className="user-name">{user.fullName}</td>
                    <td>{user.email}</td>
                    <td>{user.phone}</td>
                    <td className="user-role">{user.role}</td>
                    <td>{getStatusBadge(user.status)}</td>
                    <td className="text-right">
                      {user.status === 'BLOCKED' ? (
                        <button onClick={() => handleStatusChange(user.id, 'ACTIVE')} className="btn-action-unblock">
                          Mở khóa
                        </button>
                      ) : (
                        <button onClick={() => handleStatusChange(user.id, 'BLOCKED')} className="btn-action-block">
                          Khóa tài khoản
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\client\services\authService.ts =====  
import type { CustomerRegisterPayload, CustomerResponse } from "../../shared/types/customer";

// Địa chỉ backend. Có thể đặt VITE_API_URL trong file .env của frontend
const API_URL: string = import.meta.env?.VITE_API_URL || "http://localhost:8080";

export class ApiRequestError extends Error {
  status: number;
  fieldErrors?: Record<string, string>;

  constructor(status: number, message: string, fieldErrors?: Record<string, string>) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.fieldErrors = fieldErrors;
  }
}

export async function registerCustomer(payload: CustomerRegisterPayload): Promise<CustomerResponse> {
  const res = await fetch(`${API_URL}/api/customers/register`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
  });

  const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!res.ok) {
throw new ApiRequestError(res.status, data.message || `Đăng ký chưa thành công (mã lỗi ${res.status})`, data.errors);
  }
  return data as CustomerResponse;
}
export async function loginCustomer(payload: { identifier: string; password: string }): Promise<CustomerResponse> {
  const res = await fetch(`${API_URL}/api/customers/login`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
  });

  const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!res.ok) {
throw new ApiRequestError(res.status, data.message || `Đăng nhập chưa thành công (mã lỗi ${res.status})`, data.errors);  }
  return data as CustomerResponse;
}  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\client\store\clientStore.ts =====  
import { create } from 'zustand';
import type { CartItem, OrderMode, ClientOrderDraft, PlacedOrder } from '../../shared/types/client';

// ─── State Interface ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface ClientState {
  // Branch context (set when user selects a branch on home page)
  selectedBranchId: string;
  selectedBranchName: string;
  setSelectedBranch: (id: string, name: string) => void;

  // Cart management
  cart: CartItem[];
  addToCart: (item: Omit<CartItem, 'quantity'>, quantity?: number) => void;
  removeFromCart: (menuItemId: string) => void;
  updateQuantity: (menuItemId: string, delta: number) => void;
  clearCart: () => void;

  // Order mode & draft
  orderMode: OrderMode;
  setOrderMode: (mode: OrderMode) => void;
  orderDraft: Partial<ClientOrderDraft>;
  setOrderDraft: (patch: Partial<ClientOrderDraft>) => void;

  // Placed order (result after checkout)
  placedOrder: PlacedOrder | null;
  placeOrder: (draft: ClientOrderDraft) => PlacedOrder;
  clearPlacedOrder: () => void;

  // Cart drawer open/close
  isCartOpen: boolean;
  openCart: () => void;
  closeCart: () => void;
}

// ─── Selectors (used with useClientStore(selector)) ───────────────────────────

export const selectTotalItems = (s: ClientState) =>
  s.cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);

export const selectTotalAmount = (s: ClientState) =>
  s.cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.price * item.quantity, 0);

// ─── Store ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

let orderCounter = 8000;

export const useClientStore = create<ClientState>((set, get) => ({
  // Branch
  selectedBranchId: '',
  selectedBranchName: '',
  setSelectedBranch: (id, name) => set({ selectedBranchId: id, selectedBranchName: name }),

  // Cart
  cart: [],

  addToCart: (item, quantity = 1) =>
    set((state) => {
      const existing = state.cart.find((c) => c.menuItemId === item.menuItemId);
      if (existing) {
        return {
          cart: state.cart.map((c) =>
            c.menuItemId === item.menuItemId
              ? { ...c, quantity: c.quantity + quantity }
              : c
          ),
        };
      }
      return { cart: [...state.cart, { ...item, quantity }] };
    }),

  removeFromCart: (menuItemId) =>
    set((state) => ({
      cart: state.cart.filter((c) => c.menuItemId !== menuItemId),
    })),

  updateQuantity: (menuItemId, delta) =>
    set((state) => {
      const item = state.cart.find((c) => c.menuItemId === menuItemId);
      if (!item) return state;
      const newQty = item.quantity + delta;
      if (newQty <= 0) {
        return { cart: state.cart.filter((c) => c.menuItemId !== menuItemId) };
      }
      return {
        cart: state.cart.map((c) =>
          c.menuItemId === menuItemId ? { ...c, quantity: newQty } : c
        ),
      };
    }),

  clearCart: () => set({ cart: [] }),

  // Order
  orderMode: 'DINE_IN',
  setOrderMode: (mode) => set({ orderMode: mode }),
  orderDraft: {},
  setOrderDraft: (patch) =>
    set((state) => ({ orderDraft: { ...state.orderDraft, ...patch } })),

  // Place order
  placedOrder: null,
  placeOrder: (draft) => {
    orderCounter += 1;
    const state = get();
    const branchCode = draft.branchId.split('-').slice(1).join('').toUpperCase() || 'XX';
    const order: PlacedOrder = {
      id: `client-${Date.now()}`,
      orderNumber: `ORD-${branchCode}-${orderCounter}`,
      branchName: draft.branchName,
      orderMode: draft.orderMode,
      tableNo: draft.tableNo,
      customerName: draft.customerName,
      items: [...state.cart],
      totalAmount: state.cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.price * item.quantity, 0),
      status: 'PENDING',
      placedAt: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
      estimatedMinutes: draft.orderMode === 'DELIVERY' ? 35 : draft.orderMode === 'TAKEAWAY' ? 20 : 15,
      paymentMethod: draft.paymentMethod,
    };
    set({ placedOrder: order, cart: [], orderDraft: {} });
    return order;
  },
  clearPlacedOrder: () => set({ placedOrder: null }),

  // Cart drawer
  isCartOpen: false,
  openCart: () => set({ isCartOpen: true }),
  closeCart: () => set({ isCartOpen: false }),
}));

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\shared\api\authService.ts =====  
import api from './axiosClient';

// Enums / Types
export type RoleType = 'ADMIN' | 'STORE_MANAGER' | 'CASHIER' | 'CHEF' | 'WAITER' | 'CUSTOMER';
export type UserStatus = 'PENDING_VERIFICATION' | 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE' | 'BLOCKED';
export type OtpPurpose = 'REGISTER_VERIFICATION' | 'PASSWORD_RESET';

export interface RegisterRequest {
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  password: string;
}

export interface VerifyRegisterOtpRequest {
  email: string;
  otpCode: string;
}

export interface LoginRequest {
  usernameOrEmail: string;
  password: string;
}

export interface AuthResponse {
  token: string;
  userId: string;
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  role: RoleType;
  status: UserStatus;
}

export interface SendOtpRequest {
  email: string;
}

export interface VerifyResetOtpRequest {
  email: string;
  otpCode: string;
}

export interface ResetPasswordRequest {
  email: string;
  otpCode: string;
  newPassword: string;
}

export interface UserAdminDto {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  role: RoleType;
  status: UserStatus;
  createdAt: string;
}

export const authApi = {
  // 1. Đăng ký tài khoản
  register: async (data: RegisterRequest) => {
    const res = await api.post('/auth/register', data);
    return res.data;
  },

  // 2. Xác minh OTP Đăng ký
  verifyRegisterOtp: async (data: VerifyRegisterOtpRequest) => {
    const res = await api.post('/auth/verify-register-otp', data);
    return res.data;
  },

  // 3. Đăng nhập
  login: async (data: LoginRequest) => {
    const res = await api.post('/auth/login', data);
    return res.data;
  },

  // 4. Yêu cầu mã OTP Quên mật khẩu
  forgotPassword: async (data: SendOtpRequest) => {
    const res = await api.post('/auth/forgot-password', data);
    return res.data;
  },

  // 5. Xác minh OTP Quên mật khẩu
  verifyResetOtp: async (data: VerifyResetOtpRequest) => {
    const res = await api.post('/auth/verify-reset-otp', data);
    return res.data;
  },

  // 6. Đặt lại mật khẩu mới
  resetPassword: async (data: ResetPasswordRequest) => {
    const res = await api.post('/auth/reset-password', data);
    return res.data;
  },

  // 7. Gửi lại mã OTP
  resendOtp: async (email: string, purpose: OtpPurpose) => {
    const res = await api.post(`/auth/resend-otp?email=${encodeURIComponent(email)}&purpose=${purpose}`);
    return res.data;
  },

  // --- ADMIN APIs ---

  // 8. Admin lấy danh sách người dùng
  getAllUsers: async () => {
    const res = await api.get('/auth/admin/users');
    return res.data;
  },

  // 9. Admin cập nhật trạng thái tài khoản
  updateUserStatus: async (userId: string, status: UserStatus) => {
    const res = await api.put(`/auth/admin/users/${userId}/status`, { status });
    return res.data;
  },
};

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\shared\api\client.ts =====  
import axios, { AxiosError } from 'axios';

export const API_BASE_URL = 'http://localhost:8080/api';

export const apiClient = axios.create({
  baseURL: API_BASE_URL,
  timeout: 5000,
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  },
});

export interface ClientError {
  status: number;
  error: string;
  error_description?: string;
  data?: unknown;
}

export const parseErrorBody = (status: number, data: unknown): ClientError => {
  if (typeof data === 'object' && data !== null) {
    const errObj = data as Record<string, unknown>;
    return {
      status,
      error: String(errObj.error || errObj.message || 'api_error'),
      error_description: String(errObj.error_description || errObj.message || 'An error occurred'),
      data,
    };
  }
  return {
    status,
    error: 'http_error',
    error_description: String(data || 'Request failed'),
  };
};

export const transformAxiosErrorResponse = (error: AxiosError | unknown): ClientError => {
  if (axios.isAxiosError(error) && error.response) {
    return parseErrorBody(error.response.status, error.response.data);
  }
  const errMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Network connection failed';
  return {
    status: 500,
    error: 'network_error',
    error_description: errMessage,
  };
};

export const toQueryString = (params?: Record<string, unknown>): string => {
  if (!params) return '';
  const cleanParams: Record<string, string> = {};
  Object.keys(params).forEach((key) => {
    const val = params[key];
    if (val !== undefined && val !== null && val !== '') {
      cleanParams[key] = String(val);
    }
  });
  const queryString = new URLSearchParams(cleanParams).toString();
  return queryString ? `?${queryString}` : '';
};

export const checkBackendConnection = async (): Promise<boolean> => {
  try {
    const res = await apiClient.get('/branches', { timeout: 3000 });
    return res.status >= 200 && res.status < 400;
  } catch (error: unknown) {
    if (axios.isAxiosError(error) && error.response && error.response.status) {
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
};

import type { MenuItem, OrderRequest, CustomerResponse, CustomerStatus } from '../types/customer';

const API_BASE_URL = 'http://localhost:8080/api'; // Đường dẫn chuẩn API Backend

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// PHẦN DÀNH CHO ADMIN (Quản lý khách hàng hội viên)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// 1. Lấy danh sách khách hàng cho trang Admin
export const fetchCustomers = async (): Promise<CustomerResponse[]> => {
  const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/customers`);
  if (!res.ok) {
    const errorData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(errorData.message || 'Không thể tải danh sách khách hàng');
  }
  return res.json();
};

// 2. Cập nhật trạng thái khách hàng (active / inactive)
export const updateCustomerStatus = async (
  id: string,
  status: CustomerStatus | string
): Promise<CustomerResponse> => {
  const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/customers/${id}/status?status=${status}`, {
    method: 'PATCH',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    const errorData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(errorData.message || 'Cập nhật trạng thái thất bại');
  }
  return res.json();
};

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// PHẦN CỦA BẠN BẠN LÀM (Khách hàng vãng lai & Đặt món online)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export const customerApi = {
  // Gửi OTP
  sendOtp: async (phone: string): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string }> => {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/v1/customer/auth/send-otp`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ phone }),
    });
    return res.json();
  },

  // Xác thực OTP
  verifyOtp: async (phone: string, otp: string): Promise<{ success: boolean; token?: string; message: string }> => {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/v1/customer/auth/verify-otp`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ phone, otp }),
    });
    return res.json();
  },

  // Lấy danh sách thực đơn
  getMenu: async (): Promise<MenuItem[]> => {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/v1/customer/menu`);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Không thể tải thực đơn');
    return res.json();
  },

  // Gửi đơn đặt món
  createOrder: async (order: OrderRequest): Promise<{ success: boolean; orderId: string }> => {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/v1/customer/orders`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(order),
    });
    return res.json();
  },

  // Gắn thêm 2 hàm Admin vào object để gọi theo cách nào cũng được
  fetchCustomers,
  updateCustomerStatus,
};

export default customerApi;

===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\shared\components\BackendConnectionGate.tsx =====  
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { checkBackendConnection } from '../api/client';

interface Props {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export const BackendConnectionGate: React.FC<Props> = ({ children }) => {
  const [isConnected, setIsConnected] = useState<boolean>(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    const checkAndConnect = async () => {
      const connected = await checkBackendConnection();
      if (connected && isMounted) {
        setIsConnected(true);
      }
    };

    checkAndConnect();

    // Thử lại định kỳ 5 giây/lần cho tới khi Backend được bật thành công
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      checkAndConnect();
    }, 5000);

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      clearInterval(timer);
    };
  }, []);

  // KHI BACKEND CHƯA CHẠY: GIỮ NGUYÊN MÀN HÌNH LOADING TRUNG TÂM, KHÔNG CHO VÀO GIAO DIỆN
  if (!isConnected) {
    return (
      <div
        style={{
          height: '100vh',
          width: '100vw',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'center',
          backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            width: '40px',
            height: '40px',
            border: '3px solid #e2e8f0',
            borderTopColor: '#0f172a',
            borderRadius: '50%',
            animation: 'spin 0.75s linear infinite',
          }}
        />
        <style>{`
          @keyframes spin {
            0% { transform: rotate(0deg); }
            100% { transform: rotate(360deg); }
          }
        `}</style>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return <>{children}</>;
};
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\shared\components\Modal.tsx =====  
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';

interface ModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  maxWidth?: string;
}

export const Modal: React.FC<ModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  title,
  children,
  maxWidth = '560px',
}) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    if (isOpen) {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    }
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        position: 'fixed',
        inset: 0,
        zIndex: 1000,
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.6)',
        backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
        padding: '20px',
      }}
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          width: '100%',
          maxWidth,
          backgroundColor: 'var(--bg-surface)',
          borderRadius: 'var(--radius-lg)',
          boxShadow: 'var(--shadow-xl)',
          border: '1px solid var(--border-color)',
          overflow: 'hidden',
          animation: 'fadeIn 0.25s ease-out',
        }}
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Header */}
        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'space-between',
            padding: '16px 24px',
            borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border-color)',
          }}
        >
          <h3 style={{ fontSize: '1.1rem', fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
            {title}
          </h3>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="btn btn-secondary btn-icon"
            style={{ width: '32px', height: '32px' }}
          >
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Body */}
        <div style={{ padding: '24px', maxHeight: '80vh', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
          {children}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
  
===== FILE: D:\project4\omni-resto\frontend\src\shared\components\OtpModal.tsx =====  
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { authApi, type OtpPurpose } from '../api/authService';
import './OtpModal.css';

interface OtpModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  email: string;
  purpose: OtpPurpose;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess: (otpCode: string) => void;
}

interface ApiErrorResponse {
  response?: {
    data?: {
      message?: string;
    };
  };
}

// Component con: Tự tạo mới state khi modal bật lên, sạch lỗi render
const OtpModalDialog: React.FC<Omit<OtpModalProps, 'isOpen'>> = ({
  email,
  purpose,
  onClose,
  onSuccess,
}) => {
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState<string[]>(['', '', '', '', '', '']);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string>('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [countdown, setCountdown] = useState<number>(60);
  const inputRefs = useRef<(HTMLInputElement | null)[]>([]);

  const canResend = countdown === 0;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (countdown <= 0) return;

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setCountdown((prev) => (prev > 0 ? prev - 1 : 0));
    }, 1000);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [countdown]);

  const handleChange = (value: string, index: number) => {
    if (!/^\d*$/.test(value)) return;

    const newOtp = [...otp];
    newOtp[index] = value.slice(-1);
    setOtp(newOtp);

    // Tự động focus sang ô tiếp theo
    if (value && index < 5) {
      inputRefs.current[index + 1]?.focus();
    }
  };

  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>, index: number) => {
    if (e.key === 'Backspace' && !otp[index] && index > 0) {
      inputRefs.current[index - 1]?.focus();
    }
  };

  const handlePaste = (e: React.ClipboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const pasted = e.clipboardData.getData('text').trim();
    if (/^\d{6}$/.test(pasted)) {
      setOtp(pasted.split(''));
      inputRefs.current[5]?.focus();
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const fullOtp = otp.join('');
    if (fullOtp.length < 6) {
      setError('Vui lòng nhập đủ 6 chữ số OTP');
      return;
    }

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError('');
      if (purpose === 'REGISTER_VERIFICATION') {
        await authApi.verifyRegisterOtp({ email, otpCode: fullOtp });
      } else {
        await authApi.verifyResetOtp({ email, otpCode: fullOtp });
      }
      onSuccess(fullOtp);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const apiErr = err as ApiErrorResponse;
      setError(apiErr.response?.data?.message || 'Mã OTP không chính xác hoặc đã hết hạn');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleResend = async () => {
    try {
      setError('');
      await authApi.resendOtp(email, purpose);
      setCountdown(60);
      setOtp(['', '', '', '', '', '']);
      inputRefs.current[0]?.focus();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const apiErr = err as ApiErrorResponse;
      setError(apiErr.response?.data?.message || 'Không thể gửi lại mã OTP, vui lòng thử lại');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="otp-modal-backdrop"
      style={{
        position: 'fixed',
        inset: 0,
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.65)',
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
        zIndex: 9999,
        padding: '16px',
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="otp-modal-card"
        style={{
          backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
          borderRadius: '16px',
          padding: '32px 24px',
          width: '100%',
          maxWidth: '430px',
          textAlign: 'center',
          boxShadow: '0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25)',
        }}
      >
        <h3 className="otp-modal-title">Xác Nhận Mã OTP</h3>
        <p className="otp-modal-desc">
          Mã xác thực gồm 6 chữ số đã được gửi đến email:
          <br />
          <span className="otp-modal-email">{email}</span>
        </p>

        {error && <div className="otp-modal-error">{error}</div>}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          {/* Hàng 6 ô vuông bắt buộc nằm ngang */}
          <div
            className="otp-inputs-row"
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              flexDirection: 'row',
              justifyContent: 'center',
              gap: '8px',
              marginBottom: '24px',
            }}
          >
            {otp.map((digit, index) => (
              <input
                key={index}
                ref={(el) => {
                  inputRefs.current[index] = el;
                }}
                type="text"
                inputMode="numeric"
                maxLength={1}
                value={digit}
                onChange={(e) => handleChange(e.target.value, index)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => handleKeyDown(e, index)}
                onPaste={handlePaste}
                className="otp-box-input"
                style={{
                  width: '46px',
                  height: '52px',
                  fontSize: '1.4rem',
                  fontWeight: 800,
                  textAlign: 'center',
                  border: '1.5px solid #cbd5e1',
                  borderRadius: '10px',
                  backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                  outline: 'none',
                }}
              />
            ))}
          </div>

          <div>
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="otp-btn-confirm">
              {loading ? 'Đang xác minh...' : 'Xác Nhận'}
            </button>

            <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="otp-btn-cancel">
              Hủy bỏ
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>

        <div className="otp-resend-text">
          {canResend ? (
            <button onClick={handleResend} className="otp-resend-link">
              Gửi lại mã OTP
            </button>
          ) : (
            <span>
              Gửi lại mã sau: <strong style={{ color: '#ea580c' }}>{countdown}s</strong>
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export const OtpModal: React.FC<OtpModalProps> = (props) => {
  if (!props.isOpen) return null;
  return <OtpModalDialog {...props} />;
};

  category: MenuCategory;
  price: number;
  quantity: number;

export type OrderMode = 'DINE_IN' | 'TAKEAWAY' | 'DELIVERY';
export type ClientPaymentMethod = 'CASH' | 'CARD' | 'QR_CODE';

export interface ClientOrderDraft {
  branchId: string;
  branchName: string;
  orderMode: OrderMode;
  tableNo?: string;
  customerName?: string;
  customerPhone?: string;
  deliveryAddress?: string;
  notes?: string;
